### [1] [Anatomy of a Query: W5H Dimensions and FAR Patterns for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05525)
*Vicki Stover Hertzberg,Eduardo Valverde,Joyce C. Ho*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QUEST框架基于FAR结构不变性和W5H维度框架，对文本到SQL查询进行语义理解评估。在12万+样本中验证：FAR适用于所有领域，W5H维度分布因领域而异（医疗查询集中于WHEN和WHO维度），因果和机制推理近乎为零，表明机器推理仍需突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据库接口日益普及，但评估和设计这些系统的理论基础仍不完善。

Method: 提出QUEST框架，包含FAR结构不变性（查询可归约为Filter、Aggregate、Return操作）和W5H维度框架（过滤条件映射到Who、What、Where、When、Why、How六个语义维度），并在五个文本到SQL数据集（120,464个样本）上进行验证。

Result: FAR一致性在所有领域和模式类型中普遍存在；W5H维度分布因领域而异。医疗查询集中在WHEN（80.4%）和WHO（73.0%）维度，因果（WHY）和机制（HOW）推理在所有领域中几乎为零。

Conclusion: 识别出机器对结构化数据进行真正推理所需突破的前沿边界。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces to databases have gained popularity, yet the theoretical foundations for evaluating and designing these systems remain underdeveloped. We present QUEST (Query Understanding Evaluation through Semantic Translation), a framework resting on two independently motivated components: the FAR structural invariant, which holds that every well-formed query reduces to Filter, Aggregate, and Return operations; and the W5H dimensional framework, which holds that all filtering criteria map to six semantic dimensions (Who, What, Where, When, Why, and How). Validated across five text-to-SQL datasets (n = 120,464), FAR conformance is universal across all domains and schema types, while W5H dimensional profiles vary substantially. Healthcare queries are strongly concentrated in temporal (WHEN: 80.4%) and person-centric (WHO: 73.0%) dimensions far exceeding general-domain benchmarks, and causal (WHY) and mechanistic (HOW) reasoning are near-zero everywhere, with apparent HOW exceptions reflecting quantitative aggregation rather than genuine procedural reasoning. These results identify a frontier that must be crossed for genuine machine reasoning over structured data.

</details>


### [2] [An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)
*Sicheng Pan,Shuxian Wang,Wesley Zheng,Zirong Zeng,Vijay Sharma,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Rulescript是一种引擎无关的领域特定语言(DSL)，用于开发查询重写规则。它通过关系代数核心语言将规则定义与执行基础设施分离，支持模式匹配和变换两阶段分解，所有重写自动经过形式化验证确保正确性，实现了"一次编写，随处部署"的查询计划重写范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统中重写规则通常手动实现、与特定执行引擎紧密耦合且缺乏形式化正确性保证。开发新引擎需要重新实现新旧规则，带来巨大的工程成本，限制了可移植性，且每次新实现都可能引入新错误。

Method: Rulescript设计了一种引擎无关的DSL，核心基于关系代数，将规则显式分解为匹配和变换两个阶段。开发者使用核心操作符进行模式匹配并构造语义等价的变换计划，所有重写自动进行形式化验证。支持通过核心语言定义自定义操作符以捕获引擎特定语义，通过轻量适配器与目标系统集成。

Result: 通过从Apache Calcite重新实现33条重写规则并扩展若干自定义操作符进行评估，自动将这些规则部署到CockroachDB和Apache Data Fusion两个后端差异显著的引擎上，验证了可移植性。

Conclusion: Rulescript实现了"一次编写，随处部署"的查询计划重写范式，只需极少的工作量即可将已编写的规则部署到新的数据引擎上。

Abstract: Logical query plan rewriting transforms a relational database query into an equivalent but more efficient form and is crucial to the performance of database-backed applications. In existing systems, rewrite rules are typically implemented manually, tightly coupled to specific execution engines, and often lack formal correctness guarantees. Consequently, developing a new engine requires reimplementing both legacy and new rules, incurring significant engineering cost, limiting portability, and every new implementation is an opportunity for introducing new bugs.
  We introduce Rulescript, an engine-agnostic domain-specific language (DSL) for developing query rewrite rules. Rulescript separates rule definition from execution infrastructure via a relational algebra-inspired core language and an explicit decomposition of rules into matching and transformation phases. Developers express rewrites by pattern-matching query plans using Rulescript's core operators and constructing semantically equivalent transformed plans, with all rewrites automatically verified formally to ensure correctness. Rulescript is extensible: users can define custom operators in terms of the core language to capture engine-specific semantics. To integrate with an existing system, developers need only implement a lightweight adapter that maps Rulescript's core and custom operators to the operators implemented in the target engine.
  We evaluate Rulescript by reimplementing 33 rewrite rules from Apache Calcite and extending the language with several custom operators. To demonstrate portability, we automatically deploy these rules to CockroachDB and Apache Data Fusion, two engines with substantially different backends. Our results show that Rulescript enables "write once, deploy everywhere" paradigm for query plan rewriting, with minimal effort required to deploy previously written rules on a new data engine.

</details>


### [3] [Low-Latency Out-of-Core ANN Search in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05787)
*Ziwen Song,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang,Junhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SkipDisk是一种磁盘-内存混合的近似最近邻搜索方法，通过紧致下界过滤、三级数据剪枝和异步I/O策略，在仅使用HNSW约10%内存的情况下，实现85%的搜索延迟，或在20%内存下实现63%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内存图式ANN搜索性能优异但内存开销大，磁盘方法虽降低内存但面临高磁盘访问延迟，亟需一种能在显著降低内存占用的同时保持低延迟搜索的方法。

Method: 1) 为每个点设计专用枢轴（pivot）以增强三角不等式下界紧致性，并基于此设计估计式过滤方法；2) 采用三级数据剪枝策略，仅在内存中保留高信息量数据；3) 通过将邻居节点存储在内存中，解耦内存搜索与磁盘访问，设计异步I/O策略。

Result: 在约10%内存占用下达到HNSW 85%的延迟，在约20%内存占用下延迟仅为HNSW的63%。

Conclusion: SkipDisk证明了通过紧致下界过滤与异步I/O解耦设计，磁盘-内存混合方案能在大幅降低内存开销的同时实现与纯内存方法相当甚至更优的搜索延迟。

Abstract: In-memory graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search has superior search performance but incurs significant memory footprint. Disk-based methods reduce memory usage but suffer from high disk access latency. A common challenge is how to achieve low-latency search while significantly reducing memory footprint. In this paper, we propose SkipDisk, a disk-memory hybrid ANN search that significantly reduces memory footprint while achieving search latency comparable to or lower than in-memory method HNSW. By analyzing existing disk-based methods, we observed that disk access remains the primary bottleneck, and existing lower bound based filtering methods are two loose to effectively reduce disk access. Therefore, we design SkipDisk to achieve tight lower bound with low memory footprint to reduce the search latency. First, we design a dedicated pivot for each point to improve the lower bound of the triangle inequality for effective filtering. We further design an estimation-based approach based on this lower bound. Second, to reduce the memory footprint, we employ a three-level data pruning strategy to preserve informative data in memory. Third, to further reduce search latency, we design an asynchronous I/O strategy based on the decoupling of in-memory search and disk access by storing neighbor nodes in memory. Experiments show that our method achieves a latency of 85 of HNSW's latency with approximately 10 memory footprint, and a latency to 63 of HNSW's with a slightly higher memory footprint of around 20.

</details>

### [4] [Performance Characterization of dApps in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05426)
*Conrado Boeira,Eduardo Baena,Andrea Lacava,Tommaso Melodia,Dimitrios Koutsonikolas,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在裸机和容器环境中部署并评估了O-RAN实时应用（dApps），分析了延迟、可扩展性和资源利用的权衡，并证明将dApps卸载到智能网卡可缓解性能瓶颈、提升实时响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O-RAN实时应用部署主要依赖裸机服务器，且缺乏对智能与非智能dApp资源使用模式的可见性，阻碍了合理的部署决策。

Method: 实现代表性dApps，在裸机和容器两种部署场景下进行实验，评估延迟、可扩展性和资源利用率，并测试将dApps卸载到智能网卡的效果。

Result: 识别了关键性能瓶颈，并证明通过智能网卡卸载可显著缓解这些限制，改善实时响应能力。

Conclusion: 容器化部署结合硬件加速卸载是提升O-RAN系统实时性能的有效路径，为dApps的部署优化提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Despite recommendations to deploy real-time Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) applications (dApps) in containerized environments, existing approaches predominantly rely on bare-metal servers. Moreover, current dApp deployments offer limited visibility into the resource usage patterns of both intelligent and non-intelligent dApps, hindering informed deployment decisions. This work addresses these gaps by implementing and evaluating representative dApps across multiple deployment scenarios (bare-metal and containers) to characterize the trade-offs in latency, scalability, and resource utilization. Additionally, we identify key performance bottlenecks and demonstrate how offloading dApps to emerging hardware accelerators, such as smart Network Interface Cards (NICs), can alleviate these limitations and improve real-time responsiveness in O-RAN systems.

</details>

### [5] [Governed Metaprogramming for Intelligent Systems: Reclassifying Eval as a Governed Effec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05248)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出受控元编程（governed metaprogramming）语言设计，将代码到可执行程序的转换（materialization）重新定义为受控效应，而非无限制的eval原语。系统通过结构检查、能力需求分析和策略合规性审查，在允许执行前对生成的程序进行治理。在MashinTalk DSL中实现，并集成454个Rocq定理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM生成代码、智能体构建工作流、自改进系统等场景中，运行时合成可执行结构日益普遍。传统同像性和分阶段语言中，代码表示到执行的转换不受限制，eval是语言原语而非受治理操作。本文认为在受治理的智能系统中，这种转换构成权限放大（authority amplification），必须像其他效应一样被中介管理。

Method: 提出受控元编程语言设计：(1) 程序表示（machine forms）作为一等值；(2) 形式操作是纯计算；(3) materialization（形式到可执行程序的转换）是受治理效应，需经过结构检查。形式化两个判断：纯形式求值（不产生指令）和受控materialization（产生恰好一个受治理指令）。证明三个性质：形式操作纯度、无绕过定理、边界保持性。在MashinTalk DSL中实现，编译到BEAM字节码。

Result: 实现了MashinTalk DSL，集成454个已有机器检查的Rocq定理。形式化证明了三个关键性质：形式操作保持纯度、无法绕过治理系统、系统边界保持完整。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将eval从语言原语重新分类为受控效应（governed effect），为AI系统运行时代码合成提供了形式化的安全保障框架。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly synthesize executable structure at runtime: LLMs generate programs, agents construct workflows,self-improving systems modify their own behavior. In classical homoiconic and staged languages, the transition from coderepresentation to execution is unrestricted. eval is a language primitive, not a governed operation. We argue that ingovernedintelligent systems, this transition is an authority amplification: it converts symbolic structure into executableauthority andmust be mediated like any other effect. We present governed metaprogramming, a language design where programrepresentations(machine forms) are first-class values, form manipulation is pure computation, and materialization (the transition fromform toexecutable machine) is a governed effect subject to structural inspection. The governance system analyzes the proposedprogram'scapability requirements, policy compliance, and resource estimates before permitting execution. We formalize twojudgments: pureform evaluation (which emits no directives) and governed materialization (which emits exactly one governed directive). Weprovethree properties: purity of form manipulation, the no-bypass theorem, and boundary preservation. We implement the designinMashinTalk, a DSL for AI workflows compiling to BEAM bytecode, and report on integration with 454 existingmachine-checked Rocqtheorems. The central contribution is reclassifying eval from a language primitive into a governed effect.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond BLEU: A Semantic Evaluation Method for Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05282)
*Julius Näumann,Sven Keidel,Amir Molzam Sharifloo,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义等价性的代码翻译评估方法，引入语义正确性分数，发现BLEU与语义正确性相关性极低，LLM方法显著优于启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码翻译评估指标（如BLEU）仅衡量句法相似性，忽略了程序语义，无法真实反映翻译的正确性。

Method: 将编译器测试方法应用于代码翻译领域，定义语义正确性分数（产生正确执行结果的翻译比例），评估基于LLM和启发式的二进制提升（反编译）模型。

Result: LLM方法显著优于启发式方法；BLEU与语义正确性相关性极弱（r = -0.127至0.354），句法指标无法预测功能正确性。

Conclusion: 语义等价性评估比句法相似性指标更可靠，现有BLEU等指标不适用于代码翻译任务的正确性评估。

Abstract: Code translation is one of the core capabilities of LLMs. However, evaluating the correctness of translations remains difficult, as commonly used metrics such as BLEU measure only syntactic similarity, disregarding program semantics. We propose a novel evaluation methodology for code translation tasks, emphasizing semantic equivalence over surface-level string similarity. Our approach applies established compiler testing methodology to a new domain, allowing the assessment of an LLM fine-tuned for binary lifting tasks (i.e. decompiling binaries to higher-level representations). We introduce a semantic correctness score, defined as the proportion of translations that produce correct execution outcomes, and demonstrate its application by evaluating LLM-based and heuristic decompilers. Our findings show that LLM-based approaches significantly outperform heuristic ones, while BLEU scores show negligible correlation with semantic correctness (r = -0.127 to 0.354), demonstrating that syntactic metrics fail to predict functional accuracy.

</details>

### [7] [Making AI Drafts Count: A Quality Threshold in Audio Description Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05348)
*Lana Do,Shasta Ihorn,Charity M. Pitcher-Cooper,Sanjay Mirani,Gio Jung,Hyunjoo Shim,Zhenzhen Qin,Kien T. Nguyen,Vassilis Athitsos,Ilmi Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究AI生成的音频描述草稿质量如何影响人工编辑过程。通过GenAD（符合无障碍指南的AD生成管道）和RefineAD（人工编辑界面），发现高质量AI草稿可将完成时间缩短一半以上并显著降低认知负荷，而低质量草稿（简单无引导提示生成）效益有限。提出了AI辅助应达到与目标内容复杂度相匹配的"质量阈值"设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明AI生成的草稿能帮助新手描述者产出更高质量的音频描述并降低门槛，但草稿质量如何塑造编辑过程仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 开发了GenAD（结合无障碍指南和视频上下文信息的AD生成管道）和RefineAD（人工编辑界面）；通过被试内实验，比较"从头创作"与"编辑不同质量AI草稿"在文本、时间、交付三个维度上的人机贡献。

Result: GenAD草稿将完成时间缩短一半以上，显著降低认知负荷；基线草稿（简单无引导提示生成）仅带来有限效益；定性发现质量阈值具有内容依赖性——视觉复杂度越高，所需AI草稿质量越高。

Conclusion: 有效的AI辅助应达到与目标内容匹配的质量阈值，而非仅仅提供AI草稿本身。

Abstract: Audio description (AD) narrates visual elements in video for blind and low-vision audiences. Recent work has shown that giving novice describers an AI-generated draft to start from helps produce higher-quality AD and lowers the barrier to entry. What remains an open question is how draft quality shapes the editing process. We investigate this through GenAD, an AD generation pipeline that incorporates accessibility guidelines and contextual video information, and RefineAD, an editing interface for human revisions. Human-AI contributions are measured across text, timing, and delivery. In a within-subjects study, we compared authoring from scratch against editing AI drafts of varying quality. GenAD drafts cut completion time by more than half and significantly reduced cognitive load. In contrast, baseline drafts generated from simple, unguided prompts offered only modest benefits, pointing to a minimum quality threshold for effectiveness. Qualitative findings suggest this threshold is content-dependent; as visual complexity increases, so does the quality needed from AI drafts. We propose this as a design principle: effective AI assistance should clear a quality threshold suited to the target content, rather than simply be present.

</details>


### [8] [Every(bot) Makes Mistakes: Coding Big Five Personalities, Context, and Tone into an LLM Chatbot Recovery Code Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05391)
*Rachel Hill,Tom Owen,Julian Hough*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大语言模型聊天机器人的任务上下文、人格特质（大五人格中的尽责性、宜人性、开放性、外向性）、语气及三阶段恢复指令映射到结构化恢复代码中的方法。实验表明，经过恢复代码训练的Claude Sonnet 4.6模型在恢复质量、语气一致性和适当性三个维度上平均提升27.8%，其中适当性维度表现最佳（83.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别探索了LLM的恢复策略、语气、上下文和人格等设计维度，但缺乏将这些变量整合为结构化指导框架的工作。人机交互中的错误恢复不佳会破坏交互流畅性、损害用户信任并降低用户参与度，因此需要一种系统化的恢复代码来指导LLM聊天机器人在不同上下文中进行更有效的错误恢复。

Method: 采用探索性方法论，无人类参与者。设计恢复代码将四种常见LLM聊天机器人任务上下文映射到四种大五人格特质、相应语气及三阶段恢复指令；同时设计了包含三个维度（恢复质量、语气一致性、适当性）和九个子维度的评估框架。采用组间设计：条件A（基线）中四个Claude Sonnet 4.6智能体未接受恢复代码训练；条件B（编码）中四个Claude Sonnet 4.6模型接受恢复代码训练。使用相同的用户提示和错误场景，由八个LLM评估智能体对恢复响应进行五分制评分。

Result: 编码恢复响应平均得分76.7%，相比基线响应的48.9%提升27.8%。条件B在适当性维度表现最强（83.3%），人格适当性显著提升（75%对比50%），提供解释维度提升尤为显著（60%对比20%）。

Conclusion: 结构化的人格、上下文和语气感知恢复代码可以被LLM聊天机器人成功学习和应用，从而在不同上下文任务中提高错误恢复质量。该方法为构建更系统化的人机交互错误恢复框架提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Despite careful design involving classifiers, parameters, and safeguarding, errors during human/AI interaction are not rare. Poor error recovery can disrupt interaction flow, damage user trust, and decrease user engagement. Whilst existing work has explored LLM recovery, tone, context, and personality as separate design dimensions, no existing work has combined these variables into a structured guidance framework. This paper presents a recovery code that maps four common LLM chatbot task contexts to associated personality traits (four Big Five personalities: Conscientiousness, Agreeableness, Openness, and Extraversion), tones, and three-stage recovery instructions. A recovery evaluation rubric was also designed, comprising three dimensions (Recovery quality, Tone alignment, and Appropriateness) and nine sub-dimensions. The methodology is exploratory, with no participants used. A between-subjects design was employed across two conditions: Condition A (baseline, uncoded), four separate Claude Sonnet 4.6 agents received no recovery code training; Condition B (coded), four separate Claude Sonnet 4.6 models were trained on the recovery code. Identical 'user' prompts and error scenarios were used across both conditions. Eight LLM evaluator agents assessed the recovery responses using the evaluation rubric, producing scores out of 5 for each sub-dimension. Results found a 27.8% average performance increase in coded recovery responses (76.7%) compared to baseline responses (48.9%). Condition B performed strongest in the appropriateness dimension (83.3%), with notable improvement in personality appropriateness (75% versus 50%) and providing explanation (60% versus 20%). These findings suggest that structured personality, context, and tone-informed recovery codes can be successfully learnt and applied by LLM chatbots to improve error recovery quality across varying contextual tasks.

</details>


### [9] [Why Someone Asked "Why": Foil Inference in Human and LLM Question Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05401)
*Britt Besch,Tobias Gerstenberg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了人们如何推断"为什么问题"中隐含的对比对象（foil），发现事后期望（hindsight expectation）最能预测 foil 的选择，而大型语言模型在此任务上表现不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释本质是对比性的，但对比对象（foils）很少被明确提及，需要根据上下文推断。理解人们如何选择预期的 foil 对于人机交互和对话系统至关重要。

Method: 通过实验让参与者阅读故事并对其事先期望、相似度、事后期望以及他们认为提问者心中所想的 foil 进行判断，同时测试了大型语言模型在相同任务上的表现。

Result: 参与者的 foil 选择最好由事后期望判断来预测；而 LLMs 的显式期望判断与他们的 foil 选择之间存在不一致的耦合关系。

Conclusion: 人们通过考虑提问者在结果发生后发现什么令人惊讶来推断 foil；LLMs 在此任务上的表现与人类不一致，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Explanations are inherently contrastive: E happened rather than E' because of C rather than C'. However, these contrasts, or "foils", are rarely mentioned explicitly but have to be inferred in context. Here, we investigate how people select the intended foil E' of a why-question. Participants read vignettes and judged, for each foil, their prior expectation (what will happen next), closeness (what is most similar to what happened), and hindsight expectation (what could have happened instead), as well as which foil they thought the question asker had in mind when they asked the why-question. We found that foil selections were best predicted by hindsight expectation judgments. This suggests that people infer the foil by considering what a question asker finds surprising after the outcome occurred. Since correct foil selection is relevant not only in human-human interaction but also increasingly in dialogues with large language models, we investigated their performance on the same task. The coupling between LLMs' explicit expectation judgments and their foil selections is inconsistent.

</details>


### [10] [The Ambivalent Experience of Eye Contact for People with Visual Impairments: Mechanisms and Design Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05437)
*Markus Wieland,Phillip Koch,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对17名视障者的访谈，揭示了在混合能力协作中，目光接触作为默认的注意力与话轮转换线索对盲人不可及的问题。采用批判实在论视角，识别出三种因果机制：呼唤姓名替代目光分配话语权、模糊的语音进入线索导致注意力分裂和疲劳、目光接触规范扭曲参与评判。据此提出五个设计挑战，将可及目光接触重新定义为可配置的交互契约而非简单的视觉化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合能力协作研究将目光接触默认为注意力与话轮转换的线索，但这些视觉信号对视障者不可及。先前工作侧重技术解决方案，缺乏对视障者与明眼伙伴互动中机制层面的解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对17名视障者进行半结构化访谈，涵盖工作、教育和社交场景的日常互动；采用批判实在论（critical-realist）视角，将具体事件与可能的因果机制联系起来进行分析。

Result: 识别出三种重复出现的因果机制：(1) 当目光无法分配话语权时，可及性依赖于明确称呼姓名；(2) 模糊的语音进入线索和持续的接入工作分裂注意力并累积疲劳，有时导致退缩；(3) 目光接触规范可能扭曲参与评判，促使主动管理可见性。

Conclusion: 将上述机制转化为五个设计挑战，重新定义可及目光接触：不是简单地使目光可视化，而是支持可配置的交互契约（configurable interaction contracts）。

Abstract: In mixed-ability collaboration, eye contact is often treated as a default cue for attention and turn-taking. As these signals are primarily visual, they are not reliably accessible to people with visual impairments. While prior work emphasized technical solutions, mechanism-level explanations of their experiences with sighted partners remain scarce. We interviewed 17 people with visual impairments about everyday interactions across work, education, and social settings. Using a critical-realist lens, we link events to plausible causal mechanisms and identify three recurring mechanisms: First, when gaze cannot allocate the floor, addressability hinges on explicit naming. Second, unclear speech entry cues and ongoing access work split attention and build fatigue, sometimes leading to withdrawal. Third, eye-contact norms can skew judgments of participation, prompting active management of visibility. We translate these mechanisms into five design challenges that reframe accessible eye contact as supporting configurable interaction contracts rather than merely making gaze visible.

</details>


### [11] [Cross-individual generalizability of machine learning models for ball speed prediction in baseball pitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05487)
*Ryota Takamido,Chiharu Suzuki,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究利用50名投手的运动数据，通过留一法交叉验证评估了机器学习模型在棒球投掷球速预测中的跨个体泛化能力。结果显示，跨个体评估下的预测性能（R²=0.38）远低于个体内评估（R²=0.91）；模型倾向于高估中级投手的球速；躯干和 pivot leg 表现出较高的泛化性能，尤其在重心转移起始阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于机器学习的运动表现预测研究缺乏对跨个体泛化能力的理解，限制了模型在体育场景中的实际应用价值。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用50名不同竞技水平投手的运动数据集，使用留一被试交叉验证评估跨个体泛化性；分析专业水平限制和时空运动信息约束对模型泛化能力的影响。

Result: (1) 跨个体评估下R²从0.91降至0.38；(2) 模型对中级投手存在显著的高估偏差（p<.05）；(3) 躯干和 pivot leg 具有较高的泛化性能，pivot leg 在重心转移起始阶段R²>0.25。

Conclusion: 强调了跨个体评估对于提升ML在体育场景中实际应用价值的重要性，并促进了对目标动作生物力学因素的深入理解。

Abstract: Although machine learning (ML)-based performance outcome prediction is an important topic in contemporary sports science, one important issue is the limited understanding of the cross-individual generalizability of ML models in sports contexts. To address this issue, this study aimed to evaluate the cross-individual generalizability of ML models for predicting ball speed in baseball pitching. A dataset comprising 50 pitchers from various competitive levels was analyzed. Cross-individual generalizability was assessed using leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Specifically, the effects of expertise level and restrictions on spatiotemporal motion information were examined to identify factors influencing model generalizability. The results revealed that, under cross-individual evaluation, (1) predictive performance was markedly lower than under within-individual evaluation, with R-squared value decreasing from 0.91 to 0.38; (2) the model tended to overestimate the performance of Intermediate pitchers relative to Expert pitchers, with a significant group difference in signed prediction error (p < .05); and (3) the trunk and pivot leg demonstrated relatively high generalization performance, with the pivot leg showing notable generalizability even during the weight-shift initiation phase (R-squared value > 0.25). These findings underscore the importance of cross-individual evaluation in enhancing the practical applicability of ML in sports settings and contribute to a deeper understanding of the biomechanical factors underlying the target movement.

</details>


### [12] [Designing with Tensions: Older Adults' Emotional Support-Seeking Under System-Level Constraints in Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05552)
*Mengqi Shi,Tianqi Song,Zicheng Zhu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对18位老年人的访谈研究发现，他们常因其他社交支持不可及而转向对话式AI寻求情感支持，但当前AI的安全干预措施会打断情感投入、削弱控制感，甚至导致情绪困扰。研究提出了在设计上需考虑社会情境、匹配情感节奏和保持用户自主性的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人越来越多地使用对话式AI作为情感支持来源，但现有研究对日常使用中AI系统限制、重定向或干预情感支持互动时的用户体验知之甚少。

Method: 对18名老年人进行访谈，调查他们在何时寻求AI情感支持、在情感脆弱时刻如何互动，以及当支持被打断时的反应。

Result: 老年人常在社交支持不可及时依赖AI；当前安全相关的干预措施会打断情感投入或转移控制权，这种干扰会削弱老年人的情感参与能力，甚至导致情绪困扰。

Conclusion: 设计情感支持型对话式AI时，需将安全干预置于老年人的社会情境中实施，匹配用户的情感节奏，并保持用户的自主感。

Abstract: Older adults have increasingly turned to conversational AI as a source of emotional support. However, little is known about how emotionally supportive interactions are experienced in everyday use, particularly when AI systems limit, redirect, or intervene during these interactions. We interviewed 18 older adults about their experiences using conversational AI for emotional support, examining when they turn to AI, how they engage during emotionally vulnerable moments, and how they respond when support feels disrupted. Our findings show that older adults often rely on AI when other forms of social support feel inaccessible. However, current safety-related interventions can redirect interactions in ways that participants experience as interruptions to emotional engagement or as shifts in control away from them. Such disruptions can undermine older adults' ability to remain emotionally engaged and, in some cases, contribute to emotional distress. We discussed design implications for emotionally supportive conversational AI, emphasizing the need for safety interventions that are enacted within older adults' social contexts, align with users' emotional pacing, and preserve their sense of agency.

</details>


### [13] [UX in the Age of AI: Rethinking Evaluation Metrics Through a Statistical Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05600)
*Harish Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADUX-Stat框架，将AI系统可用性重构为概率信号分布，通过交互熵指数（IEI）、时间漂移系数（TDC）和贝叶斯可用性置信分数（BUCS）三个新构念，解决传统可用性指标（如SUS、NPS、任务完成率）在随机、上下文敏感的AI驱动界面中的结构性失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UX评估框架（SUS、NPS、任务完成率）建立在确定性、规则驱动界面上，而AI系统具有随机性、上下文敏感性和时间变异性，导致这些指标结构性失效，亟需新的评估模型。

Method: 提出ADUX-Stat框架，包含三个核心构念：交互熵指数（IEI）量化AI响应的不可预测性；时间漂移系数（TDC）衡量纵向感知可用性变化；贝叶斯可用性置信分数（BUCS）在不确定性下生成可用性质量的置信区间估计。通过五个AI产品类别进行概念验证。

Result: ADUX-Stat框架在概念层面得到验证，可作为可复现、可部署的方法论，弥合HCI研究、统计建模与AI产品评估之间的关键缺口。

Conclusion: ADUX-Stat为UX从业者和研究者提供了适用于AI概率性界面的评估方法论，有望替代传统静态标量指标，成为AI驱动系统可用性评估的标准工具。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of artificial intelligence (AI) in consumer-facing digital products has disrupted the assumptions underlying classical user experience (UX) evaluation frameworks. Legacy metrics such as the System Usability Scale (SUS), Net Promoter Score (NPS), and task completion rate were engineered for deterministic, rule-based interfaces where identical inputs yield identical outputs. In AI-mediated systems -- spanning conversational agents, generative interfaces, and recommendation engines -- outputs are stochastic, context-sensitive, and temporally variable, rendering these metrics structurally insufficient. This paper introduces the Adaptive Dynamic UX Statistical Framework (ADUX-Stat), a novel evaluation model that reconceptualises usability as a probabilistic signal distribution rather than a static scalar score. ADUX-Stat integrates three original constructs: (1) Interaction Entropy Index (IEI), quantifying the unpredictability of AI responses from a user perception standpoint; (2) Temporal Drift Coefficient (TDC), measuring longitudinal degradation or improvement of perceived usability over interaction sessions; and (3) Bayesian Usability Confidence Score (BUCS), producing credible interval estimates of usability quality under uncertainty. The framework is validated conceptually against five established AI product categories. ADUX-Stat addresses a critical gap at the intersection of HCI research, statistical modelling, and AI product evaluation, offering a reproducible, field-deployable methodology for UX practitioners and researchers alike.

</details>


### [14] [The Capacity to Care: Designing Social Technology for Sustained Engagement With Societal Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05651)
*JaeWon Kim,Lindsay Popowski,Louisa Conwill,Elizabeth `Lizzie' Li,Meryl Ye,Jiaying `Lizzy' Liu,Jose A. Guridi,Theia Henderson,Bingxu Han,Dennis Wang,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang,Susan Wyche,Yasmine Kotturi,Gillian R. Hayes,Angela D. R. Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何通过社会技术设计支持用户对气候、不公等人道主义危机的持续关注，指出问题不在于冷漠而在于能力不足，并基于Tronto关怀伦理学框架分析社交媒体如何阻碍或促进可持续的关怀行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们并非缺乏对气候变化、不公正和人道主义危机的关注，而是缺乏持续参与的能力。社交媒体架构放大问题意识的同时，却缺乏有意义的行动路径，导致心理困扰、倦怠和脱离，尤其影响年轻人。因此亟需理解如何设计支持可持续关怀的社会技术系统。

Method: 基于Tronto的关怀伦理学框架（关怀需要关注、责任、能力和社区四阶段），结合道德心理学和平台研究，分析主流社交媒体如何阻碍关怀过程（停滞在早期阶段），并邀请研究者和设计师识别消耗或支持关怀能力的平台设计，提出可持续关怀的设计方向。

Result: 指出主流社交媒体架构将关怀过程停滞在最初的"关注"阶段，缺乏对责任、能力和社区的支持，导致用户心理负担加重而无法持续参与。

Conclusion: 需要重新设计社交媒体平台，使其从单纯的注意力捕获机制转向能够支持用户长期、可持续地参与社会问题的系统，避免倦怠和脱离。

Abstract: People care about climate change, injustice, and humanitarian crises. The challenge is not apathy but capacity: sustained engagement with large-scale problems is psychologically costly, and social media architecture often amplifies awareness while providing few pathways to meaningful action. The result is rising distress, overwhelm, and disengagement -- particularly among young people who encounter global suffering through platforms designed for attention capture rather than constructive response. This workshop examines how social technology design shapes the conditions for sustained engagement with societal challenges. Drawing on Tronto's care ethics framework and research in moral psychology and platform studies, we ask why caring at scale is difficult and how social media can both exacerbate and potentially mitigate this difficulty. Tronto's framework shows that good care requires more than awareness: it demands responsibility, competence, and community. Dominant social media architectures stall the caring process at its earliest phase. We invite researchers and designers to identify platform designs that deplete or support the capacity to care, and to develop design directions for \textit{sustainable care}: engagement that people can maintain over time without burning out.

</details>


### [15] [PersonaTeaming: Supporting Persona-Driven Red-Teaming for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05682)
*Wesley Hanwen Deng,Mingxi Yan,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Akshita Jha,Lauren Wilcox,Kenneth Holstein,Motahhare Eslami,Leon A. Gatys*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了PersonaTeaming框架，通过引入角色（persona）来增强生成式AI的红队测试。该框架包括自动化角色驱动的对抗提示生成流程（PersonaTeaming Workflow）和用户交互界面（PersonaTeaming Playground）。实验表明，该方法相比现有方法具有更高的攻击成功率和提示多样性，且通过用户研究发现该平台能促进红队成员的多样化策略和跳出思维定式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试方法缺乏对人类身份和视角的考虑，很少融入人类输入，而人工红队测试又受限于规模和效率。本文旨在通过引入角色驱动的方法来弥补自动化红队测试与人类视角之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 提出PersonaTeaming Workflow，将角色（persona）嵌入对抗性提示生成流程中以探索更广泛的对抗策略；2) 将该工作流实例化为PersonaTeaming Playground，提供用户交互界面让红队成员自主创建角色并与AI协作修改和优化提示；3) 通过11名行业从业者的用户研究进行评估。

Result: 1) PersonaTeaming Workflow相比RainbowPlus（现有最优自动化红队方法）实现了更高的攻击成功率和提示多样性；2) 用户研究发现PersonaTeaming Playground使红队成员能够采用多样化的策略并生成有用的输出；3) AI生成的建议即使在未被严格遵循的情况下，也能鼓励用户跳出思维定式。

Conclusion: 本文同时推进了自动化和人在环中的红队测试方法，并为生成式AI红队测试中的人机协作提供了交互模式和设计洞见。

Abstract: Recent developments in AI safety research have called for red-teaming methods that effectively surface potential risks posed by generative AI models, with growing emphasis on how red-teamers' backgrounds and perspectives shape their strategies and the risks they uncover. While automated red-teaming approaches promise to complement human red-teaming through larger-scale exploration, existing automated approaches do not account for human identities and rarely incorporate human inputs. In this work, we explore persona-driven red-teaming to advance both automated red-teaming and human-AI collaboration. We first develop PersonaTeaming Workflow, which incorporates personas into the adversarial prompt generation process to explore a wider spectrum of adversarial strategies. Compared to RainbowPlus, a state-of-the-art automated red-teaming method, PersonaTeaming Workflow achieves higher attack success rates while maintaining prompt diversity. However, since automated personas only approximate real human perspectives, we further instantiate PersonaTeaming Workflow as PersonaTeaming Playground, a user-facing interface that enables red-teamers to author their own personas and collaborate with AI to mutate and refine prompts. In a user study with 11 industry practitioners, we found that PersonaTeaming Playground enabled diverse red-teaming strategies and outputs that practitioners perceived as useful, and that AI-generated suggestions in the PersonaTeaming Playground encouraged out-of-the-box thinking even when practitioners did not follow them strictly. Together, our work advances both automated and human-in-the-loop approaches to red-teaming, while shedding light on interaction patterns and design insights for supporting human-AI collaboration in generative AI red-teaming.

</details>


### [16] [Priming, Path-dependence, and Plasticity: Understanding the molding of user-LLM interaction and its implications from (many) chat logs in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05767)
*Shengqi Zhu,Jeffrey M. Rzeszotarski,David Mimno*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对7,955名全球用户的14万次聊天会话分析，发现LLM用户并非白板或持续适应，而是交互模式在早期轨迹中快速形成并稳定；早期探索与纵向结果（如文本模式重复率、留存率）强相关；存在任务类型驱动的表达组织等平行动态。揭示了"能动性悖论"：尽管LLM输入空间无约束且由用户驱动，但用户探索行为反而更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室研究无法揭示真实世界中用户与LLM交互的关键因素（如先前经验和个体探索），因此需要探索通过大规模野外聊天日志研究真实用户-LLM交互的新方法。

Method: 对来自7,955名匿名全球用户的14万次聊天会话进行纵向分析，研究用户表达模式的形成、稳定及与早期探索的关系。

Result: (1) LLM用户交互模式在早期快速形成并稳定，而非持续适应；(2) 早期探索与文本模式重复率、留存率等纵向结果强相关；(3) 存在任务类型（如情感支持）驱动的表达组织及模型版本更新引发的平行动态。

Conclusion: 揭示了"能动性悖论"：LLM输入空间虽无约束且由用户驱动，但实际用户探索行为更少。呼吁在设计研究中关注"塑造过程"及其整合。

Abstract: User interactions with LLMs are shaped by prior experiences and individual exploration, but in-lab studies do not provide system designers with visibility into these in-the-wild factors. This work explores a new approach to studying real-world user-LLM interactions through large-scale chat logs from the wild. Through analysis of 140K chatbot sessions from 7,955 anonymized global users over time, we demonstrate key patterns in user expressions despite varied tasks: (1) LLM users are not tabula rasa, nor are they constantly adapting; rather, interaction patterns form and stabilize rapidly through individual early trajectories; (2) Longitudinal outcomes, such as recurring text patterns and retention rates, are strongly correlated with early exploration; (3) Parallel dynamics are present, including organizing expressions by task types such as emotional support, or in response to model-version updates. These results present an ``agency paradox'': despite LLM input spaces being unconstrained and user-driven, we in fact see less user exploration. We call for design consideration surrounding the molding procedure and its incorporation in future research.

</details>


### [17] [GazeMind: A Gaze-Guided LLM Agent for Personalized Cognitive Load Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05790)
*Bin Wang,Yue Liu,Benjamin Newman,Ajoy S. Fernandes,Zhiyuan Wang,Robert Cavin,Michele A. Cox,Vijay Rajanna,Takumi Bolte,Melissa Hunfalvay,Ulas Bagci,Michael J. Proulx*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GazeMind是一种基于 gaze 引导的 LLM 智能眼镜认知负荷评估框架，结合眼动跟踪与 LLM 推理，实现跨场景、免微调、可解释的认知状态预测，并在最大规模数据集 CogLoad-Bench 上取得 SOTA 性能（领先基线 20%+）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜 AI 助手缺乏对用户内部认知状态的感知能力，无法主动预判用户需求。现有认知负荷评估方法要么依赖不适用于轻量眼镜的不实用传感器，要么基于眼动模型但可解释性差、需任务特定微调且泛化性差。

Method: 提出 GazeMind 框架，将眼动追踪数据编码为结构化表示，供 LLM 推理使用；通过任务引导推理（task-guidance reasoning）实现跨场景泛化且无需 LLM 微调；通过引入用户个性化特征和历史参考实现个性化适配。同时构建 CogLoad-Bench 数据集，包含 152 名参与者、40+小时多模态数据、10000+实时标注。

Result: 在所有指标上超越基线方法 20% 以上，达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: GazeMind 为智能眼镜上的认知负荷评估提供了一种可解释、免微调、跨场景泛化的有效解决方案，显著提升了认知状态感知能力。

Abstract: Smart glasses with AI assistants are increasingly used in daily life. However, current systems lack awareness of the user's internal cognitive state, leaving them unable to proactively anticipate users' needs without access to cognitive load. Existing methods for assessing cognitive load either rely on impractical sensors for lightweight eyewear or utilize eye gaze-based models that suffer from poor interpretability, and require task-specific fine-tuning, often failing to generalize across individuals. We propose GazeMind, a gaze-guided LLM agent framework for cognitive load assessment on smart glasses. It encodes eye-tracking data into structured representations for LLM-based reasoning and provides interpretable cognitive load predictions. Importantly, GazeMind generalizes across scenarios without LLM fine-tuning through a novel task-guidance reasoning approach and achieves personalized adaptation by incorporating user-specific characteristics and historical references. To support evaluation, we introduce CogLoad-Bench, the largest gaze-based cognitive load dataset with 152 participants, 40+ hours of multimodal data, and 10K+ real-time annotations across controlled and real-world tasks. Experiments show that GazeMind achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming baselines by over 20% across all metrics.

</details>


### [18] [Can providing feedback on gaze and mental-effort synchrony improve pair programming performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05836)
*Anahita Golrang,Kshitij Sharma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过双人眼动追踪实验，研究了基于联合视觉注意力和联合心理努力的AI反馈对结对编程协作效果的影响，发现前瞻性反馈比反应性反馈更能提升调试成功率、减少任务时间并提高反馈采纳率，同时更好地维护学习者的自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结对编程作为一种广泛使用的协作学习实践，其有效性常因伙伴间的协调、注意力和认知调节中断而大幅波动，亟需探索如何通过AI支持的反馈来改善协作编程表现。

Method: 采用两项双人眼动追踪实验，实时捕获协作调节指标。研究1测试基于阈值偏离的反应性反馈，研究2评估基于机器学习预测的前瞻性反馈，通过调试成功率、任务时间和代码变更衡量的反馈采纳率来评估效果。

Result: 多模态反馈显著提升协作表现；反应性反馈在结合视觉注意力和心理努力时改善最明显；前瞻性反馈进一步减少任务时间、增加建设性反馈采纳，同时降低干预侵入性；前瞻性反馈更好地维护高绩效结对的学习者自主性。

Conclusion: 凝视和心理努力同步性可作为AI支持协作学习的可靠可操作触发信号，反馈时机、透明度和预期性调节在支持有效结对编程中至关重要。

Abstract: Pair programming is a widely used collaborative learning practice in computer science education yet its effectiveness varies substantially due to breakdowns in coordination attention and cognitive regulation between partners. This paper investigates whether AI supported feedback grounded in joint visual attention and joint mental effort can improve collaborative programming performance and how feedback timing shapes learner AI interaction. Two experimental studies using dual eye tracking capture real time indicators of collaborative regulation during debugging tasks. Study 1 examines reactive feedback that intervenes when observed joint visual attention or joint mental effort deviates beyond predefined thresholds while Study 2 evaluates proactive feedback that forecasts future regulatory breakdowns using machine learning models and intervenes pre emptively. Across both studies feedback effectiveness is assessed through debugging success time on task and feedback uptake reflected in code changes. Multimodal feedback significantly improves collaborative performance compared to no feedback conditions. Reactive feedback yields strong gains in debugging success and efficiency particularly when joint visual attention and joint mental effort based feedback are combined. Proactive forecast based feedback further enhances performance reduces time on task and increases constructive feedback uptake while relying less on intrusive interventions. Proactive feedback better preserves learner agency by maintaining optimal collaboration states, particularly for high-performing pairs. These findings demonstrate that gaze and mental effort synchrony can serve as reliable actionable triggers for AI supported collaborative learning highlighting the importance of feedback timing transparency and anticipatory regulation in supporting effective pair programming.

</details>


### [19] [EventColumn: Integrating Event Sequences into Tabular Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06065)
*Jakob Zethofer,Andreas Hinterreiter,Lukas Schiefermüller,Belgin Mutlu,Marc Streit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EventColumn 是一种新型列类型，将事件序列数据与异构表格属性集成到统一表中，支持在实例和组级别同时比较事件序列与数值、分类和时间属性，已在钢铁行业和公共电商数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只能单独比较事件序列或表格数据，缺乏同时比较两者的能力。与钢铁行业合作者共同开发，以促进生产事件和仓库物流分析。

Method: 提出EventColumn列类型，将事件序列与数值、分类、时间等异构表格属性集成到统一表中。提供压缩概览、热力图组摘要、按事件类型对齐以及相似历史项目的箱线图等功能。

Result: 在钢铁生产物流数据和公共电商数据集上，通过集成Taggle和Microsoft Power BI展示了EventColumn的有效性。

Conclusion: EventColumn 可泛化到多种带有额外表格属性的事件序列数据集，为事件序列与表格数据的联合分析提供了新方案。

Abstract: We introduce EventColumn, a new column type that integrates event-sequence data with heterogeneous tabular attributes into a single unified table. EventColumn lets analysts compare event sequences alongside numerical, categorical, and temporal attributes at both instance and group levels, offering a compressed overview, heatmap group summaries, alignment by event types, and boxplots of similar historical items. We developed EventColumn together with collaborators from the steel industry to facilitate the analysis of production events and warehouse logistics, but the solution generalizes to a wide range of event sequence datasets with additional tabular attributes. Unlike most existing approaches that compare either event sequences or tables, EventColumn supports simultaneous comparison of both. We demonstrate its integration with Taggle and Microsoft Power BI on data from steel production logistics and on a public e-commerce dataset.

</details>


### [20] [AffectGPT-RL: Revealing Roles of Reinforcement Learning in Open-Vocabulary Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06126)
*Zheng Lian,Fan Zhang,Lan Chen,Yazhou Zhang,Rui Liu,Jinyang Wu,Haoyu Chen,Xiaobai Li,Xiaojiang Peng,Bin He,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AffectGPT-RL框架，将强化学习引入开放词汇多模态情感识别（OV-MER），解决传统token级损失与评估指标不匹配的问题，在OV-MER和基础情感识别任务上均取得显著性能提升，并在MER-UniBench上达到最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OV-MER方法使用token级损失进行训练，但该目标与OV-MER评估指标不一致，且这些指标无法通过梯度反向传播直接优化，导致训练与评估之间存在misalignment。

Method: 提出AffectGPT-RL框架，采用强化学习策略优化非可微的评估指标；利用生成式模型捕捉完整情感谱系，并结合情感轮（EW）进行度量计算。

Result: 在OV-MER任务上取得显著性能提升；在基础情感识别任务上达到MER-UniBench上的state-of-the-art结果；实验揭示了推理过程、不同奖励函数的影响以及方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 这是首个探索强化学习在OV-MER中作用的工作，为后续研究提供了有价值的指导；代码将开源以促进未来研究。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Multimodal Emotion Recognition (OV-MER) aims to predict emotions without being constrained by predefined label spaces, thereby enabling fine-grained emotion understanding. Unlike traditional discriminative methods, OV-MER leverages generative models to capture the full spectrum of emotions and employs emotion wheels (EWs) for metric calculation. Previous approaches primarily rely on token-level loss during training. However, this objective is misaligned with the metrics used in OV-MER, and these metrics cannot be directly optimized via gradient backpropagation. To address this limitation, we turn our attention to reinforcement learning, as this strategy can optimize non-differentiable objectives. We term this framework AffectGPT-RL. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments to elucidate the role of reinforcement learning in this task, revealing the necessity of the reasoning process, the impact of different rewards, and the generalizability to other emotion tasks such as sentiment analysis and basic emotion recognition. Experimental results demonstrate that AffectGPT-RL yields significant performance improvements on OV-MER. Beyond this task, we also achieve remarkable performance gains on basic emotion recognition, attaining state-of-the-art results on MER-UniBench. To the best of our knowledge, this is the pioneering work exploring the role of reinforcement learning in OV-MER, providing valuable guidance for subsequent researchers. Our code is provided in the supplementary material and will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [21] [LearnMate^2: Design and Evaluation of an LLM-powered Personalized and Adaptive Support System for Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06257)
*Xinyu Jessica Wang,Christine P. Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用大语言模型设计个性化、自适应的在线学习工具，通过两轮用户实验（n=24, n=16）验证了所提出的\tool{}系统在提升学习效果和用户体验方面优于现有平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习虽普及但缺乏个性化指导，大语言模型的进步为弥补这一缺口提供了契机，因此探索LLM驱动的工具如何支持个性化与自适应学习。

Method: 迭代设计\tool{}系统（包含个性化学习计划、实时上下文帮助和自适应学习活动），先进行初步研究（n=24）评估有效性和可用性并优化系统，再与现有最优在线学习平台+LLM组合进行对比评估（n=16）。

Result: \tool{}在提升学习效果和用户体验方面均优于现有的在线学习与支持工具。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对个性化、AI驱动教育工具设计空间的理解，展示了其在用户体验方面的潜在影响，推动了AI教学法的发展。

Abstract: Personalization is crucial for effective learning, yet online learning, designed for widespread availability and open access, lacks personalized guidance. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) offer opportunities to bridge this gap. We explore how LLM-driven tools may be designed to support personalized and adaptive learning and examine how they shape user experience and learning outcomes. We iteratively designed \tool{} to support online learning by providing personalized study plans, real-time contextual assistance, and adaptive learning activities. A preliminary study ($n=24$) assessed the effectiveness and usability of \tool{} and informed refinements in our system, which we then evaluated ($n = 16$) against a combination of a state-of-the-art online learning platform and an LLM for learning support. Results indicate that \tool{} advances AI pedagogy by improving both learning outcomes and user experience compared to existing online learning and support tools. This work advances our understanding of the design space of personalized, AI-driven educational tools and their potential impact on user experience.

</details>


### [22] [LLM-Based Educational Simulation: Evaluating Temporal Student Persona Stability Across ADHD Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06307)
*Jana Gonnermann-Müller,Jennifer Haase,Nicolas Leins,Thomas Kosch,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探究LLM模拟学生角色的稳定性，发现自述特征在高强度下稳定，但行为表达在非脚本对话中会出现漂移；脚本化互动可消除此漂移，为教师培训和自适应辅导等应用提供设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的学生模拟为教育研究和教师培训提供可扩展方案，但其效度依赖于模型能否在长时间交互中保持稳定的角色身份（persona）。

Method: 采用双重评估框架，分别测量自述特征（self-reported）和观察者评定的行为表达（observer-rated）。通过两个实验，测试四种ADHD角色条件、五种LLM和三种提示设计，评估会话间稳定性（N=4,968）和会话内稳定性（N=3,952，9轮交互）。

Result: 高强度的自述特征保持稳定，是有效行为模拟的必要前提。但观察者评定的行为表达在高和中度ADHD角色下出现会话内漂移；使用脚本化交互（显式任务提示）可完全消除此漂移。

Conclusion: 稳定的、符合角色设定的模拟学习者需要结构化交互设计来维持行为一致性，这对教师培训、自适应辅导及任何需要持续、路径依赖的学习者交互场景具有重要意义。

Abstract: Student simulation with Large language models (LLMs) offers a scalable alternative for educational research and teacher training. Yet, its validity depends on whether models maintain stable personas across extended interactions. We test this prerequisite using a dual-assessment framework measuring self-reported characteristics and observer-rated behavioral expressions. Across two experiments testing four clinically-grounded ADHD persona conditions, five LLMs, and three prompt designs, we quantify between-conversation stability (N=4,968) and within-conversation stability (N=3,952 across 9 turns). Self-reported characteristics remain stable for high intensities, constituting a necessary prerequisite for valid behavioral simulation. Observer-rated behavioral expression reveals selective instability: within-conversation drift occurs in unscripted dialog for high and moderate ADHD personas. Scripted interactions with explicit task prompts eliminate this drift entirely. Stable, persona-aligned simulated learners benefit from a structured interaction design to maintain behavioral coherence, which holds significant implications for teacher training, adaptive tutoring, and any application requiring sustained, path-dependent learner interactions.

</details>


### [23] [Human-AI Co-Evolution and Epistemic Collapse: A Dynamical Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06347)
*Xuening Wu,Yanlan Kang,Qianya Xu,Kexuan Xie,Jiaqi Mi,Honggang Wang,Yubin Liu,Zeping Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一视角，将人类与语言模型视为通过使用、生成和再训练反馈循环耦合的动态系统。通过一个三变量（人类认知、数据质量、模型能力）最小模型，识别出三种动态 regime：协同进化增强、脆弱平衡和退化趋同。仿真表明，过度依赖AI会导致系统向低多样性、次优平衡状态转变，信息论上对应人类-AI回路中的信息瓶颈和熵减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究孤立地考虑人类的认知卸载和递归训练中的模型崩溃，缺乏将两者统一分析的视角。本文希望揭示人类与AI之间反馈循环的整体动力学特性。

Method: 提出一个三变量（人类认知、数据质量、模型能力）的最小动态系统模型，通过数学分析和仿真实验，刻画不同参数下的动态 regime，并从信息论角度（信息瓶颈、熵减）解释观测到的现象。

Result: 识别出三种动态 regime：协同进化增强（良性循环）、脆弱平衡（临界状态）和退化趋同（恶性循环）。仿真证实，增加对AI的依赖会触发向低多样性、次优平衡的相变。

Conclusion: AI系统的演化轨迹不仅受模型设计影响，更深层地由人机协同进化的动力学所塑造。这提醒我们在追求AI能力提升的同时，需警惕反馈回路中信息多样性的丧失。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are reshaping how knowledge is produced, with increasing reliance on AI systems for generation, summarization, and reasoning. While prior work has studied cognitive offloading in humans and model collapse in recursive training, these effects are typically considered in isolation. We propose a unified perspective: humans and language models form a coupled dynamical system linked by a feedback loop of usage, generation, and retraining. We introduce a minimal model with three variables -- human cognition, data quality, and model capability -- and show that this feedback can give rise to distinct dynamical regimes. Our analysis identifies three regimes: co-evolutionary enhancement, fragile equilibrium, and degenerative convergence. Through a simple simulation, we demonstrate that increasing reliance on AI can induce a transition toward a low-diversity, suboptimal equilibrium. From an information-theoretic perspective, this transition corresponds to an emergent information bottleneck in the human-AI loop, where entropy reduction reflects loss of diversity and support under closed-loop feedback rather than beneficial compression. These results suggest that the trajectory of AI systems is shaped not only by model design, but by the dynamics of human-AI co-evolution.

</details>


### [24] [SIGMA-ASL: Sensor-Integrated Multimodal Dataset for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06351)
*Xiaofang Xiao,Guangchao Li,Guangrong Zhao,Qi Lin,Wen Ma,Hongkai Wen,Yanxiang Wang,Yiran Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SIGMA-ASL，一个大规模多模态美国手语识别数据集，集成了RGB-D相机、毫米波雷达和IMU传感器，包含20名参与者、160个ASL手势、93,545个同步多模态片段，并提供了标准化预处理流程和基准协议，旨在推动鲁棒、隐私保护的手语识别系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLR研究主要依赖视觉数据集，存在光照和遮挡敏感、隐私问题突出、跨模态多样性不足等局限，亟需引入互补的多模态数据来克服这些挑战。

Method: 构建包含Azure Kinect RGB-D相机、毫米波雷达和双腕戴IMU的多模态传感框架，在受控工作室环境下采集20名参与者执行160个ASL手势的数据，实现毫秒级跨模态同步，并设计了标准化预处理流程和用户相关/无关的基准评估协议。

Result: 生成了93,545个时间同步的词级多模态片段，通过大量实验验证了数据集质量，并展示了其在单模态和多模态SLR评估中的有效性。

Conclusion: SIGMA-ASL为开发鲁棒、隐私保护且普适的手语识别系统提供了宝贵的多模态数据资源，有望推动SLR研究从单一视觉范式向跨模态融合方向发展。

Abstract: Automatic sign language recognition (SLR) has become a key enabler of inclusive human-computer interaction, fostering seamless communication between deaf individuals and hearing communities. Despite significant advances in multimodal learning, existing SLR research remains dominated by vision-based datasets, which are limited by sensitivity to lighting and occlusion, privacy concerns, and a lack of cross-modal diversity. To address these challenges, we introduce SIGMA-ASL, a large-scale multimodal dataset for SLR. The dataset integrates an Azure Kinect RGB-D camera, a millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar, and two wrist-worn inertial measurement units (IMUs) to capture complementary visual, radio-reflection, and kinematic information. Collected in a controlled studio environment with 20 participants performing 160 common American sign language (ASL) signs, SIGMA-ASL provides 93,545 temporally synchronized word-level multimodal clips. A unified sensing framework achieves millisecond-level alignment across modalities, enabling reliable sensor fusion and cross-modal learning. We further design standardized preprocessing pipelines and benchmarking protocols under both user-dependent and user-independent settings, offering a comprehensive foundation for evaluating single and multimodal SLR. Extensive experiments validate the dataset's quality and demonstrate its potential as a valuable resource for developing robust, privacy-preserving, and ubiquitous sign language recognition systems.

</details>

### [25] [A diagrammatic proof-theoretic semantics for the Greimas semiotic square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05273)
*Michael Fowler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文基于蜘蛛图形式系统，为格雷马斯符号方阵中结构语义学的一个片段建立了图解证明系统，通过推导规则实现矛盾与蕴含关系的转换，并统一刻画了四个元术语的构造过程。研究还表明，图解否定并非布尔补余，而是一种由区域决定的语义对立操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式语义工具难以捕捉符号学方阵中元术语之间的结构关系，尤其是“+”操作缺乏基于推导的语义解释。作者希望用图解推理的形式系统弥补这一空白。

Method: 以蜘蛛图为底层形式系统，定义基本项为图解配置，将矛盾与蕴含关系解释为推理规则下的图变换；通过轮廓引入与生境变换规则构成统一推导模式，证明四个元术语均可由合取对偶配置推导得出。

Result: 四个元术语均可通过固定推导模式（轮廓引入+生境变换规则）从合取基本配置一致地推导出来；图解否定被刻画为一种受限于区域的语义对立操作，而非经典布尔否定的补余。

Conclusion: 该框架为结构语义学中的组合操作提供了基于推导的语义解释，扩展了蜘蛛图的表达能力，并展示了图解推理如何用于形式化非经典语义操作。

Abstract: We develop a diagrammatic proof system for a fragment of structural semantics inspired by the Greimas semiotic square, using spider diagrams as the underlying formalism. The basic terms are represented as diagrammatic configurations, and the relations of contradiction and implication are interpreted as transformations governed by a set of inference rules. These transformations are realised as derivations, with proof trees serving as witnesses. Our main result shows that the construction of the four meta-terms can be captured uniformly: each is derivable from a conjunctive pair of basic configurations via a fixed derivation schema composed of contour introduction and habitat transformation rules. This yields a proof-theoretic account of the combinatorial operation underlying meta-term formation, and provides a semantic interpretation of the Greimasian operation `+' as a derivational construction rather than a logical combination. We further show that diagrammatic negation in this setting is not a Boolean complement, but a restricted, zone-determined semantic counter-position, reflecting the relational character of opposition in structural semiotics. The resulting framework provides a compositional, rule-based semantics in which complex configurations are generated constructively from simpler ones. In addition to extending the expressive scope of spider diagram calculi, the system illustrates how diagrammatic reasoning can be used to formalise non-classical semantic operations within a unified inferential setting.

</details>


### [26] [Paraconsistent Semantics for Extended Fuzzy Logic Programs via Approximation Fixpoint Theory [Extended Version]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05286)
*Pascal Kettmann,Hannes Strass,Jesse Heyninck,Jeroen Spaans*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文利用近似不动点理论，为同时包含“失败否定”和强否定的模糊逻辑程序定义了良好行为的语义，该框架推广了多种现有语义并产生了一系列新语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑程序中的否定可以有多种解释，最著名的是“失败否定”（没有证据证明p为真则not p为真）和强否定（需要有证据证明p为假）。为同时包含这两种否定的逻辑程序定义语义是一项挑战性任务，当与模糊逻辑等其他扩展结合时更为困难。

Method: 采用近似不动点理论框架（approximating fixpoint theory）来构建包含失败否定和强否定的模糊逻辑程序的语义。

Result: 该框架推广了多种现有的语义，并产生了一系列新的语义。

Conclusion: 近似不动点理论为包含两种否定的模糊逻辑程序提供了统一且可推广的语义基础。

Abstract: In logic programming, negation can be interpreted in various ways. Probably best known is the concept of "negation as failure", where "$\mathit{not}\, p$" is true if we have no evidence for $p$. On the other hand, strong negation requires that we have evidence for $p$ being false. Defining semantics for logic programs containing both kinds of negation is a challenging task, and this becomes even more challenging when combining this with other extensions of logic programming, e.g. fuzziness. In this work, we use the framework of approximating fixpoint theory to formulate well-behaved semantics for fuzzy logic programs containing both "by-failure" and strong negation. We show that this framework generalizes several existing semantics as well as giving rise to a host of new semantics.

</details>


### [27] [A Practical Specification Language for Automatic Quantum Program Verification (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05786)
*Wei-Lun Tsai,Yu-Fang Chen,Ondřej Lengál*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种扩展的基于集合的量子程序规约语言，并实现从规约到自动机的线性时间转换算法，从而支持全自动的Hoare风格量子程序验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hoare风格的量子程序验证虽然原理完善，但无法实现全自动验证；基于自动机的验证方法虽然可扩展，但将高层基于集合的断言翻译为自动机时存在指数级爆炸问题，严重限制了实用性。

Method: 引入扩展的基于集合的规约语言，并提出规约到自动机的翻译算法，通过受控自动机构造和量子比特重排序，将复杂度从指数级降低为量子比特数的线性级。

Result: 生成的紧凑自动机使得固定量子比特的量子程序的全自动Hoare风格验证能够在以前不可行的规模上进行，同时显著提高了表达能力而不损失效率。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持效率的前提下大幅提升表达力，使全自动Hoare风格量子程序验证达到此前无法企及的规模。

Abstract: Hoare-style verification provides a principled foundation for reasoning about the correctness of quantum programs, but existing approaches do not allow fully automatic verification. While automata-based verification scales well when specifications are given directly as automata, prior frameworks incur exponential blow-up when translating high-level set-based assertions into automata, which severely limits practicality. We introduce an extended set-based specification language and a specification-to-automata translation algorithm whose complexity is linear in the number of qubits, enabled by controlled automaton construction and qubit reordering. The resulting compact automata enable fully automatic Hoare-style verification of fixed-qubit quantum programs at previously infeasible scales, while substantially improving expressiveness without compromising efficiency.

</details>

Conclusion: 动态认知逻辑为分析带错误的分布式自纠错协议提供了有效的形式化工具；自纠错可无需中央协调实现，但会对协议的最优性产生约束；相比有界内存和全信息协议，自纠错机制在纠错能力和资源需求之间呈现不同的权衡特性。

Abstract: We investigate self-correcting gossip protocols with errors. In distributed computing, protocols with errors have been widely investigated in temporal epistemic logics. Instead, we propose a dynamic epistemic logic. We show how to correct transmission errors due to faulty messages without a central authority coordinating protocol execution, how this affects optimality, and how this compares to bounded memory and full information protocols.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将符号结构从线性整数算术扩展到任意可接受标准模型的基础理论，并利用符号模型性质证明更广泛的一阶逻辑片段的可判定性。

Method: 1. 泛化符号结构，使其支持任意具备标准模型的基础理论。2. 定义符号模型性质：若每个可满足公式均存在符号模型，则该性质成立。3. 针对放松量词交替约束、允许自循环函数的分片公式扩展片段，通过从任意模型构造符号模型来建立符号模型性质。4. 构造与正确性证明采用泛化方式，可实例化到其他受限片段。

Result: 证明了若干扩展的分片公式片段的可判定性，这些片段放宽了量词交替约束，允许在特定限制下引入自循环函数。

Conclusion: 符号模型性质是一种通用且有效的可判定性证明工具，其构造方法可迁移至其他类似受限的一阶逻辑片段。

Abstract: Recently, symbolic structures were proposed as finite representations of potentially infinite first-order structures, where Linear Integer Arithmetic terms and formulas define the domain and interpretations of a structure. We generalize symbolic structures to use any base theory that admits a standard model. Symbolic structures induce a symbolic model property, which holds for a fragment of first-order logic if every satisfiable formula in the fragment has a symbolic model. The symbolic model property implies decidability, since the model-checking problem for symbolic structures is decidable. We use the symbolic model property to prove decidability for several fragments that extend the fragment of stratified formulas, relaxing the quantifier-alternation constraints by allowing one sort to have self-looping functions, under certain restrictions. To establish the symbolic model property for these fragments we construct a symbolic model for a formula from an arbitrary model. The construction and its correctness are proved in a generic fashion, which may be instantiated to other similarly restricted fragments.

</details>

*Elena Di Lavore,Jonas Forster,Mario Román*

TL;DR: 本文提出分级余代数（graded coalgebras of graded monads）来建模连续时间迁移系统，发展了包括分级分配律、终余代数存在条件、分支时间与迹语义等理论，并与Feller-Dynkin过程建立联系，同时给出了余代数模态逻辑的不变性与表达性判据。

Method: 引入分级余代数（graded coalgebras of graded monads）概念；发展分级分配律理论；给出终余代数存在条件；定义分支时间语义和迹语义；建立与Feller-Dynkin过程的联系；构造余代数模态逻辑并给出不变性与表达性判据。

Abstract: Functor coalgebras capture a wide range of transition systems that must however evolve in discrete steps. We introduce graded coalgebras of graded monads and propose them to model continuous-time transition systems. We develop the theory of graded coalgebras-including graded distributive laws between graded monads-and we give conditions for the existence of terminal coalgebras. We define both branching-time and trace semantics, linking them to recent work on Feller-Dynkin processes. Finally, we develop coalgebraic modal logics for both process semantics and state criteria for invariance and expressivity.

</details>

*Piotr Kozicki,Alex Kavvos*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过构建塔斯基对偶和托马森对偶的关系扩展，建立了关系框架（作为计算系统）与代数（作为逻辑谓词）之间的范畴对偶，并将系统间的关系（如互模拟）与谓词间的关系对应起来，进而衍生出跨系统的证明系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克里普克语义学可以通过范畴对偶从关系框架导出代数，但现有对偶仅关注系统与谓词之间的对应，未能将系统间的关系（如互模拟）与谓词间的关系对应起来。本文旨在将这一现象扩展到关系层面。

Method: 通过构造塔斯基对偶（针对无穷经典命题逻辑）和托马森对偶（针对无穷经典模态逻辑）的特定关系扩展，将系统间的良好关系（如互模拟）与谓词间的关系建立对应。

Result: 成功构建了关系扩展的对偶，将计算系统间的关系与逻辑谓词间的关系对应起来，并据此勾勒出一个跨系统的证明系统。

Conclusion: 范畴对偶不仅能在系统与谓词之间建立对应，还能将系统间的关系映射到谓词间的关系，从而为跨系统的逻辑推理提供形式化基础。

Abstract: The Kripke semantics of various logics arises via categorical dualities between a category of relational frames and their maps, and a category of algebras and logical homomorphisms. When the relational frames are considered as computational systems (e.g. the states of a machine), the corresponding algebra is one of logical predicates on these systems (e.g. predicates on these states, i.e. program logics). Our aim is to extend this phenomenon to relations, putting well-behaved relations between systems (e.g. bisimulations) in correspondence with relations between predicates. This is achieved by constructing particular relational extensions of Tarski duality (for infinitary classical propositional logic) and Thomason duality (for infinitary classical modal logic). We sketch how these dualities give rise to a proof system that relates formulae between different systems.

</details>

### [32] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯递归投影校准(BRPC)框架，通过分离无差异粒子更新和条件高斯过程差异更新，在线处理流式数据中的模型偏差与非平稳性，并集成重启机制应对突变，在理论和实证上优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-差异混杂问题且是离线静态的，不适配数字孪生中系统随时间演化、存在渐变漂移和突变 regime shift 的动态场景；而数据同化方法虽可序贯更新但一般不显式建模系统偏差，在突变下效果较差。

Method: 将投影校准扩展到在线设定：(1) 分离差异-free的粒子更新（用于校准参数）和条件高斯过程更新（用于差异函数），保持可辨识性；(2) 集成重启机制检测 regime shift 并重置校准过程，以处理突变。

Result: 在合成数据和植物仿真基准上，BRPC在渐变条件下校准精度提升；重启增强的BRPC在突变条件下进一步提升了鲁棒性和预测性能，优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线。

Conclusion: BRPC框架为数字孪生中在线、非平稳环境下的偏差感知校准提供了理论保证（渐变跟踪性能、重启误报与检测行为）和实证有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [33] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上统一了无监督预训练中的互信息技能学习（MISL）与目标条件强化学习（GCRL），将两者解释为控制最大化问题。研究发现三种典型GCRL公式本质不等价，但均可理解为目标敏感度最大化；MISL目标则对应技能敏感度。通过建立MISL目标与下游GCRL目标敏感度之间的上界关系，论文为每种GCRL公式找到了匹配的MISL预训练目标，为强化学习预训练提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MISL方法在实践中能学出多样性技能并用于下游目标达成，但其理论原因不明。GCRL和MISL皆为宽泛术语，包含多种不同定义，缺乏统一的理论框架来解释为何MISL学习的技能能够支持目标达成。

Method: 1) 将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化框架；2) 识别三种典型GCRL公式并证明其本质不等价；3) 揭示GCRL策略的本质是未来轨迹对目标的高度敏感；4) 证明MISL目标与GCRL目标敏感度之间存在上界关系，建立精确对应。

Result: 三种GCRL公式本质不等价，可导致同一环境下不兼容的最优策略；所有GCRL公式共享"目标敏感度"解释；MISL目标与GCRL目标敏感度之间存在上界关系，每种GCRL公式都有匹配的MISL目标。

Conclusion: 为无监督RL预训练奠定了理论基础，揭示了技能多样性与下游目标敏感度之间的精确对应关系，为实际应用中选择预训练目标提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [34] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略优化量化模型的权重分配，通过迭代微调少量权重的量化状态，以提升预训练量化模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备、自主系统等场景中受限于复杂度和内存大小，量化是重要的压缩手段，但基于最近邻量化的方法无法保证最优精度。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法，每次迭代仅改变少量权重的值，将其调整到不同的量化状态，通过合适的算子与参数设置快速提升量化模型精度。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet（图像分类与检测）以及自编码器架构上验证了该方法能够有效提升量化模型的准确率。

Conclusion: 进化策略搭配合适的算子与参数，能够在预训练量化模型上快速提升精度，表明最近邻量化并非最优策略。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [35] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过将动量机制引入线性注意力（LA）的在线SGD更新中，并设计分块并行算法和稳定门控约束，在保持训练效率的同时显著提升了长序列建模性能，在400M和1.3B参数规模上优于Transformer、Mamba2和GDN等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型（如Mamba2、GDN）将线性递归解释为闭式在线SGD，但朴素SGD更新存在信息快速衰减和优化次优的问题。动量优化器虽能缓解，但在训练效率和有效性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 设计了分块并行算法，通过几何重排更新系数实现逐步骤动量规则；2) 从动力系统视角分析动量递归为二阶系统，引入复共轭特征值，据此设计稳定门控约束；3) 基于Triton内核实现，使训练吞吐量与Mamba2、KDA等竞争模型相当。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数规模上的大量实验表明，MDN在多种下游评测基准上一致优于Transformer、Mamba2和GDN等强基线模型。

Conclusion: 动量机制结合分块并行算法和稳定门控约束，能有效克服线性注意力中SGD更新的信息衰减和次优收敛问题，实现训练效率与效果的双赢，为线性注意力模型提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [36] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种训练递归智能体的强化学习方法，使其能够递归地生成和委派子任务，实现推理时扩展，在训练效率、上下文窗口扩展、泛化能力和计算效率上优于单智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单智能体系统在处理长上下文和复杂任务时面临瓶颈，需要一种能够通过分治策略递归分解任务、在推理时动态扩展能力的训练方法。

Method: 采用强化学习框架训练递归智能体，使其学会何时以及如何委派子任务并进行通信，实现递归式的任务分解与执行。

Result: 递归智能体训练效率更高；能够扩展到超出模型上下文窗口的任务；能泛化到远高于训练难度的任务；相比单智能体系统降低了实际运行时间。

Conclusion: RAO提供了一种有效的递归智能体训练范式，通过推理时扩展和分治策略显著提升了智能体的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>
