Motivated by this, we propose KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT), a continued pretraining procedure that incentivizes the emergence of compressible representations. We introduce a train-time KV sparsification policy that masks KV slots during training. This forces the model to use fewer KV slots and encourages it to learn representations amenable to post-hoc compression. Empirically, we show that KV-CAT improves the quality-budget tradeoff of downstream compression methods across retrieval, long-context question answering, and perplexity-based evaluation of compressed-prefix continuation.

</details>


### [111] [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975)
*Sicheng Ma,Tianyue Yang,Xiuzhe Wu,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型，用于从低保真观测数据快速重建高保真流场。在三个流体力学基准上，蒸馏后的学生模型在保持教师模型频谱性能的同时，参数量减半，推理速度提升12倍，且在相同训练预算下相比直接训练的单步模型SSIM提升23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流匹配模型依赖迭代采样，计算成本高，难以适用于集合预报、实时可视化等对延迟敏感的科学流场重建任务。作者希望将高保真生成模型压缩为紧凑的单步模型，在保证重建质量的同时大幅提升推理速度。

Method: 将最优传输流匹配（OT flow-matching）教师模型蒸馏为一致性模型（consistency model）。推理时，将低保真观测数据沿传输路径添加噪声作为生成轨迹的初始点，使无条件的流匹配模型能够执行条件重建，无需重新训练教师模型。

Result: 在烟羽浮力、湍流通道流和科尔莫戈罗夫流三个流体基准上，蒸馏学生模型在频谱指标上保持教师模型性能，参数量减少约50%，推理速度提升12倍。相同训练预算下，蒸馏学生模型相比从零训练的单步一致性模型SSIM提升23.1%。

Conclusion: 教师蒸馏不仅加速采样，还提升了训练效率，为将高容量科学生成模型转化为更快速训练、更低成本运行、更易部署的紧凑重建模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity flow fields from low-fidelity observations is a central problem in scientific machine learning, yet recent diffusion and flow-matching models typically rely on iterative sampling, making them costly for latency-sensitive workflows such as ensemble forecasting, real-time visualization, and simulation-in-the-loop inference. We study whether a high-fidelity flow-matching generative model can be compressed into a compact one-step model for fast scientific flow reconstruction. Our approach distills an optimal-transport flow-matching teacher into a one-step consistency model. Low-fidelity observations are incorporated at inference by initializing the generative trajectory from a noised observation along the transport path, allowing an unconditional high-fidelity flow model to perform conditional reconstruction without retraining the teacher. We evaluate this distillation strategy on three fluid benchmarks, Smoke Buoyancy, Turbulent Channel Flow, and Kolmogorov Flow, using coarse-to-fine reconstruction as a controlled testbed at field sizes up to $256 \times 256$. Across these settings, the distilled student retains similar performance of the teacher's model on spectrum metrics, while using roughly half as many parameters and achieving a $12\times$ inference speedup over the flow-matching teacher. Under the same training budget, the distilled student also outperforms a one-step consistency model trained directly from scratch by $23.1\%$ in SSIM, showing that teacher distillation improves training efficiency rather than merely accelerating sampling. These results suggest a promising route for turning future high-capacity scientific generative models into compact reconstruction models that are faster to train, cheaper to run, and easier to deploy.

</details>


### [112] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半空间（halfspaces）的细粒度一致收敛行为，超越了最坏情况下的VC界。对于$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d\ge 2$）中的非齐次半空间，标准一阶VC界本质上是紧的；而对于$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间，则表现出截然不同的行为：可实现情形下误差为$O(1/n)$，agnostic情形下通过关键楔形局部化论证得到了无对数的带状偏差界，且$\ln\ln n$的额外开销是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的VC界对半空间的学习问题只给出最坏情况下的保证，而实际中不同结构和维度下的半空间可能表现出更精细的收敛行为。本文旨在探索半空间一致收敛的细粒度刻画，揭示维度与结构（齐次vs非齐次）对收敛速率的关键影响。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，对非齐次半空间证明了标准一阶VC界的紧性（下界构造）；对二维齐次半空间，在可实现情形下利用几何结构推导$O(1/n)$误差界，在agnostic情形下提出"关键楔形局部化"（critical-wedge localization）论证，在每一dyadic风险带上建立无对数的偏差界，并通过并集界引入仅$\ln\ln n$的额外开销。

Result: (1) 对于$d\ge 2$的非齐次半空间，可实现情形下一致假设的误差下界为$\Theta(d\ln(n/d)/n)$，agnostic情形下偏差随真实误差$\tau$按$\sqrt{\tau\ln(1/\tau)}$缩放，证明标准VC界本质紧。(2) 对于$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间，可实现情形误差为$O(1/n)$；agnostic情形得到带状对数-free偏差界，整体并集后仅额外损失$\ln\ln n$，且匹配下界表明该开销不可避免。

Conclusion: 本文给出了半空间一致收敛问题的细粒度且近乎完整的刻画，揭示了维度（$d=2$ vs $d\ge 2$）和结构（齐次vs非齐次）是决定收敛行为的关键阈值条件。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息充分性框架理论分析多LLM系统中的决策问题，证明投票和辩论方法的信息结构不会优于所有智能体私有信息的汇集。基于此理论，提出一种通过估计每个智能体后验分布并采用后验乘积估计器近似汇集后验的实用方法，在六个QA基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投票和辩论的多LLM聚合方法缺乏理论基础和形式化保证，无法确保决策的信息充分性，因此需要一种具有理论保障的原则性方法。

Method: 使用Blackwell信息充分性框架对多LLM决策进行理论分析，证明投票和辩论诱导的信息结构不会优于所有智能体私有信息的贝叶斯汇集；提出基于后验乘积估计器近似贝叶斯汇集后验的实用方法。

Result: 在六个QA基准测试上，所提方法优于当前最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯汇集后验最大化是Blackwell序下的信息理论上界决策规则；基于后验乘积估计的实用方法可有效逼近这一上界。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

</details>


### [114] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（4,218次运行）系统评估了合成数据在时间序列预测中的增强效果，发现其效果高度依赖模型架构：通道混合模型（TimesNet, iTransformer）多数受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear, PatchTST）持续退化。仅在低资源场景下，TimesNet使用10%数据+合成增强可超越全数据基线。总体而言，67%的实验显示增强有害，仅季节性-趋势生成器可靠，硬课程切换导致MSE增加24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在语言模型训练中已广泛应用，但在时间序列预测中的作用尚不明确，缺乏系统性的大规模实证研究来指导其使用。

Method: 设计了9个实验组、4,218次运行，系统评估了跨5种架构（TimesNet, iTransformer, DLinear, PatchTST等）、4种合成信号、7个数据集的时序数据增强效果。

Result: 通道混合模型在多数试验中受益，通道独立模型持续退化；低资源场景下TimesNet用10%数据+合成增强可超越全数据基线；总体67%试验中增强有害；仅季节性-趋势生成器可靠；硬课程切换导致MSE恶化24%。

Conclusion: 提供了具体可操作的指导原则：合成增强应配合通道混合架构使用；采用渐进式退火调度；低资源增强需考虑架构和数据集依赖性。

Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

</details>


### [115] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输的选择性奖励模型（SelectiveRM），通过联合一致性差异和质量松弛机制，自动排除噪声偏好样本，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的奖励模型常受偏好噪声污染，现有去噪方法基于同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂异质性，导致模型过拟合错误标注。

Method: 基于最优传输理论，设计联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy）对齐模型预测与偏好数据分布；引入质量松弛（Mass Relaxation）机制，通过部分传输自动排除语义不一致的噪声样本。

Result: 理论证明SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险更紧的上界；实验在多个基准上显著超过SOTA基线。

Conclusion: SelectiveRM通过最优传输框架有效处理了RLHF中的复杂偏好噪声，为奖励模型训练提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [116] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Feather是一种基于强化学习的前缀感知调度器，通过轻量级数据结构CHT实现快速前缀检测，在保持批处理大小与前缀同质性之间的最优权衡下，相比现有调度器实现了2-10倍的端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型自回归解码过程中，KV缓存访问是内存瓶颈。现有调度器虽然会利用前缀共享来减少KV缓存内存占用，但未能识别出更小、更同质的前缀批次可能带来更高的解码吞吐量。同时，现有前缀检测依赖基数树遍历，CPU开销巨大，常与GPU执行时间相当。

Method: 提出Feather调度器，使用强化学习学习批处理大小与前缀同质性之间的最优权衡；引入Chunked Hash Tree (CHT)轻量级数据结构，支持快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免昂贵的树遍历开销。将Feather集成到vLLM和SGLang推理引擎中。

Result: 相比现有调度器，Feather实现了2-10倍的端到端吞吐提升；在前缀共享不足的工作负载下性能不劣于现有方案；通过减少KV缓存访问总量，超越了具有相同目标的前缀感知注意力内核的性能。

Conclusion: 前缀共享工作负载中，更小但更同质的前缀批次在解码吞吐上优于更大的异构批次，Feather通过RL和CHT有效实现了这一优化策略，显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

</details>


### [117] [TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06047)
*Duong Nguyen,Mohammed Jawhar,Nicolas Chesneau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TFM-Retouche，一种轻量级、架构无关的输入空间残差适配器，通过在输入空间学习残差修正来对齐输入数据与预训练表格基础模型的归纳偏好，无需微调模型权重，且在适配无效时可回退到原始模型。在TabArena-Lite基准上，TabICLv2-Retouche在51个数据集上取得排行榜第一，Elo评分提升+56，处于预测质量与训练/推理时间的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFMs）在零样本场景下表现优异，但其归纳偏好在推理时固定不变。适应特定数据集需要昂贵的全微调或需针对内部架构定制的参数高效微调方法（如LoRA），且权重空间微调对精度或校准的提升效果存在争议。因此需要一种轻量、架构无关、且能保证适配有效性的方法。

Method: 提出TFM-Retouche，一个输入空间残差适配器。它在输入数据上学习一个小的残差修正，通过冻结的TFM进行端到端训练。包含一个训练后的恒等守卫（identity guard），当适配在验证集上无帮助时回退到未修改的TFM。该适配器与TFM的内部架构无关（architecture-agnostic）。

Result: 在TabArena-Lite（51个数据集，涵盖二分类、多分类和回归）上，基于TabICLv2实例化的TabICLv2-Retouche在排行榜上排名第一，相比冻结的TabICLv2基线，Elo评分提升+56，并且位于预测质量与训练/推理时间的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: TFM-Retouche提供了一种轻量、高效、架构无关的表格基础模型自适应方法，通过输入空间修正避免了权重微调的高成本和不确定收益，同时通过恒等守卫保证了安全性，在多个任务上取得了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs), such as TabPFN-2.6, TabICLv2, ConTextTab, Mitra, LimiX, and TabDPT, achieve strong zero-shot performance through in-context learning, but their inductive biases remain fixed at inference time. Adapting a pretrained TFM to a specific dataset or task typically requires either full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive, or parameter-efficient tuning methods (PEFT) such as LoRA, which must be tailored to the internal architecture of each TFM. Furthermore, the evidence on whether weight-space fine-tuning improves accuracy or calibration is mixed \citep{tanna_exploring_2026,rubachev_finetuning_2025}. We introduce TFM-Retouche, a lightweight input-space residual adapter that is architecture-agnostic by design with respect to the frozen TFM backbone. TFM-Retouche learns a small residual correction in the input space to align the input data with the inductive biases of the pretrained model. The adapter is trained end-to-end through the frozen TFM, with a post-training identity guard that falls back to the unmodified TFM whenever adaptation does not help on held-out validation. On TabArena-Lite (51 datasets spanning binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression), TabICLv2-Retouche -- the framework instantiated on TabICLv2 -- is the top-ranked method on the leaderboard with light per-task tuning and ensembling, lifting aggregate Elo by +56 over the frozen TabICLv2 base and sitting on the Pareto front of predictive quality versus both training and inference time.

</details>


### [118] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何用部分生成甚至仅输入信息来估计LLM的不确定性，提出了统一框架，以及Logit Magnitude（基于部分生成前缀的不确定性估计）和MetaUE（基于输入提示的轻量级不确定性估计器）两种低成本方法，实验表明有效的不确定性估计所需生成量比通常假设的更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险应用（如医疗、金融）中，LLM的幻觉可能流畅但事实错误，用户难以判断输出是否可信。现有不确定性估计方法需要完整自回归生成，推理成本高且延迟评估，因此需要探索更低成本的早期不确定性估计方法。

Method: 1) 提出统一框架，将不确定性估计形式化为自回归生成过程中的早期估计问题，按信息观测量（从多代到输入仅预测）组织现有和提出的估计器。2) 提出Logit Magnitude，利用早期停止生成前缀的top-M logit证据来估计不确定性。3) 提出MetaUE，将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为轻量级的仅输入估计器。

Result: 在通用和领域特定基准上的大量实验表明：Logit Magnitude取得了强性能，LLM的部分生成通常足以有效估计不确定性；MetaUE在多种设置下提供了有竞争力的仅输入近似。

Conclusion: 有效的不确定性估计所需的生成量比通常假设的更少，使得不可靠响应能够被更早识别，从而降低推理成本和延迟。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

</details>


### [119] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoExVQA是一个自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计实现可验证的推理过程，在PFL-DocVQA数据集上ANLS提升12%，达到可解释DocVQA的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DocVQA模型将问题相关证据定位与答案定位纠缠在一起，作为黑箱运行，缺乏验证预测如何依赖视觉证据的手段。

Method: CoExVQA通过链式解释设计实现可解释推理：首先识别问题相关证据，然后显式定位答案区域，最后仅从该定位区域解码答案。

Result: 在PFL-DocVQA数据集上，ANLS比现有可解释基线提升12%，达到可解释DocVQA的SOTA性能，同时提供透明且可验证的预测。

Conclusion: 将解码限制在已定位的证据区域可以实现更透明、可验证的DocVQA推理，且不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [120] [Geometry-Aware Simplicial Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06061)
*Elena Xinyi Wang,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何单纯形Weisfeiler-Lehman (GSWL) 检验，将顶点坐标融入颜色细化过程，用于区分具有相同连通性但不同嵌入的几何单纯复形。GSWL界定了几何感知单纯形消息传递方案的表达能力，并结合欧拉特征变换提供了完整的几何表达性刻画与近似框架。实验验证了从组合模型到几何感知模型的层次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的WL检验及其单纯形扩展(SWL)仅能区分组合结构（连通性），而对几何结构（嵌入）视而不见，即具有相同连接方式但不同顶点坐标的网格无法被区分。需要一种能够感知几何信息的检验方法。

Method: 提出GSWL检验，将顶点坐标信息纳入颜色细化过程；理论上证明几何感知单纯形消息传递的表达能力受GSWL界定，且存在参数使GSWL的区分能力与这些方案在有限家族上匹配；结合欧拉特征变换(ECT)构建完整不变性与近似框架。

Result: GSWL成功区分了仅靠组合信息无法区分的几何单纯复形；建立了从组合WL→单纯形SWL→几何GSWL的表达性层次；与ECT结合可获得完整的几何不变性刻画。

Conclusion: GSWL填补了单纯形消息传递在几何感知方面的理论空白，提供了几何表达性的理论表征和实用近似框架，实验验证了理论层次的有效性。

Abstract: The Weisfeiler--Lehman (WL) test and its simplicial extension (SWL) characterize the combinatorial expressivity of message passing networks, but they are blind to geometry, i.e., meshes with identical connectivity but different embeddings are indistinguishable. We introduce the Geometric Simplicial Weisfeiler--Lehman (GSWL) test, which incorporates vertex coordinates into color refinement for geometric simplicial complexes. In addition, we show that (i) the expressivity of geometry-aware simplicial message passing schemes is bounded above by GSWL, and (ii) that there exist parameters such that the discriminating power of GSWL is matched by these schemes on any fixed finite family of geometric simplicial complexes. Combined with the Euler Characteristic Transform (ECT), a complete invariant for geometric simplicial complexes, this yields a geometric expressivity characterization together with an approximation framework. Experiments on synthetic and mesh datasets serve to validate our theory, showing a clear hierarchy from combinatorial to geometry-aware models.

</details>


### [121] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MTG-Causal-RL，一个基于《万智牌》的因果强化学习基准，包含高维部分观测、大规模掩码动作空间、显式结构因果模型（SCM）以及标准统计评估协议。实验表明，掩码PPO和所提出的CGFA-PPO达到了具有竞争力的胜率，且因果因子级校准轨迹揭示了标量胜率无法捕获的诊断结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果强化学习缺乏能够同时结合序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准。现有基准无法同时支持因果信用分配、跨架构迁移和策略可审计性等核心问题的研究。

Method: 构建基于《万智牌》的Gymnasium基准，包含3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散空间、五种标准赛制套牌、三种奖励方案及手写SCM。提出CGFA-PPO，使用SCM中胜率父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并引入干预校准损失。采用配对种子、配对自助法置信区间和Holm-Bonferroni校正进行统计比较。

Result: 掩码PPO和CGFA-PPO在分布内达到竞争性胜率并超过随机基线；每因子校准轨迹和留一法跨架构迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法单独捕获的诊断结构。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL为因果RL、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个共享测试平台，能够同时支持掩码动作空间下的因果信用分配、跨架构结构性迁移和基于SCM的策略可审计性等现有基准无法共同提出的问题。

Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

</details>


### [122] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: nGPT架构由于将权重和隐层表示约束在单位超球面上，对低精度（4-bit）算术具有内在鲁棒性，无需额外干预即可实现稳定的NVFP4训练，且这种优势随隐层维度增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以4-bit精度训练大语言模型对效率至关重要，但标准架构在低精度下模型质量会下降，通常需要随机Hadamard变换和逐张量缩放等干预措施来维持质量，作者希望消除这些额外操作。

Method: 分析nGPT（将权重和隐层表示约束在单位超球面上的架构）在4-bit精度下的表现；从点积角度解释鲁棒性：超球面约束增强了逐元素乘积中弱正相关的信号积累，同时噪声保持不相关，从而获得更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失景观。

Result: 在1.2B密集模型和高达3B/30B的混合(Mamba-Transformer)MoE模型上验证了该方法，实现了无需额外干预的稳定端到端NVFP4训练，且优势随隐层维度增大而增强。

Conclusion: nGPT架构天然适应低精度训练，随模型规模扩大优势更明显，为大语言模型的低精度高效训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

</details>


### [123] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张扩散模型的泛化理解需要全新理论框架，指出记忆与泛化在此类模型中不相容，并通过CIFAR-10实验提出从"为何不记忆"转向"预记忆阶段学到什么"的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计学习理论和良性过拟合范式无法解释扩散模型的泛化行为，因为扩散模型中训练数据记忆与生成新样本存在根本性冲突。

Method: 基于立场分析（position paper），结合CIFAR-10数据集上的实证研究，提炼出关键开放性问题。

Result: 指出容量限制、隐式正则化、架构归纳偏置等现有理论解释间的关系尚不明确，并提出了具体开放性问题。

Conclusion: 研究重心应从解释扩散模型为何不记忆，转向探究预记忆阶段模型实际学习的内容。

Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

</details>


### [124] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多分类softmax交叉熵中的广义Gauss-Newton（GGN）曲率矩阵，提出了一种精确分解方法，将其拆分为"真类vs其余"项和"类内协方差"项，并据此设计了Fast Gauss-Newton（FGN）方法，通过丢弃后者实现对完整GGN的半正定低秩近似。FGN在二分类情况下精确，且其阻尼更新可转化为等价的whitened行空间系统，通过共轭梯度法无矩阵求解。实验验证了FGN在竞争类质量集中或阻尼较大时最接近完整GGN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分类softmax交叉熵下，完整GGN曲率通过softmax协方差耦合所有输出logits，导致曲率-向量乘积在大类别数场景下计算难以扩展。

Method: 1) 将多分类GGN精确分解为"真类vs其余"项（保留）和"类内协方差"项（丢弃）；2) 利用精确的标量间隔表示（scalar-margin representation）推导FGN；3) 将阻尼更新转化为whitened行空间系统，利用共轭梯度法结合Jacobian-vector乘积和vector-Jacobian乘积无矩阵求解。

Result: FGN在竞争类质量集中或阻尼较大时最接近完整softmax GGN；当丢弃的类内协方差增长时，FGN与完整GGN的偏差增大。

Conclusion: FGN通过精确的曲率分解和近似，在保持计算效率的同时，在关键场景下与完整GGN高度接近，为大规模多分类任务中的二阶优化提供了可行的近似方案。

Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT通过将RTG信息注入状态表示中而非作为独立token，将序列长度减少三分之一，在D4RL基准上超越标准DT并达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Decision Transformer中RTG作为标量token包含信息远少于状态/动作向量，却消耗相同计算预算，且自注意力复杂度随序列长度二次增长，RTG作为独立token带来不必要的计算开销。

Method: SlimDT将RTG信息在序列建模前注入状态表示中，移除RTG作为独立token，使Transformer仅处理紧凑的(状态, 动作)序列，序列长度减少三分之一。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，SlimDT在各种任务中超越标准DT，性能与现有SOTA方法相当。

Conclusion: 将稀疏的条件信号从信息丰富的序列中解耦，既能带来计算收益，又能提升任务性能。

Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

</details>


### [126] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了增广拉格朗日方法和乐观原始-对偶方法在矩阵值校正下满足可加性原理：原始轨迹只取决于总校正矩阵，而非其在两个通道间的分配。基于此设计自由度，提出了混合校正规则，在非线性等式约束问题上优于纯增广和纯乐观方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法和乐观原始-对偶方法都能稳定等式约束优化，但机制不同（前者增加约束相关原始曲率，后者增加对偶记忆）。已有工作证明标量参数下两者等价，本文将其推广到矩阵值校正情形，并探索不同分解方式对可行性的影响。

Method: 1) 证明了矩阵参数下的可加性原理：对称矩阵参数下，理想原始轨迹仅依赖于总校正矩阵之和，与分配方式无关。2) 揭示了设计自由度：代数等价的分解因增广校正影响原始曲率、乐观校正影响对偶记忆尺度，导致不同有限步可行性。3) 构建了步长受限的设计问题，推导出闭式混合规则：选择矩阵校正、分解到两个通道、使用局部谱权重选择原始和对偶步长。

Result: 在非线性等式约束问题（控制约束雅可比条件数）上的实验表明：混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观端点，紧密追踪网格搜索的混合oracle，在轻中度病态条件下与一阶原始-对偶基线具有竞争力。同时识别了局限性：约束雅可比病态时，精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正。

Conclusion: 增广与乐观两种校正机制在矩阵值情形下满足可加性原理，据此可设计混合校正策略以兼顾两者优势。该方法在中度病态条件下表现良好，但在严重病态条件下受限于校正矩阵规模增长。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

</details>


### [127] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGamma，一种实用的深度演员-评论家方法，通过学习状态相关的折扣函数并结合回报一致性目标，实现了状态依赖折扣在深度强化学习中的稳定应用，在连续控制基准和京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中均取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习方法在所有状态使用单一固定折扣因子，而状态依赖折扣虽然概念上吸引人，但朴素深度演员-评论家实现会变得不稳定并退化为TD误差崩溃，因此需要一种实用的方法来使状态依赖折扣在深度RL中有效工作。

Method: AdaGamma方法包含：（1）学习一个状态依赖的折扣函数；（2）引入回报一致性目标（return-consistency objective）来正则化诱导的备份结构，防止目标操控退化；理论上分析了状态依赖折扣诱导的贝尔曼算子的适定性；实验上集成到SAC和PPO中。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中，集成到SAC和PPO均取得一致性改进；在京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中取得统计显著的收益提升。

Conclusion: 状态依赖折扣在深度RL中结合回报一致性目标可以有效发挥作用，该正则化目标防止了退化性的目标操控，使状态依赖折扣变得实用且有效。

Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform. These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

</details>


### [128] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM）框架，通过镜像下降熵最大化和混合行为先验机制，解决基于流的离线强化学习中流行度偏差和支持绑定问题，在稀疏奖励连续控制任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q学习伴随匹配（QAM）方法虽然稳定了策略优化，但受限于固定行为分布，导致流行度偏差（抑制低密度区域的高奖励动作）和支持绑定（限制流形外探索）。已有补救方案（如附加残差高斯策略）又会重新引入单峰分布的表示瓶颈。

Method: ME-AM框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 镜像下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以从离线数据中提取最优策略；(2) 混合行为先验，从数学上扩展几何支持以包含分布外高奖励区域，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励连续控制环境中，ME-AM相比现有SOTA方法表现出竞争性甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: ME-AM通过统一框架解决了离线流策略学习中行为分布依赖的根本问题，在保持生成模型表达能力的同时实现了有效的分布外探索。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [129] [One Algorithm, Two Goals: Dual Scoring for Parameter and Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06166)
*Xinrui Chen,Liu Yang,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DualSFT 算法，基于共享梯度统计信息一次性生成参数掩码和数据子集，实现联合约束下的高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM微调中，参数选择和数据选择通常采用独立的评分机制，导致冗余开销且难以协同优化。本文旨在将二者统一为双层级选择问题，实现联合高效选择。

Method: 将参数和数据选择建模为两个双层选择问题，在共同验证目标下推导出共享的局部响应替代评分规则。通过一阶和二阶验证改进近似，参数重要性和数据效用分别表现为单个梯度交互矩阵的列聚合与行聚合，形成闭式行列对应关系。基于此提出 DualSFT 算法，从共享梯度统计中一次性生成参数掩码和数据子集。

Result: 在3B-9B规模LLM上，单轴DualSFT变体在同组对比中增强了目标任务性能和稳定性-可塑性权衡；完整DualSFT在匹配预算下比顺序混合基线取得了更优的联合约束权衡。

Conclusion: 参数选择和_data选择可以通过共享梯度统计信息统一求解，DualSFT实现了比独立或顺序选择方法更高效的联合微调策略。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning, parameter and data selection are common strategies for reducing fine-tuning cost, yet they are typically driven by separate scoring mechanisms. When a parameter mask and data subset jointly determine restricted fine-tuning, this separation incurs redundant overhead and makes coordinated selection difficult. We cast parameter and data selection as two bilevel selection problems under a common validation objective and derive a shared local response-surrogate scoring rule. Under first- and second-order validation-improvement approximations, parameter importance and data utility emerge as column-wise and row-wise aggregations of a single gradient interaction matrix, yielding a closed-form row-column correspondence for co-extracting both signals. Building on this structure, we propose DualSFT (Dual-Selection Fine-Tuning), a one-shot dual-scoring algorithm that produces a parameter mask and data subset from shared gradient statistics. On 3B-9B LLMs, single-axis DualSFT variants strengthen target-task performance and stability-plasticity trade-offs within their comparison groups, while full DualSFT yields a more favorable joint-constrained trade-off than sequential hybrid baselines under matched budgets.

</details>


### [130] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈感知的上下文黑箱优化（FICBO），通过预训练Transformer同时利用历史观测和廉价辅助反馈来加速黑箱优化，在合成和真实任务上优于现有基准，并能自适应评估反馈源的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱优化方法要么仅处理单一反馈源，缺乏跨任务泛化能力；要么仅依赖优化历史，无法利用丰富的辅助信息（如专家建议、仿真器、预训练预测器等）。然而辅助信息可能存在偏差、输入依赖或误导性，亟需一种能灵活利用多种反馈源且具备鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出FICBO框架：1）设计结构化反馈先验，对反馈源的访问性、相关性和失真度进行建模；2）基于该先验预训练反馈感知的Transformer，使其在测试时能同时以历史观测和当前候选集的廉价辅助反馈为条件进行优化；3）模型通过将目标值与辅助信号对比，在上下文中在线估计各反馈源的可靠性，从而改进查询选择。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务上，FICBO能有效利用信息性反馈，同时对弱或误导性反馈保持鲁棒性，整体表现优于其他基线方法。实验还揭示了模型如何感知测试时的反馈源，展示了其可解释性和决策过程。

Conclusion: FICBO成功将多种辅助反馈信息融入上下文黑箱优化，实现了跨任务泛化与反馈源自适应评估，在提升优化效率的同时保持了对不可靠反馈的鲁棒性，为实际工程与科学中的黑箱优化提供了更灵活有效的方案。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [131] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束下的对抗性上下文老虎机问题，基于SquareCB框架提出一种利用在线回归或acles将约束问题简化为标准无约束上下文老虎机问题的模块化方法，在对抗性上下文下获得改进的保证及高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算约束老虎机工作大多聚焦于随机上下文场景，缺乏对更具挑战性的对抗性上下文（如广告投放中竞对策略动态变化）的有效方法，且已有算法分析复杂、不透明。

Method: 基于SquareCB框架，通过在线回归或acles自适应地定义替代奖励函数，将有预算约束的上下文老虎机问题转化为标准无约束上下文老虎机问题。

Result: 在对抗性上下文场景下，所提方法相比先前工作获得了更优的遗憾与预算违反约束的权衡保证，并给出了简洁高效算法。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架适用于对抗性上下文，分析紧凑透明，为预算约束在线学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [132] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放系统中多智能体在线学习问题，提出了统一的开系统多臂老虎机框架，引入预训练度、稳定性和全局动态遗憾等新概念，并开发了带有理论保证的全局UCB学习方法，证明了遗憾上界和下界的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开系统学习研究假设了实际中常被违反的结构性条件，缺乏适用于一般开系统的统一学习范式。

Method: 提出统一开系统老虎机问题形式化，引入预训练度、稳定性和全局动态遗憾等概念，设计全局UCB学习算法并给出理论保证。

Result: 遗憾上界显示进入不确定性通过预训练度线性进入遗憾；在稳定系统中，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需时间和智能体到达模式决定；通过下界证明这些依赖关系的紧致性。

Conclusion: 建立了开系统在线学习的通用理论框架，揭示了新智能体到达带来的核心挑战及其对遗憾的影响规律。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [133] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开创性地研究了对比学习场景下的极限辨识与极限生成问题，提出了对比闭包维数等概念，揭示了无噪声下的精确可辨识性条件，并证明了在有限对抗污染下对比方法相比正例学习具有根本性的鲁棒性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极限辨识与极限生成模型仅关注正例或全标注数据，但许多自然监督信号本质上是关系型的（如对比对）。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究从对比数据中在极限意义下进行辨识与生成的理论基础。

Method: 定义对比展示（无序对流），引入公共交叉图作为统一技术工具，提出对比闭包维数表征均匀对比生成，给出对比可辨识类的精确几何刻画（对Angluin条件的细化），并在有限对抗污染下分析鲁棒性。

Result: (1) 给出对比可辨识类的充要条件（Angluin条件的单行几何精细化）；(2) 对比闭包维数精确刻画均匀对比生成及其样本复杂度；(3) 对比生成与正例辨识互不包含的严格层级关系；(4) 有限对抗污染下对比辨识具有根本性优势，存在可被对比辨识但正例辨识下连一次污染都不可容忍的类别。

Conclusion: 对比学习在极限模型中具有独特的表达能力和鲁棒性优势，公共交叉图是刻画对比信息结构的统一框架，为理解关系型监督信号的理论能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [134] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆注入（Memory Inception, MI），一种无需训练的大模型操控方法，通过在选定的注意力层插入文本生成的KV对，在潜在注意力空间中进行选择性引导，在个性操控、可更新引导和结构化推理任务上均取得领先效果，同时将内容匹配的KV存储压缩最高达118倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型操控方法存在权衡困境：指令提示（prompting）控制力强但会在每层缓存引导token，导致长交互场景臃肿；激活操控（activation steering）虽紧凑但通常控制力较弱，且不支持大规模结构化提示。亟需一种兼具强控制力与低存储开销的操控方法。

Method: 记忆注入（MI）是一种无需训练的方法，在潜在注意力空间中通过仅在选定层插入文本衍生的KV对（key-value banks）来实现选择性引导。与将提示内容物化到整个提示缓存不同，MI将操控视为选择性KV分配，仅在模型路由到的位置注入潜在槽位。

Result: 在匹配的个性操控任务中，MI在控制-漂移权衡上取得最佳整体表现，与prompting竞争力相当且持续优于CAA。在可更新引导任务中，MI支持对话中途行为切换而无需重写可见对话记录，在Qwen3上取得最高的切换后对齐度。在结构化推理任务中，MI在HARDMath和PHYSICS上超越可见prompting（10/12个subject×mode单元格），并将内容匹配的KV存储压缩最高达118倍。

Conclusion: MI定位为一种强大的操控方法，特别适用于引导具有持久性、结构化或在可见对话中存储成本高昂的场景。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [135] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯平滑的软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG）方法，通过平滑贝尔曼方程定义新动作价值函数，消除对评论家动作梯度的依赖，解决非光滑Q函数下策略梯度不定义的问题，并将其实例化为Soft DDPG算法。在标准连续控制任务及其离散奖励变体上，Soft DDPG在密集奖励下保持竞争力，在离散奖励环境下显著优于标准DDPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性策略梯度（DPG）依赖评论家对动作的可微性，但在稀疏或离散奖励等实际控制问题中，该假设被违反，导致策略梯度定义不良且学习不稳定。

Method: 通过高斯平滑定义平滑贝尔曼方程，构造新的动作价值函数，导出软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），消除对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，确保梯度在非光滑Q函数下仍有良好定义。

Result: 在密集奖励环境下与标准DDPG竞争力相当；在大多数离散奖励变体环境中，Soft DDPG相比标准DDPG取得明显性能提升，后者对不规则的评论家景观更为敏感。

Conclusion: Soft-DPG框架有效解决了DPG在非光滑环境中的梯度失效问题，为连续控制中非平滑奖励场景提供了更稳定的替代方案。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [136] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态推荐系统在逃避式推广攻击中存在跨模态梯度不匹配问题，并提出UAT-MC方法，通过梯度对齐机制协调多模态扰动，显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统易受逃避式推广攻击，现有防御主要针对单一模态和投毒式攻击，对逃避式威胁研究不足，且多用户推广场景下跨模态梯度不匹配会削弱攻击有效性并导致鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出UAT-MC，将全部项目视为潜在目标以应对逃避式攻击中目标未知的挑战，并引入梯度对齐机制显式校正跨模态梯度不匹配，实现各模态扰动同步最大化对抗强度进行鲁棒训练。

Result: 实验表明UAT-MC在防御-准确率权衡下显著提升对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统在逃避式推广攻击下的脆弱性问题，通过跨模态协调实现了鲁棒性与推荐性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [137] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层的校准诊断协议，证明图语言模型在大型规模下能够学习结构规律性而不仅仅是记忆训练图，主要在高频区域表现出结构对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚合保真度指标无法区分图语言模型是学习了结构规律性还是仅仅记忆了训练图，因此需要一种能够解耦记忆与结构对齐的诊断方法。

Method: 提出一种校准诊断协议，结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层；在5个TU基准上评估LLaMA风格图语言模型，使用规范DFS码和动作序列两种图序列化方式，在从3.75M图的小规模到大规模上进行实验。

Result: 大规模下逐字记忆急剧下降而秩相关接近上限；高频模式被良好复现，稀有模式覆盖不足且随容量增加改善有限；两种序列化下观察到相同的尺度依赖交叉现象；固定子样本分析中仅限新颖子集的频繁子图挖掘与全生成Spearman相关性紧密跟踪。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在足够大规模下能够获取超越记忆的结构规律性，但主要局限于高频区域；当前模型对稀有模式的泛化能力仍然有限，这为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [138] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Forward-Forward (FF)训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象：后期层会依赖前期层已完成的类别分离，导致自身判别梯度呈指数衰减。作者提出了三种局部修复方法（逐块、硬度门控、深度缩放），显著改善了深层分离统计量（提升4-45倍），但对最终准确率影响甚微（<1%）。实验表明，架构和数据增强等选择对准确率的影响远超训练规则修改。因此，层搭便车虽是真实存在的优化病理，但并非限制FF训练精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Forward-Forward训练的累积好客度变体中，后期层会继承前期层已部分分离的任务，导致自身学习退化。作者将此形式化为“层搭便车”现象，并旨在诊断和修复这一问题。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明在softplus FF准则下，达到第d块的类别判别梯度随前期块累积的正间隔呈指数衰减。2. 提出三种局部修复方法：逐块（per-block）、硬度门控（hardness-gated）、深度缩放（depth-scaled），均不依赖反向传播梯度。3. 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny ImageNet上进行实验验证。

Result: 三种修复方法在深层实现了4-45倍的层分离统计量提升，但非退化训练程序下准确率变化不足1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet上展示了相同定性模式。校准实验显示架构和数据增强对最终准确率的影响远大于训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是真实且可修复的优化病理，但在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集下，它不是限制可达精度的主导因素。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [139] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图池化在图分类中效果不稳定的原因，发现池化操作需要节点特征与图拓扑良好对齐，并提出了特征质量的量化指标。实验证明，当满足这一条件时，池化能够提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化在图分类任务中相对于标准WL-1表达力的GNN带来的经验提升往往微弱或不一致，这一现象背后的原因尚不明确，亟需理论分析来解释该差距。

Method: 通过分析节点特征与图拓扑之间的交互作用及其对池化目标的影响，形式化定义了节点特征实现有效池化的基本要求，并提出了一种特征质量的量化度量方法。

Result: 实验验证了当节点特征满足与图拓扑对齐的条件时，池化能够带来性能提升，在合适的数据集上表现更优。

Conclusion: 池化操作的有效性依赖于节点特征与图拓扑的对齐程度，这一条件在实践中常被忽视且无法保证；满足该条件时池化是有益的。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图联邦学习中异质性挑战，提出FedGMC框架，通过双流形校准机制从统一流形视角处理语义和结构异质性，在保持全局共性的同时最大化局部个性化表征空间，在11个图上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐模型参数或原型来处理异质性，但隐含地依赖全局线性假设，用单一全局一致的表示空间来概括局部数据分布，严重压缩了客户端的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样的局部图分布。

Method: 提出FedGMC框架，引入双流形校准机制：(1) 语义异质性方面，服务器通过等距语义锚构建几何最优的语义流形，指导局部语义流形的校准；(2) 结构异质性方面，服务器通过构建全局结构模板构建全局结构流形，指导局部结构流形的校准；(3) 服务器通过聚合局部流形动态优化全局语义流形和结构流形。

Result: 在11个同配和异配图上的大量实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性和局部个性化，显著优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC通过统一流形视角处理语义和结构异质性，无需强制对齐，为图联邦学习中的异质性问题提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [141] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TARDIS，一种在推理阶段对预训练扩散模型进行微调的框架，通过双向Chamfer精炼（BCR）模式，在15个基准测试上实现中位数+8.6%的下游任务提升，达到甚至超过真实数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型生成的表格数据在效用上仍难以超越真实数据，此前改进仅局限于训练阶段（架构、缩放、重训练），推理阶段精炼预训练模型这一方向尚未被探索。

Method: TARDIS框架：基于冻结的预训练扩散骨干，通过树结构Parzen估计器（TPE）搜索逆扩散过程中的分数级引导；内部网格搜索后验样本选择器和软标签蒸馏步骤。核心模式为双向Chamfer精炼（BCR），在连续域通过分数级梯度、离散域通过批量排序，最小化合成样本与真实样本之间的对称Chamfer泛函。

Result: 在15个二分类、多分类和回归基准上，TARDIS相比真实数据训练模型提升中位数+8.6%（95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], p=0.016, 11/15胜出）；相比TabDiff骨干在所有15个数据集上提升（平均+12.9%, p<10^-4）；在流形保真度、多样性和样本级隐私方面与骨干持平；单消费级GPU上1-80分钟完成。

Conclusion: 推理时精炼预训练表格扩散骨干可以在不重新训练的情况下达到并超越真实数据效用，BCR被确认为主导的精炼模式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [142] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向端到端自动驾驶规划的分层归因框架，通过从粗到细的区域归因策略定位关键视觉证据，并提取三种归因统计量（熵、空间方差、基尼系数）作为规划风险的预测信号，实验表明这些信号与轨迹误差和碰撞检测具有显著相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶模型缺乏可解释性，辅助监控或文本解释方法脱离规划过程，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据；归因方法在规划任务中面临多视图输入和连续多步轨迹的挑战，且归因图能否支持风险识别尚未被探索。

Method: 以原始轨迹的L2一致性为目标，设计从粗到细的区域归因策略；在全六视图输入中搜索候选区域并精细化归因；提取三种归因统计量作为风险预测信号：归因熵、帧内空间方差、跨相机基尼系数。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD、GenAD三个模型上，归因统计量与轨迹误差的Spearman相关系数为0.30±0.07，碰撞检测AUROC达0.77±0.04，且泛化到未见过场景时性能几乎无下降，在替代归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 分层归因框架能有效揭示端到端规划中的视觉证据，且归因统计量可作为可靠的规划风险预测信号，具备良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [143] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出函数投影流匹配（FP-FM），通过学习速度场的基函数并对新分布进行最小二乘投影，实现无需额外训练即可从样本直接生成新分布数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（如自然语言条件图像生成）虽取得巨大成功，但从示例数据点直接进行模型适配的问题仍未被充分探索且具有挑战性。

Method: FP-FM学习一组基函数来张成对应训练分布的速度场，对于新分布，仅需计算简单的基于最小二乘的投影到该基函数上即可适配。同时提出多种变体（如系数依赖于时间）以在表达能力和计算量之间进行权衡。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上，FP-FM相比基线方法在精确率和召回率上有显著提升，尤其在未见过的分布上优势更为明显。

Conclusion: FP-FM能够高效地从多样化目标分布生成样本，且在推理时无需额外训练，具有较好的泛化能力。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [144] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HACE是一种替代标准交叉熵的分类损失函数，通过将类别层次结构直接融入损失中，在多种架构和数据集上显著提升了分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失平等对待所有错误分类，忽略了类别层次结构中蕴含的语义距离信息，导致模型无法利用类别间的语义关系。

Method: HACE包含两个核心组件：1) 预测聚合——将模型概率质量沿类别层次结构向上传播，使父节点累积子节点的置信度；2) 祖先标签平滑——将真实标签信号沿从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分布。

Result: 在端到端训练中，HACE在18个架构-数据集组合中的15个上优于标准交叉熵，平均提升4.66%；在冻结DINOv2-Large特征的线性探针实验中，HACE在所有三个数据集上优于所有对比方法，平均比最佳基线高出2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为标准交叉熵的即插即用替代方案，能有效利用类别层次结构信息，在不同架构（卷积和注意力机制）和数据集上均取得一致且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [145] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一个两阶段框架，先通过理论指导主动生成新学习器增强集成多样性，再对丰富后的集成进行剪枝，在忠实度可控的前提下优于现有剪枝和压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽性能优异，但大量弱学习器导致部署困难、可解释性差及鲁棒性验证等问题，现有剪枝和压缩方法各有局限。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过理论指导主动生成新学习器增强多样性，第二阶段对丰富后的集成进行剪枝；两个阶段均可精细控制对原始集成的忠实度。

Result: 实验表明PACE在性能上优于现有剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供原理性的忠实度保证。

Conclusion: PACE通过交错剪枝与压缩范式，实现了对集成模型的高效精简，兼顾性能与可控忠实度。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [146] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INEUS是一种针对偏积分微分方程（PIDE）的网格自由迭代神经求解器，通过单跳采样替代显式非局部跳跃积分计算，将PIDE求解转化为递归回归问题，在高维线性和非线性问题上实现了准确且可扩展的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统方法（包括PINNs）在处理PIDE中的非局部项时计算成本高、难以扩展到高维空间的问题。

Method: 采用网格自由框架，用单跳采样替代显式非局部跳跃积分计算，将PIDE求解重构为序列递归回归问题，避免了对完整PIDE残差的计算微分。

Result: 在线性PIDE上通过基于收缩的收敛性证明提供了理论保障，数值实验表明在高维线性和非线性问题上均能提供准确且可扩展的解。

Conclusion: INEUS在保持PINNs全局求解优势的同时，更高效地处理非局部项，特别适用于高维PDE和PIDE问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [147] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"元博弈"（metagame）概念框架，通过将归因方法本身视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，量化模型解释的二阶交互效应（即特征j对特征i归因的定向影响），理论上证明了归因可分层分解为元归因，实验上在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器跨模态相似性及多模态扩散Transformer文本到图像概念等场景验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法主要关注一阶特征重要性归因，但忽略了特征之间如何相互影响归因结果的二阶交互效应，即一个特征的存在或改变如何定向地影响另一个特征被归因的重要性。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出能够量化这种"归因的归因"的框架。

Method: 将归因方法本身视为一个合作博弈，通过计算该博弈的Shapley值来定义元归因（meta-attribution）$\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$，度量特征j对特征i归因值的定向影响。该方法将一阶归因分解为二阶元归因的层次结构，并证明这些元归因是现有交互指标的定向扩展。

Result: 在三大应用场景中验证了有效性：（1）指令微调语言模型中的token交互量化；（2）视觉-语言编码器中的跨模态相似性解释；（3）多模态扩散Transformer中文本到图像概念的解释。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架为理解模型解释中的二阶交互效应提供了理论统一视角和实践工具，能够揭示一阶归因无法捕捉的特征间定向影响关系，拓宽了可解释性研究的边界。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices.
