GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [186] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign提出了一种通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑音频分词化的框架，将分词视为条件序列生成，通过内容保持视图间的交叉预测学习紧凑、非退化的符号序列，在TIMIT检索任务中以55%的令牌压缩率保留了编辑距离搜索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频分词器依赖量化、聚类或编解码重建，以局部方式分配令牌，导致序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止符学习和编辑相似性等全局性质很少被直接优化。

Method: PairAlign将分词建模为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射为连续条件，自回归解码器从起始符生成令牌，学习令牌身份、顺序、长度和终止符位置。给定两个内容保持视图，每个视图的序列在对方表示下具有高似然，无关样本提供竞争序列，从而可扩展地近似编辑距离保持并防止多对一坍缩。初始化为VQ风格分词，通过EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀破坏、似然对比和长度控制进行精炼。

Result: 在3秒语音上学到紧凑、非退化序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视图一致性强。在TIMIT检索中，以55%的存档令牌数减少保留了编辑距离搜索性能。连续扫描探测显示比密集几何分词器更低的局部重叠，但更强的长度控制和在100ms偏移下有界的编辑轨迹。

Conclusion: PairAlign是一种序列符号预测学习器：类似JEPA风格目标，它从一个视图预测另一个视图的抽象目标，但输出的是可学习的变长符号序列而非连续潜变量。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [187] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分布鲁棒优化的超参数学习方法，通过学习凸优化一阶算法的步长等参数，在经典学习优化(L2O)和最坏情况最优算法设计(PEP)之间建立统一框架，并证明所学算法的真实风险有理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习优化(L2O)方法在训练分布上表现良好，但缺乏对分布偏移的鲁棒性保证；而最坏情况最优算法设计(PEP)虽提供鲁棒性保证，但过于保守。本文旨在填补两者之间的空白，实现既具鲁棒性又具有良好平均性能的算法参数学习。

Method: 1) 构建Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化框架，最小化算法参数(如步长)上的分布鲁棒性能估计问题；2) 通过随机梯度下降求解，每一步对内层半定规划进行微分；3) 在无约束二次优化、LASSO和线性规划等基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 建立了理论保证：真实风险不超过样本内L2O最优加上随样本量缩小的松弛项，且不差于最坏情况PEP界；2) 在多个基准上，学习算法在分布外数据上表现出强泛化性能与可认证鲁棒性；3) 同时优于最坏情况最优算法和普通L2O基线。

Conclusion: 所提分布鲁棒方法有效统一了L2O和PEP两种范式，在算法参数学习中实现了平均性能与鲁棒性的平衡，具有理论保障和实证优势。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [188] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次严格证明了带权重衰减的Transformer目标函数满足Villani coercive能量函数准则，推导出对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并通过GPT-Neo-125M实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减广泛用于大语言模型，但其对Transformer损失景观的理论作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 运用泛函分析证明正则化损失满足Villani准则（无限可微、二次增长、高斯可积尾部、微分增长条件），推导显式对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，引入Villani诊断量Ψ_s并通过Hutchinson迹估计在1亿参数模型上验证。

Result: 实验证实了二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀以及指数收敛行为，与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅提升泛化能力，还能为快速Langevin混合和曲率感知优化提供数学基础。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [189] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPRO，首个理论有效的动态预算分配框架，用于在多轮LLM交互中为事件发生时间提供置信下界，相比静态基线方法具有更紧的覆盖保证和更低的方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话场景中评估LLM性能（如越狱、任务完成等关键事件）计算成本高昂，且现有共形生存分析方法采用静态预算分配，效率低下。

Method: 提出DAPRO（动态分配与投影优化），一种动态预算分配框架；理论贡献包括：满足预算约束、无分布假设的有限样本覆盖保证、基于平均删失权重平方根而非最坏情况权重的更紧覆盖界。

Result: 在Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5上的实验涵盖智能体任务成功、对抗越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉，DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，始终达到更接近名义水平的覆盖率和更低的方差。

Conclusion: DAPRO为多轮LLM交互中的时间-事件预测提供了首个动态预算分配框架，理论上更优，实验上有效，并可推广到群体级评估指标的估计。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [190] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带softmax注意力的Transformer如何在线性分类数据上执行上下文学习（ICL）。作者构建了一类多层Transformer，其每一层精确执行一步对上下文损失的归一化梯度下降，从而实现上下文逻辑回归。通过单层自注意力训练（由一步梯度下降监督）并循环应用该层得到循环模型。提供了自注意力层的训练收敛保证和循环模型的分布外泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer如何在上下文中隐式执行算法以增强预测和生成能力，特别是softmax注意力机制在ICL中的工作机制。

Method: (i) 构建多层Transformer，每层执行一步归一化梯度下降；(ii) 训练单层自注意力层（由一步梯度下降监督）；(iii) 循环应用训练好的层得到循环模型。

Result: 提供了自注意力层的训练收敛保证和循环模型的分布外泛化保证。

Conclusion: 推进了ICL机制的理论理解，展示了softmax Transformer如何有效充当上下文学习器。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [191] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE提出了一种动态Top-K稀疏自编码器，通过可微分的Soft Top-K算子学习输入相关的稀疏度，从而根据数据复杂度自适应调整活跃特征数量，实验证明该方法能选择合适的特征数量且保留有意义的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TopK SAE使用固定的稀疏度K，忽略了真实世界数据在不同样本间局部内在维度的变化——简单输入只需要少量特征而复杂输入需要更多特征，固定K会导致简单输入引入噪声或复杂输入丢失重要结构。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，采用可微分的Soft Top-K算子实现动态Top-K选择机制，让模型学习与输入相关的稀疏度k，从而根据每个输入的复杂度自适应调整活跃特征数量。

Result: 实验结果表明SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征。

Conclusion: 动态调整稀疏度能更好地匹配数据的内在结构，使解释长度反映输入的信息量，优于固定稀疏度的传统SAE方法。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [192] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面解释了符号优化算法（SignSGD和Muon）为何以及何时优于传统SGD。作者通过引入ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞-光滑性和可分离噪声模型，证明了在稀疏噪声条件下SignSGD能将复杂度降低d倍（d为维度），并将该框架扩展至矩阵域为Muon优化器提供了最优下界，最后在124M参数GPT-2预训练中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号优化算法（如SignSGD和Muon）在训练大型基础模型中表现出色，但缺乏理论解释。现有理论表明SGD在标准设置下已达到极小最优，因此需要新的理论框架来理解符号方法的优势。

Method: 采用ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞-光滑性和可分离噪声模型来捕捉符号更新的坐标级特性；推导SignSGD的匹配上下界；与SGD的下界进行对比；将框架扩展至矩阵域分析Muon优化器。

Result: 在稀疏噪声条件下，SignSGD相比SGD可将复杂度降低d倍；Muon优化器在矩阵域保持了这种最优的维度缩放特性；理论预测在124M参数GPT-2预训练中与实际收敛加速一致。

Conclusion: 通过提出新的问题几何框架，首次从理论上证明了符号优化算法在特定条件下（稀疏噪声）可以超越SGD，并将理论结果与大规模实践联系起来。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [193] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念级溯因和对比解释，通过概念擦除技术建立高层概念与模型预测间的因果联系，并枚举所有最小解释集合，支持单张图像及图像集合层面的模型行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的解释方法要么缺乏因果联系，要么表达能力有限（仅能处理单一概念）；而形式化溯因/对比解释虽计算最小因果特征集，但仅关注像素级低级特征。两类工作未能融合，缺乏既能处理高层概念又能建立因果关系的解释方法。

Method: 提出概念级溯因和对比解释（concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations），定义覆盖高层概念的最小因果集；设计系列算法，利用概念擦除（concept erasure）建立因果关系，枚举所有最小解释；通过聚合解释支持单图像和图像集合层面的模型行为分析。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上验证，所提方法能够生成有效、用户友好的解释。

Conclusion: 融合概念解释与形式化溯因/对比解释两条研究路线，实现了兼具高层语义表达力和因果严谨性的模型解释，并支持从个体预测到群体行为的多层次分析。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [194] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二元分类和有界回归推广到无界回归场景，通过引入共形预测元素，并在仿真和实证研究中证明基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在大训练集上能略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器在二元分类中具有良好的有效性属性，但无法直接应用于无界回归问题，限制了其适用范围。本文旨在填补这一空白，将其推广到更一般的无界回归场景。

Method: 通过引入共形预测（conformal prediction）元素，将Venn-Abers框架从有界回归扩展到无界回归场景，并基于此构建点回归器。

Result: 仿真和实证研究表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在训练集规模较大时，能够略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。

Conclusion: Venn-Abers预测器可成功推广到无界回归，且导出的点回归器在大样本条件下对预测效率有温和但正向的改进作用。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [195] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在缺乏标注基准的情况下对语言模型进行安全性比较评分的框架（benchmarkless comparative safety scoring），并开发了SimpleAudit工具。通过构建工具效度链（instrumental-validity chain）替代传统基于ground-truth的一致性验证，在挪威语安全包上验证了其区分效力（AUROC 0.89-1.00），并在挪威公共部门采购案例中展示了实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多部署场景需要在目标语言、行业或监管框架相关的标注基准尚未存在的情况下，比较候选语言模型的安全性。本文旨在形式化这一无基准比较安全评分的设置，并明确基于场景的审计可作为部署证据的契约条件。

Method: 通过工具效度链（instrumental-validity chain）替代ground-truth一致性，包含三个环节：(1) 对受控的安全vs消融（abliterated）对比的响应性；(2) 目标驱动方差主导审计师和评判者人工痕迹；(3) 跨重跑的稳定性。实例化为SimpleAudit工具，在挪威语安全包上通过AUROC、方差成分分析（η²）和稳定性分析进行验证。

Result: 安全与消融目标的分离AUROC值为0.89-1.00；目标身份是主导方差成分（η²≈0.52）；严重性剖面在十次重跑后稳定。在Petri工具上同样适用。挪威公共部门采购案例显示，更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 评分、匹配差值、临界比率、不确定性以及所使用的审计师和评判者必须一起报告，而不可合并为单一排名。分数仅在固定的场景包、评分标准、审计师、评判者、采样配置和重跑预算下有效。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [196] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出优化器-模型一致性现象：在SFT阶段使用与预训练相同的优化器，相比其他优化器乃至LoRA，能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡。通过实验和理论分析，发现优化器通过对激活的正则化效应塑造模型，而SFT中的权重更新需遵循特定结构以降低遗忘，这可通过使用相同优化器实现。此外，Muon优化器在推理任务微调中表现更差，因其倾向于死记硬背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在SFT阶段使用与预训练相同优化器对学习-遗忘权衡的影响，并理解其背后的机制。

Method: 通过控制实验和理论分析，研究优化器对模型激活的正则化效应；对比Muon与AdamW在预训练和SFT全流程中的表现；设计合成语言建模实验分析优化器行为。

Result: 1) 优化器-模型一致性现象存在，相同优化器在SFT中遗忘更少、性能相当或更好；2) 优化器通过正则化激活塑造模型损失景观；3) Muon在推理任务微调中表现差，因其倾向于死记硬背而非模式习得。

Conclusion: SFT阶段应保持与预训练一致的优化器以实现更好的知识保留；Muon优化器不适合小样本微调场景。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [197] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VHG框架，通过将独立验证器引入传统的出题者-解题者二元体系，构成三方自博弈，有效生成有效、有挑战性且新颖的问题，在积分和数学推理任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题生成方法要么依赖昂贵的人类专家，要么采用朴素自博弈范式，常因奖励破解而产生无效问题。需要一种能生成有效、有挑战性且新颖问题的方法，以推动LLM训练和自主科学研究。

Method: 提出VHG框架，基于三方自博弈（出题者-验证器-解题者）。引入独立验证器约束出题者的奖励，奖励由问题有效性（验证器评估）和难度（解题者评估）共同决定。实例化了两种验证器变体：硬符号验证器和基于软LLM的验证器。

Result: 在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上的实验结果表明，VHG在所有基线方法中以明显优势胜出。

Conclusion: 通过引入独立验证器构成三方自博弈，有效解决了自博弈中的奖励破解问题，能生成有效、有挑战性且新颖的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [198] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UniPool，一种将MoE专家容量从逐层分配改为全局共享池的架构。通过池级辅助损失和NormRouter实现稳定训练。在多个模型规模上，UniPool持续优于传统MoE，且专家参数可随深度次线性增长，仅用41.6%-66.7%的专家参数即可匹敌或超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构中每层独立拥有专家集，导致深度扩展时专家参数线性增长。然而研究发现深层路由器存在冗余（随机路由仅造成1.0-1.6个点的精度下降），因此质疑每层都需要独立专家容量的必要性。

Method: 提出UniPool架构：(1) 用单一共享专家池替代每层独立专家集，各层独立路由器从同一池中选择专家；(2) 引入池级辅助损失以平衡全局专家利用率；(3) 采用NormRouter实现稀疏且尺度稳定的路由。

Result: 在5个LLaMA架构规模（182M-978M参数）上训练30B tokens，UniPool持续降低验证损失（最高降低0.0386）。缩减池变体仅用41.6%-66.7%的专家参数即可匹敌或超越传统MoE。

Conclusion: 共享池设计下专家参数无需随深度线性增长，可次线性增长且更高效。池大小成为显式的深度缩放超参数。UniPool的优势可进一步与细粒度专家分解相结合。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Auto Research的闭环实证研究框架，通过外部测量驱动假设-代码编辑-结果反馈的迭代循环，实现全自动的科研实验。在1197次主运行和600次对照实验中，无需人类干预，系统在三个经典基准任务上均取得了显著改进：参数高尔夫验证集bpb降低0.81%、NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%、CIFAR-10 Airbench96墙钟时间减少4.59%。系统可自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、应用已知技术，并生成完整的可审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动机器学习研究要么生成论文或模型检查点，缺乏可审计的实验轨迹；要么需要大量人工干预（选择提案、编辑代码、修复失败实验）。本文旨在构建一个完全自主的闭环实证循环，让外部评估结果直接驱动后续代码改进，消除人类在实验迭代过程中的参与。

Method: 设计封闭实证循环：每个提交的试验包含假设、可执行代码编辑、评估者拥有的结果及反馈。使用专业智能体分区处理配方表面，并通过谱系反馈跨试验共享测量信息。谱系反馈使智能体将评估结果（崩溃、超预算、尺寸失败、精度未达标等）转化为程序级配方编辑而非一次性建议。

Result: 在一次性设置启动后的1197次主运行和600次参数高尔夫对照实验中，人类未参与任何提案选择、配方编辑、分数覆盖或失败实验修复。三个主运行任务结果：参数高尔夫验证集bpb降低0.81%，NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%，CIFAR-10 Airbench96墙钟时间减少4.59%。包含157次主运行提交的严格架构域审计，以及如NanoChat注意力核路径更改等程序重写。

Conclusion: 该循环可在各环境内自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、应用并组合已知技术，改进公开的起始配方。系统的可审计轨迹（提案、代码差异、实验、分数、失败标签）为自动化科学研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [200] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体随机最短路径（MSSP）问题，其中k个智能体协作以最小化任一智能体到达目标状态的期望时间，分析了自主与协调两种设置下的计算复杂性和策略复杂性，并设计了高效策略合成算法，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单智能体或完全协作的多智能体路径规划，本文考虑多个智能体在随机环境中以“最先到达”为目标（而非全体到达）的最短路径问题，填补了该场景下的理论与算法空白。

Method: 首先形式化定义了MSSP问题，将其建模为多智能体马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。分别在自主设置（各智能体独立决策）和协调设置（存在中心化协调）下分析问题的计算复杂性与策略复杂度。针对不同设置设计了高效策略合成算法，并在不同规模实例上与自然基线方法进行实验对比。

Result: 在两种设置下均给出了复杂度下界与上界分析；所提算法在合成策略时相比基线方法显著降低了通信或计算开销，且在大规模实例上仍保持可扩展性。

Conclusion: MSSP问题在自主与协调设置下具有不同的策略复杂度与计算特性；所提出的策略合成算法在实际应用中优于基线方法，为多智能体随机环境下的快速到达任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于凸优化的评估框架，用于分析由磁场相互作用维持的卫星群孔径。通过卫星搭载的磁力矩器产生的电磁力实现无推进剂的位置控制，并解决电磁力与力矩模型非线性导致的非凸功耗约束问题。分析表明，增加卫星数量可提高编队保持的功率效率，为大规模空间系统构建提供了一种功率高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星群分布式孔径具有可扩展、高增益、窄波束和大孔径等优势，但长期维持虚拟结构需要稳定的编队控制。磁相互作用提供了一种无推进剂的位控策略，然而电磁力与力矩模型的非线性导致功耗约束非凸，使得系统级配置分析困难。

Method: 开发了一种基于凸优化的框架来分析大型磁驱动卫星群的功率消耗，将非凸的功耗约束转化为可处理的形式进行系统级分析。

Result: 增加卫星数量可以改善编队保持的功率效率。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种比传统少量卫星电磁编队飞行更功率高效的替代方案。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [202] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LATTE 是一种受分布式系统启发的 LLM 团队协调框架，通过共享动态协调图管理子任务依赖、分配和进度，在减少 token 消耗、时间和通信开销的同时，匹配或超越 MetaGPT、去中心化团队等现有方法的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 团队协调方法处于两个极端：高度结构化方法（固定角色/流水线/任务分解）缺乏灵活性，而完全非结构化团队虽具适应性但存在错误传播、智能体间冲突和资源浪费（时间、token、文件操作）等效率问题。

Method: LATTE 框架受分布式系统启发，多个智能体共同构建和维护一个共享的演化协调图，编码子任务依赖关系、智能体分配和子任务进度状态。该协议在保持一致性的同时，使智能体能够动态分配工作、适应协调并发现新任务。

Result: 在多个协作任务和基础模型上，LATTE 减少了 token 使用量、墙钟时间、通信量和协调失败（如文件冲突和冗余输出），同时匹配或超越了 MetaGPT、去中心化团队、自上而下 Leader-Worker 层级和静态分解等标准设计的准确性。

Conclusion: 共享演化的协调图能够有效平衡结构性与灵活性，为 LLM 团队协作提供了一种高效、低开销且可扩展的协调协议。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [203] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协调感知的评估视角，通过STAT测试平台（受约束的空间任务分配）分析多智能体强化学习方法的协调机制，发现相似的回报趋势可能掩盖不同的协调行为差异，建议将协调感知评估作为回报基准的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL基准测试主要关注回报、成功率等聚合指标，但这些指标无法揭示智能体如何协调，特别是在智能体、任务和联合分配选择组合爆炸的场景下。

Method: 设计STAT（受约束承诺的空间任务分配）测试平台，系统变化智能体数量、任务数量和环境规模，固定观测访问和任务规则；评估六种基于值函数的MARL方法（不同集中化程度），从冗余分配、分配多样性、任务完成效率等过程指标进行分析。

Result: 相似的回报趋势可能对应不同的协调机制；在受约束承诺的任务分配中，规模化性能不仅受名义动作空间大小影响，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会以及相互依赖智能体间的冗余选择影响。

Conclusion: 协调感知评估应当作为基于回报的MARL基准测试的必要补充。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Toward Space-Based Public Key Systems: Enabling Secure Space Communications through In-Orbit Trust Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05948)
*Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对New Space时代卫星数量激增带来的身份认证需求，提出了两种基于空间的公钥基础设施（PKI）架构方案：天地一体化PKI（空间验证）和完全自主的空间PKI（空间签发与验证），以减少对地面设施的依赖，提升多运营环境下的可扩展性、可用性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖地面的公钥基础设施在动态空间环境中存在延迟高、操作瓶颈大、可扩展性差等问题，无法满足近地轨道卫星群实时、安全的身份认证与协调需求。

Method: 提出了两种部署方案：1）天地一体化PKI，在轨设置验证权威节点，地面保留证书签发功能；2）完全自主空间PKI，证书的签发与验证均在空间完成。从可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本和操作复杂度五个维度进行折中分析，并进行了基线延迟分析。

Result: 两种方案在不同维度各有优劣：天地一体化方案在安全性和成本控制上更优，完全自主方案在可用性和自主性上更强。延迟分析表明，在轨信任管理可显著降低认证延迟。

Conclusion: 空间化PKI是解决New Space时代多运营实体间安全协调的关键技术路径，两种方案可根据任务需求灵活选择或组合部署。

Abstract: The New Space era has led to a rapid increase in satellites operated by independent entities in near-Earth orbit. This shift enables richer space services but also requires secure, near-real-time coordination, making efficient authentication of space assets critical for next-generation missions. Traditional ground-dependent Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) suffers from latency and operational bottlenecks that limit scalability and availability in dynamic space environments. This paper proposes architectural designs for space-based PKI that shift certificate management and validation from ground infrastructure into space, reducing reliance on ground stations while enabling interoperability and cross-entity collaboration. Two deployment schemes are introduced: a space-ground integrated PKI with in-orbit validation authorities, and a fully autonomous space-based PKI with in-space issuance and validation. We analyze deployment trade-offs in scalability, availability, security, cost, and operational complexity in multi-operator environments. A baseline latency analysis is provided to illustrate performance implications of in-orbit trust management.

</details>


### [205] [Identifier-Free Code Embedding Models for Scalable Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05251)
*Eric Wolos,Michael Doyle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，在二进制逆向工程中实现了源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向函数关联，显著超越现有基线，并泛化至未见过的常量算法关联任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数关联工具未充分利用AI搜索能力，且前人工作未覆盖源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联（需满足标准预处理要求），存在研究空白。

Method: 形式化定义函数关联问题；采用对比学习（contrastive learning）微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，以提升嵌入模型在源代码与反编译剥离代码间双向关联的性能。

Result: 微调后的模型在所有函数关联基线上以显著优势超越其他模型，并能泛化至未显式训练的常量算法关联任务。

Conclusion: 对比学习微调的嵌入模型能有效弥补源代码与反编译剥离代码间的双向关联鸿沟，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Function association is a useful process for binary reverse engineers. Search tools exist to perform association at scale, but they do not utilize the full range of capabilities that AI-enabled search provides. Prior work has explored the development of embedding models for association between certain reverse engineering code representations, but that work does not cover bidirectional association between source code and decompiled, stripped code with standard preprocessing requirements. To bridge this gap, we formalize this function association problem and evaluate the extent to which embedding models can bidirectionally associate between these two representations. To improve model performance at this task, we fine-tune a Qwen3-Embedding model with contrastive learning. We find that our new model outperforms other models on all function association baselines by a substantial margin and generalizes to a constant-algorithm association task it is not explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [206] [SecureMCP: A Policy-Enforced LLM Data Access Framework for AIoT Systems via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05260)
*Wonbae Kim,Hee-Kyong Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecureMCP，一个集成RBAC与MCP服务器的策略驱动型LLM数据访问框架，通过五层防御模块（check_policy、explain_gate、SQL Interceptor、Risk Level Filter、DB Isolation）以顺序故障关闭流水线方式，防御六种基于OWASP Top 10的提示注入攻击。在IoT-SQL数据集和Qwen3-8B上的实验表明，防御模块在保持执行精度的同时，在2400条对抗性查询上实现了82.3%的策略合规率，其中check_policy贡献了78.7%的拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的SQL查询在AIoT系统中存在严重安全风险，提示注入攻击可操纵LLM生成未授权查询以泄露敏感数据或执行破坏性操作。现有NL2SQL研究仅关注查询准确性，MCP服务器实现仅提供SQL级别保护而缺乏细粒度的基于角色的访问控制。

Method: 提出了SecureMCP框架，集成基于角色的访问控制（RBAC）与MCP服务器。框架包含五个防御模块：check_policy（表/列级RBAC）、explain_gate（高成本查询阻塞）、SQL Interceptor（危险模式检测）、Risk Level Filter（SQL风险分类）、DB Isolation（跨数据库限制），以顺序故障关闭流水线方式运行，对应六种基于OWASP Top 10的提示注入类型。

Result: 实验A显示防御模块保持执行精度，EX-in-ALLOW在四个RBAC角色中维持在65.1%-76.4%，匹配未保护基线63.8%。实验B显示SecureMCP在2400条对抗性查询上实现82.3%的策略合规率，真实防御失败率仅3.4%。防御深度分析显示check_policy贡献78.7%的拦截，次要模块额外提升17.5个百分点。注入接入率72.5%证实了LLM的高度敏感性。

Conclusion: SecureMCP通过外部策略执行有效防御LLM生成的SQL查询中的提示注入攻击，验证了多层防御架构的必要性和有效性。该框架在保持查询准确性的同时显著提升了安全性。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM)-generated SQL queries in Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems introduces critical security risks, as prompt injection attacks can manipulate LLMs into producing unauthorized queries that expose sensitive data or execute destructive operations. Existing NL2SQL research focuses on query accuracy, while MCP server implementations provide only SQL-level protections without fine-grained role-based access control. This paper proposes SecureMCP, a policy-enforced LLM data access framework integrating Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) with an MCP server to establish multi-layer defense for LLM-generated SQL execution. The framework incorporates five defense modules -- check_policy for table-and-column-level RBAC, explain_gate for cost-explosive query blocking, SQL Interceptor for dangerous pattern detection, Risk Level Filter for SQL risk classification, and DB Isolation for cross-database restriction -- operating in a sequential fail-closed pipeline mapped to six prompt injection types grounded in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. We evaluate SecureMCP on the IoT-SQL dataset (11 tables, 173 columns, 239,398 records) using Qwen3-8B. Experiment A demonstrates that defense modules preserve execution accuracy, with EX-in-ALLOW remaining within 65.1%-76.4% across four RBAC roles, matching the unprotected baseline of 63.8%. Experiment B shows that SecureMCP achieves 82.3% Policy Compliance on 2,400 adversarial queries, with genuine defense failures limited to 3.4%. The defense-in-depth analysis reveals check_policy accounts for 78.7% of blocks, while secondary modules contribute an additional 17.5 percentage-point improvement. The Injection Incorporation Rate of 72.5% confirms high LLM susceptibility, establishing the necessity of external policy enforcement.

</details>


### [207] [Differential Privacy in the Extensive-Form Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05266)
*Stephen Pasteris,Rahul Savani,Theodore Turocy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对扩展式博弈赌博机问题，提出了首个满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，且每轮时间复杂度等于服务器向用户传输简化策略所需时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展式博弈赌博机问题中用户与服务器的交互涉及策略传输，可能泄露用户隐私，而现有研究尚未考虑任何形式的差分隐私保护。

Method: 提出一种满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，通过每轮向用户传输简化策略并观察所在信息集及收益/损失来更新策略。

Result: 算法取得了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A为总动作数，S为简化策略数，T为试验次数；每轮时间复杂度（对数因子内）等于传输简化策略所需时间。

Conclusion: 首次将差分隐私引入扩展式博弈赌博机问题，且局部差分隐私作为最强隐私保护形式被实现，为隐私保护的在线学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: We consider the extensive-form bandit problem, where on each trial the learner (a user coordinated by a server) plays an extensive-form game against an oblivious adversary, observing the information sets it finds itself in as well as the resulting payoff/loss. We give an algorithm for this problem that satisfies $ε$-local differential privacy and attains a regret of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$, where $A$ is the total number of actions that the learner can possibly take, $S$ is the number of the learner's possible reduced strategies, and $T$ is the number of trials. On each trial, the time complexity of our algorithm is, up to a factor logarithmic in the maximum number of actions at an infoset, equal to the time required for the server to transmit the reduced strategy to the user. We note that local differential privacy is the strongest version of differential privacy and, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study differential privacy of any form in the extensive-form bandit problem.

</details>


### [208] [Dynamic Authorization for Knowledge-Base Agents in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05269)
*Loay Abdelrazek,Leyli Karacay,Marin Orlic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对6G多智能体系统中知识库的安全访问问题，提出了一种结合角色和一阶逻辑谓词的混合授权框架，在知识图谱层面实施零信任原则，通过三元组级别的授权控制取代传统RBAC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G架构向去中心化多智能体系统演进，传统RBAC模型无法提供自主智能体与语义数据交互所需的细粒度授权控制，亟需新的安全访问机制。

Method: 提出混合授权框架，将角色与一阶逻辑谓词相结合；在知识图谱层面实施零信任原则；通过主体-谓词-客体三元组级别的授权控制来消除权限继承。

Result: 实现了三元组级别的细粒度授权控制，确保智能体仅能访问其特定功能生命周期所需的元数据。

Conclusion: 该框架为6G多智能体系统中的知识库访问提供了更安全、更细粒度的授权控制方案。

Abstract: As 6G architectures transition toward decentralized Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), ensuring secure access to shared Knowledge Bases (KB) is critical. Traditional authorization models like RBAC fail to provide the granularity required for autonomous agents interacting with Semantic-based data. This work proposes a hybrid authorization framework that integrates roles and First-Order Logic (FOL) predicates to enforce zero-trust principles at the knowledge-graph level. We eliminate permission inheritance by enforcing authorization at the triple level (Subject-Predicate-Object), ensuring agents only access metadata required for their specific functional lifecycle.

</details>


### [209] [Shattering the Echo Chamber: Hidden Safeguards in Manuscripts Against the AI Takeover of Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05271)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Yong Yang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IntraGuard，一种基于PDF结构-视觉解耦的黑盒防御框架，通过在稿件中嵌入异构防御文本对象来检测或干扰LLM外包的同行评审，在7个商业聊天机器人和12个会议场景下达到84%防御成功率，且对人工评审无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑委员会和项目委员会日益担忧审稿人将同行评审完全外包给商业聊天机器人，而现有防御方法（如隐藏指令）因依赖同质化载荷和流间注入方式，容易被清洗或中和，亟需更鲁棒的防御方案。

Method: 提出IntraGuard框架，核心思想是利用PDF的结构-视觉解耦特性，通过三种流内注入机制将异构防御文本对象嵌入PDF底层结构而不改变视觉呈现。支持显式策略（触发拒绝/警告信号）和隐式策略（嵌入预定义文本标记）。

Result: 在7个真实商业聊天机器人设置和12个跨学科会议场景下，防御成功率最高达84%；对人工评审保持不变性；平均每篇稿件仅增加1秒开销；对抗11种自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: IntraGuard是一种轻量级、硬件无关、适用于委员会侧部署的有效防御框架，能够抵御端到端审稿外包威胁，并讨论了构建集成防御的启示。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable, editorial boards and program committees are growing concerned about reviewers who fully outsource peer review to commercial chatbots. This concern stems from prior findings that current chatbots lack the independent critical thinking and depth of reasoning required to assess scientific novelty. One promising direction for mitigating this concern is to embed hidden instructions into manuscripts that disrupt or alter chatbot-generated reviews. However, existing methods remain intuitive and fragile, as they typically rely on homogeneous payloads injected in an inter-stream manner, rendering them susceptible to sanitization or neutralization.
  In this paper, we identify End-to-End Review Outsourcing as an emerging threat and propose IntraGuard, a black-box, venue-agnostic defense framework grounded in the structural--visual decoupling inherent to the PDF. Designed for committee-side deployment, IntraGuard supports both explicit strategies that trigger refusal or warning signals, and implicit strategies that embed predefined textual markers into the generated review. These strategies can be deployed via any of three intra-stream injection mechanisms, each of which seamlessly embeds heterogeneous defensive text objects within the PDF's underlying structure without altering its visual presentation. Extensive evaluations across 7 real-world commercial chatbot settings and 12 venues spanning diverse disciplines show that IntraGuard achieves a defense success rate of up to 84%, while preserving peer-review invariance for human reviewers. IntraGuard is lightweight and hardware-independent, incurring an average overhead of only one second per manuscript on a commodity personal computer. We further evaluate 11 adaptive attacks spanning manuscript sanitization and instruction interference, and discuss the implications of constructing ensemble defenses.

</details>


### [210] [Sealing the Audit-Runtime Gap for LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05274)
*Tingda Shen,Yebo Feng,Konglin Zhu,Xiaojun Jia,Yang Liu,Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL 是首个通过去中心化链上注册表、DAO 审计和技能验证加载器 (SVL) 来弥合 LLM 技能审计与运行时鸿沟的框架，实现了从发布到运行时的加密绑定，在 86ms 内完成批量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生态系统（如 Claude Code、ChatGPT）越来越多地依赖技能（指令+可执行工具包），但技能内容与可信指令无法可靠分离，可执行端可调用特权操作，导致技能供应链面临注入、篡改和 rug-pull 攻击。现有防御存在阶段性局限（中心化签名、审计报告与运行时脱节、策略引擎无法证明已批准内容），缺乏将审计与运行时统一的可验证机制。

Method: SIGIL 提出两个核心机制：(1) 可验证托管——基于去中心化链上注册表，支持四种发布类型（Transparent, Licensed, Sealed, Committed），经 DAO 审计委员会在质押-罚没经济模型下审核后方可收录；(2) 可验证加载——技能验证加载器 (SVL) 作为强制加载路径，按类型检索和解密技能，校验链上完整性记录，并在注入上下文前强制执行权限清单。

Result: 在实际部署中对 1,023 个野外技能（覆盖六种攻击类型）进行评估，SVL 在加载时完成批量完整性验证，耗时低于 86ms，验证了实用成本下的可行性。

Conclusion: LLM 技能可以在实用成本下实现从发布到运行时的加密绑定，SIGIL 填补了审计与运行时之间的安全鸿沟。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) ecosystems such as Claude Code and ChatGPT increasingly rely on skills: packages of natural-language instructions and executable tools. Once in the LLM's context, skill content cannot be reliably separated from trusted instructions, and a skill's executable side can invoke privileged actions, exposing the skill supply chain to injection, tampering, and rug-pull attacks. Existing defenses are stage-bound: centralized signing, audit reports unbound from the runtime artifact, or policy engines that cannot attest to what was approved. We present SIGIL, the first framework that seals the audit-runtime gap for LLM skills. SIGIL delivers verifiable hosting through a tamper-evident, decentralized on-chain registry from which LLMs fetch skills directly. The registry admits four publication types, Transparent, Licensed, Sealed, and Committed, spanning plaintext public distribution, monetized access, custodial use, and off-chain workflows; before admission, every skill is vetted by a Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO) audit committee that supports pluggable auditing methods under a stake-and-slash economic model. At load time, SIGIL delivers verified loading through a skill verification protocol executed by a Skill Verification Loader (SVL) embedded as the mandatory loading path: the SVL retrieves and decrypts the skill as its type requires, verifies its integrity against the on-chain record, and enforces its permission manifest before context injection. We evaluate SIGIL on a real-world deployment against 1,023 in-the-wild skills spanning six attack types. At load time, the SVL verifies each skill's integrity against its on-chain record and enforces its approved permission manifest, completing batched verification under 86 ms. Together, these results show that LLM skills can be cryptographically bound from publication through runtime at practical cost.

</details>


### [211] [GLiNER Guard: Unified Encoder Family for Production LLM Safety and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05277)
*Bogdan Minko,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard)，一种统一编码器架构，在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测，在严格延迟和成本约束下提供高效的LLM系统护栏方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM系统在延迟和成本约束下，需要同时进行安全审核和PII检测。自回归审核器准确但昂贵，轻量编码器更快但能力不足。现有的分离式管线效率低下，亟需一种统一的高效解决方案。

Method: 提出GLiGuard统一编码器架构，包含三个变体：紧凑型单编码器和双编码器（145-147M参数）用于高吞吐服务，以及GLiGuard Omni（209M参数）用于更强的审核质量。同时发布PII-Bench，一个用于评估端到端管线中PII检测的跨度级基准。

Result: 在单张A100上使用动态批处理时，紧凑模型达到193 requests/sec，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍。Omni在公开安全基准上与更大的审核器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于编码器的护栏方案为始终在线的审核提供了低成本、实用的替代方案，在保持可竞争质量的同时显著提升吞吐量和降低延迟。

Abstract: Production LLM systems require both safety moderation and PII detection under strict latency and cost constraints. This creates a trade-off: autoregressive moderators are accurate but expensive, while lightweight encoders are faster but less capable. We present GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard), a unified encoder that performs safety classification and PII detection in a single forward pass, simplifying safety pipelines. We introduce three variants: compact uni- and bi-encoders (145-147M) for high-throughput serving, and GLiGuard Omni (209M) for stronger moderation quality. Under dynamic batching on a single A100, the compact model reaches 193 requests/sec with P99 latency below 1s, achieving 1.6x higher throughput than GLiNER2. Omni remains competitive with much larger moderators on public safety benchmarks. We also release PII-Bench, a span-level benchmark for evaluating PII detection in end-to-end pipelines. Overall, encoder-based guardrails offer a practical low-cost alternative for always-on moderation. Models and benchmarks are released on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [212] [Securing the Agent: Vendor-Neutral, Multitenant Enterprise Retrieval and Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05287)
*Francisco Javier Arceo,Varsha Prasad Narsing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对企业级RAG和Agentic AI系统中的多租户数据隔离问题，提出了一种分层隔离架构，通过策略感知的摄入、检索时门控和共享推理，结合服务端Agent编排，实现跨租户数据泄露的消除，并在OGX框架中开源实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统按相关性而非授权进行检索，导致多租户环境中一个租户的查询可能返回另一租户的机密数据。此外，工具介导的披露、跨轮次上下文积累以及客户端编排绕过等问题在Agentic系统中进一步加剧了安全风险。企业环境对多租户隔离、访问控制、合规性和成本压力有严格要求，而学术界和消费级API方案未充分解决这些问题。

Method: 提出分层隔离架构：(1) 策略感知摄入——数据摄入时嵌入访问控制策略；(2) 检索时门控（ABAC门控）——基于属性的访问控制在检索阶段过滤未授权结果；(3) 共享推理——共享基础设施但隔离状态；(4) 服务端Agent编排——将工具执行授权、状态隔离和策略执行集中到服务端，形成强制隔离点，同时允许客户端保留Agent组合和延迟敏感操作的控制权。

Result: 在OGX框架（开源、供应商中立、兼容OpenAI Responses API的多轮编排系统）中实现该架构，实证评估表明ABAC门控在引入可忽略不计的开销下完全消除了跨租户数据泄露。

Conclusion: 将授权机制与检索和推理过程深度集成，通过服务端集中化策略执行，是解决企业级多租户RAG/Agent系统数据隔离问题的有效途径。该方案在安全性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，且开源实现为业界提供了可落地的参考。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic AI systems are increasingly prevalent in enterprise AI deployments. However, real enterprise environments introduce challenges largely absent from academic treatments and consumer-facing APIs: multiple tenants with heterogeneous data, strict access-control requirements, regulatory compliance, and cost pressures that demand shared infrastructure.
  A fundamental problem underlies existing RAG architectures in these settings: retrieval systems rank documents by relevance--whether through semantic similarity, keyword matching, or hybrid approaches--not by authorization, so a query from one tenant can surface another tenant's confidential data simply because it scores highest. We formalize this gap and analyze additional shortcomings--including tool-mediated disclosure, context accumulation across turns, and client-side orchestration bypass--that arise when agentic systems conflate relevance with authorization. To address these challenges, we introduce a layered isolation architecture combining policy-aware ingestion, retrieval-time gating, and shared inference, enforced through server-side agentic orchestration. This approach centralizes security-critical operations--tool execution authorization, state isolation, and policy enforcement--on the server, creating natural enforcement points for multitenant isolation while allowing client-side frameworks to retain control over agent composition and latency-sensitive operations.
  We validate the proposed architecture through an open-source implementation in OGX, a vendor-neutral framework that implements an OpenAI-compatible, open-source Responses API with server-side multi-turn orchestration. We evaluate it empirically and show that ABAC gating eliminates cross-tenant leakage while introducing negligible overhead.

</details>


### [213] [How Far Are VLMs from Privacy Awareness in the Physical World? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05340)
*Junran Wang,Xinjie Shen,Zehao Jin,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ImmersedPrivacy，一个基于Unity模拟器的交互式音视频评估框架，用于测试具身视觉语言模型在物理环境中的隐私感知能力。通过对12个SOTA模型的评估，发现所有模型在杂乱场景中性能随复杂度增加而单调下降，在社交情境切换时准确率不超过65%，在冲突指令下最佳模型仅51%能平衡任务完成与隐私保护，揭示了当前VLM存在感知脆弱性和隐私知识无法指导情境行为的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私评估基准局限于单模态文本表示，无法捕捉真实物理环境中VLM作为具身智能体在家庭、医院等私密空间中的隐私感知需求，缺乏能够模拟物理交互和场景复杂度的评估框架。

Method: 基于Unity模拟器构建交互式音视频评估框架，设计三个渐进式测试层级：1) 杂乱场景中识别敏感物品；2) 适应社交情境变化；3) 解决显式指令与隐式隐私约束的冲突。

Result: 评估12个SOTA模型发现：所有模型在杂乱场景中表现单调下降；社交情境切换时无模型超过65%准确率；冲突指令下最佳模型gemini-3.1-pro仅51%能平衡任务与隐私。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理世界中存在感知脆弱性，其隐私知识无法有效指导情境行为，亟需改进以安全部署于具身智能系统。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous cognitive cores for embodied assistants, evaluating their privacy awareness in physical environments becomes critical. Unlike digital chatbots, these agents operate in intimate spaces, such as homes and hospitals, where they possess the physical agency to observe and manipulate privacy-sensitive information and artifacts. However, current benchmarks remain limited to unimodal, text-based representations that cannot capture the demands of real-world settings. To bridge this gap, we present ImmersedPrivacy, an interactive audio-visual evaluation framework that simulates realistic physical environments using a Unity-based simulator. ImmersedPrivacy evaluates physically grounded privacy awareness across three progressive tiers that test a model's ability to identify sensitive items in cluttered scenes, adapt to shifting social contexts, and resolve conflicts between explicit commands and inferred privacy constraints. Our evaluation of 12 state-of-the-art models reveals consistent deficits. In cluttered scenes, all models exhibit monotonic performance decay as scene complexity grows due to perceptual deficit. When social context shifts, no model exceed 65% selection accuracy. Under conflicting commands, the best model gemini-3.1-pro perfectly balances task completion and privacy preservation in only 51% of cases. These findings reveal that current VLMs in the physical world suffer from perceptual fragility and fail to let their knowledge of privacy cues govern their situated behavior. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/immersed-privacy/immersed-privacy .

</details>


### [214] [Privacy Without Losing Place: A Paradigm for Private Retrieval in Spatial RAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05459)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri,Vinay M. Shashidhar,Jong Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PAS（隐私锚点替代）机制，通过相对锚点编码实现用户位置隐私保护，在空间检索增强生成系统中实现约370-400米对抗性定位误差，同时保留半数以上检索性能，且下游生成质量保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方法直接扰动用户位置，难以与RAG管线无缝集成，需要一种结构化空间表示机制来平衡位置隐私与检索生成性能。

Method: 使用相对锚点编码（锚点+方向箱+距离箱）表示用户位置，替代直接位置扰动，实现与RAG管线的兼容。

Result: 在合成城市数据集上实现约370-400米对抗性定位误差，保留半数以上检索性能，下游生成质量稳健；发现隐私-效用关系呈非单调性。

Conclusion: 结构化空间表示为基于位置的RAG系统提供了实用的隐私保护途径，大语言模型能够补偿不完美的空间检索。

Abstract: This work introduces PAS -- Privacy Anchor Substitution, a structured mechanism for enabling user location privacy in spatial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Unlike conventional differential privacy methods that directly perturb user locations, PAS represents location with relative anchor encoding consisting of an anchor, direction bin, and distance bin, allowing seamless integration with modern RAG pipelines. We evaluate PAS on a synthetic urban dataset and show that it achieves impressive coarse privacy guarantees, with approximately 370-400m adversarial location error, while retaining more than half of the baseline retrieval performance. Despite the slight drop in retrieval performance, the downstream generation quality under PAS remains comparatively robust, indicating that large language models can compensate for imperfect spatial retrieval. Furthermore, we provide empirical analysis showing that PAS exhibits non-monotonic privacy-utility relationship with respect to privacy parameters. We attribute this to geometric bias induced by anchor discretization, making it different from continuous noise mechanisms such as geo-indistinguishability. Our results show that structured spatial representations offer a practical approach to privacy in location based reasoning in RAG systems.

</details>


### [215] [SOCpilot: Verifying Policy Compliance for LLM-Assisted Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05501)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Leonardo Vaz de Meneses,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SOCpilot框架，用于验证LLM辅助生成的应急响应计划是否符合安全策略合规性。通过对200个真实事件进行案例研究，发现确定性验证器能有效移除不合规动作且不降低召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）开始使用大语言模型（LLM）作为副驾驶来起草应急响应计划，但这些计划可能包含目录有效但违反强制步骤、必要排序或审批关卡的动作。现有方法缺乏对这种合规性问题的可测量手段。

Method: SOCpilot框架：固定事件包、动作目录、策略规则、验证器和公开证据表面；通过确定性验证器验证副驾驶建议的动作轨迹；在金融部门匿名生产SOC的200个真实事件上评估两个LLM提供商，并与分析师撰写的参考计划对比。

Result: 相同的策略文本使两个提供商朝相反方向移动；确定性验证器移除了466个不合规/需审批的动作，且不降低基线任务召回率；在固定语料库的3次重复运行中聚合率保持稳定。

Conclusion: SOCpilot能够有效衡量和验证LLM辅助生成的应急响应计划的策略合规性，移除了大量不合规动作，同时保持了任务召回率。实验结果表明该方法稳定可靠，且公开发布了可复现的工件。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) are beginning to use large language models (LLMs) as copilots to draft incident-response plans. These plans may include actions that are valid per the catalog but still violate mandatory steps, required ordering, or approval gates before analyst review. SOCpilot makes this compliance question measurable at the plan boundary. It fixes the incident package, action catalog, policy rules, verifier, and public evidence surface. Next, it verifies the copilot's proposed action trace. We evaluate two LLM providers on 200 real incidents from an anonymized production SOC in a financial-sector case study. We compare their plans to paired analyst-authored references from the same security orchestration, automation, and response (SOAR) cases. An identical inline policy text moves the two providers in opposite directions. A deterministic verifier removes 466 non-compliant, approval-gated actions, without reducing baseline-task recall. Aggregate rates remain stable across 3 reruns of the fixed corpus. The official evidence focuses on approval-gated decisions regarding recovery and containment. Separately, the artifact exposes zero-cost readiness checks for mandatory and ordering repairs. We release the runnable artifact so independent reviewers can rederive the public results without access to private incident data.

</details>


### [216] [Beyond Collection: Measuring the Detection Efficacy of Modern Security Logging Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05531)
*Ryan Holeman,John Hastings,Varghese Mathew Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了CIM、OCSF、ECS三种现代安全日志标准在检测远程代码执行漏洞方面的有效性，基于自动化SETC框架在容器化环境中生成50个可复现漏洞场景，通过测量遥测完整性和漏洞可检测性量化各标准效能，发现了显著差异并为安全实践者提供了基于证据的日志标准选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各类行业标准日志框架在威胁检测中的相对有效性尚缺乏系统研究，存在关键空白。

Method: 基于自动化的SETC框架，在容器化环境中系统生成可复现的漏洞场景，收集CIM、OCSF、ECS三种日志标准的遥测数据，通过遥测完整性和漏洞可检测性两个指标量化检测效能，涉及50种不同的远程代码执行漏洞实验。

Result: 不同日志标准在捕获关键攻击指标的能力上存在显著差异，识别出了关键差距。

Conclusion: 提供了一种新颖的、可扩展且可复现的漏洞遥测评估方法论，为安全从业者提供了基于证据的日志标准选择指导。

Abstract: Effective security logging is crucial for the timely and accurate detection of cyber threats; however, the relative effectiveness of various industry-standard logging frameworks remains understudied. This paper addresses this critical gap by presenting the first systematic evaluation of modern security logging standards utilizing a novel methodology built upon the automated Security Exploit Telemetry Collection (SETC) framework. SETC systematically generates reproducible exploit scenarios in containerized environments, collecting rich telemetry across multiple logging standards, including CIM (Common Information Model), OCSF (Open Cybersecurity Schema Framework), and ECS (Elastic Common Schema). The detection efficacy of each logging standard is quantified by measuring telemetry completeness and exploit detectability across standardized logs through detailed experiments involving 50 diverse remote code execution vulnerabilities. The resulting findings identify critical gaps and reveal significant differences in logging standards' abilities to capture key attack indicators. Our contributions include a novel evaluation methodology that enables scalable and reproducible analysis of exploit telemetry, as well as new findings that provide clear, evidence-based guidance for security practitioners to make informed decisions about adopting logging standards.

</details>


### [217] [Architecture Matters: Comparing RAG Systems under Knowledge Base Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05632)
*Samuel Korn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评估了四种RAG架构（vanilla RAG、agentic RAG、MADAM-RAG、Recursive Language Models）在知识库投毒攻击下的鲁棒性，发现架构是影响对抗鲁棒性的高变量因素：在CorruptRAG-AK攻击下，攻击成功率从vanilla的81.9%到RLM的24.4%不等，差距达58个百分点。攻击优势主要来自对抗性框架（adversarial framing）而非检索优化，漏洞集中在内容推理阶段。MADAM-RAG的检测行为被高估（精确率约48.5%），且即使检测到矛盾也无法可靠解决，甚至在干净输入上产生41.4%的无回答率。论文还引入了七类行为分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统对知识库投毒攻击的评估几乎仅限于vanilla retrieve-then-generate流程，而设计了处理冲突检索信息的架构（多智能体辩论、智能检索、递归语言模型）尚未经过对抗性优化矛盾的测试，缺乏对这些架构鲁棒性的系统比较。

Method: 在921个Natural Questions QA对上，控制单文档（N=1）投毒，比较干净基线、朴素注入和CorruptRAG-AK对抗攻击。评估四种RAG架构（vanilla RAG、agentic RAG、MADAM-RAG、Recursive Language Models），分解攻击成功率差距的来源（检索优化 vs. 对抗框架），并引入七类行为分类法。

Result: 架构是影响对抗鲁棒性的高变量因素：CorruptRAG-AK攻击下，攻击成功率从vanilla的81.9%到RLM的24.4%，跨度约58个百分点，而干净准确率均约92%。攻击优势主要来自对抗性框架而非检索优化，漏洞集中在内容推理阶段。MADAM-RAG的矛盾检测被高估（精确率约48.5%），且干净输入上产生41.4%的无回答率。

Conclusion: 不同RAG架构在对抗鲁棒性上存在显著差异，未来防御应聚焦内容推理阶段而非检索优化；现有的矛盾检测机制（如MADAM-RAG）存在严重高估和不可靠问题，需要更鲁棒的设计。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to knowledge base poisoning, yet existing attacks have been evaluated almost exclusively against vanilla retrieve-then-generate pipelines. Architectures designed to handle conflicting retrieved information - multi-agent debate, agentic retrieval, recursive language models - remain untested against adversarially optimized contradictions. We evaluate four RAG architectures (vanilla RAG, agentic RAG, MADAM-RAG, and Recursive Language Models) under controlled single-document (N=1) poisoning on 921 Natural Questions QA pairs, comparing a clean baseline, naive injection, and CorruptRAG-AK - an adversarial attack whose meta-epistemic framing targets credibility assessment. Architecture is a high-impact variable in adversarial robustness: under CorruptRAG-AK, attack success rates range from 81.9% (vanilla) to 24.4% (RLM) - a spread of nearly 58 percentage points across architectures with comparable clean accuracy (~92%). Decomposing this gap, once the poisoned document is retrieved, adversarial framing - not retrieval optimization - drives the majority of CorruptRAG-AK's advantage for three of four architectures, localizing the cross-architecture vulnerability at the content-reasoning stage. Our MADAM-RAG reimplementation shows the highest apparent contradiction detection rate, though our LLM judge over-identifies this behavior (~48.5% precision), so reported rates are upper bounds. Regardless of detection, MADAM-RAG cannot resolve contradictions reliably, producing a 41.4% non-answer rate even on clean inputs - though implementation divergences from the original may contribute. We introduce a seven-category behavioral taxonomy capturing contradiction detection, hedging, and failure modes beyond binary accuracy. Code, data, and analysis notebooks are publicly available.

</details>


### [218] [AoI-Guided Client Selection for Robust and Timely Federated Intrusion Detection in Cloud-Edge Security Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05644)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中的客户端参与问题，将时效性（AoI）引入云端入侵检测场景，提出三种轻量级调度策略（AoI优先、效用优先、混合策略），实验表明AoI感知选择可将平均AoI降低39-41%、峰值AoI降低约70%，混合策略在保持检测性能的同时提供了可解释的权衡参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产环境的联邦学习入侵检测中，仅有部分客户端参与每轮训练，异构带宽、掉队者与掉线问题导致服务器依赖过时客户端信息，而现有研究缺乏对客户端参与时效性的系统建模与优化。

Method: 将客户端参与建模为时效感知的系统问题，引入信息年龄（AoI）指标，设计并比较三种策略：AoI优先、效用优先、AoI+效用混合（含可调权衡参数），在多种数据集与对抗设定下进行评估。

Result: AoI感知选择相比随机采样降低平均AoI约39-41%、峰值AoI约70%，混合策略通常保持Macro-F1/AUC性能，结合修剪均值聚合可应对标签翻转投毒攻击。

Conclusion: 云端隐私保护入侵分析可通过轻量级调度层提升时效性，无需改变底层联邦学习的参与预算，混合策略提供了时效性、检测质量与鲁棒性之间的可解释权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is attractive for cloud-edge intrusion detection because it enables collaborative training over distributed telemetry without centralizing raw logs. In production security analytics pipelines, however, only a subset of clients participates in each round, and heterogeneous bandwidth, stragglers, and dropouts can cause the server to rely on stale client information. This paper studies client participation as a timeliness-aware systems problem using Age of Information (AoI). We compare three lightweight policies for federated intrusion detection: AoI-first, utility-first, and a hybrid AoI+utility rule with a tunable trade-off parameter.
  Across a CIC-IDS2017 DDoS/PortScan mini subset, NSL-KDD, ToN-IoT, and a synthetic drift benchmark under clean, poisoning, and poisoning-plus-robust-aggregation settings, AoI-aware selection reduces average AoI by about 39--41% and peak AoI by about 70% relative to random sampling while keeping the per-round communication budget fixed. The hybrid policy usually preserves Macro-F1/AUC and provides an interpretable knob for balancing freshness, detection quality, and robustness, although it is not uniformly Pareto-dominant once false positive rate is included. Robustness is evaluated by combining AoI-guided selection with trimmed-mean aggregation under label-flip poisoning; the selection policy itself is not intended as a standalone Byzantine defense. The main practical message is that cloud-edge, privacy-preserving intrusion analytics can improve timeliness through a lightweight scheduling layer without changing the underlying FL participation budget.

</details>


### [219] [$α$-Wasserstein Mechanism for Rényi Pufferfish Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05723)
*Ni Ding,Wenjin Yang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出α-Wasserstein机制，通过Hölder不等式将Laplace和Gaussian噪声的尺度参数与W_α度量上界关联，实现(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish隐私。该框架在α=∞时退化为经典W_∞机制，并推广至指数机制。实验表明，相比W_∞方法，α-Wasserstein机制显著降低噪声功率，且Gaussian机制优于Laplace机制，且无需δ近似即可达到精确隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pufferfish隐私框架主要基于W_∞度量，噪声添加较为保守，导致数据效用损失较大。本文旨在引入Rényi散度与Wasserstein距离的结合，设计更灵活的α-Wasserstein机制以降低噪声功率，同时保持严格的隐私保证。

Method: 利用Hölder不等式建立Laplace机制尺度参数与W_α度量上界的关系，以此校准噪声以满足(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish隐私；将相同思路推广至指数机制和Gaussian噪声机制（针对α∈(1,∞)）。

Result: 所提α-Wasserstein机制在相同隐私预算下噪声功率显著低于传统W_∞机制；Gaussian机制比Laplace机制效用更优；所有机制均实现精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish隐私，无需δ近似放松。

Conclusion: α-Wasserstein机制为Pufferfish隐私提供了一种更灵活、效用更高的噪声校准方法，统一并推广了已有W_∞和Rényi DP框架下的结果，兼具理论完备性和实践优越性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the $α$-Wasserstein mechanism for achieving Rényi Pufferfish Privacy using Laplace and Gaussian noise. By leveraging Hölder's inequality, we demonstrate that the scale parameter of the Laplace mechanism can be calibrated via an upper bound on the $W_α$ metric to satisfy $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy for $α\in (1, \infty]$. We show that at the limit $α= \infty$, this framework recovers the established $W_\infty$ mechanism for $ε$-pufferfish privacy. This result is subsequently extended to the exponential mechanism. Furthermore, we propose a $W_α$ mechanism for Gaussian noise for $α\in (1, \infty)$, demonstrating that it generalizes existing results within the Rényi Differential Privacy framework. Experimental evaluations reveal that our $α$-Wasserstein mechanism significantly reduces noise power compared to the conventional $W_\infty$-based approach, with the Gaussian mechanism providing superior utility over the Laplace mechanism. Notably, the mechanisms derived in this work achieve exact $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy without requiring additional relaxations, such as $δ$-approximations.

</details>


### [220] [Stego Battlefield: Evaluating Image Steganography Attacks and Steganalysis Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05789)
*Zhen Sun,Zongmin Zhang,Leyi Sheng,Yule Liu,Yifan Liao,Ke Li,Xinhu Zheng,Jiaheng Wei,Wenyuan Yang,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SADBench是一个系统性的图像隐写安全评估基准，包含4个核心任务（攻击能力、防御能力、效率、可迁移性），揭示了INN和自编码器稳定性最优、域内检测近乎完美但存在可迁移性不对称等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像隐写技术可被攻击者用作隐蔽通道绕过内容审核、传播有害语义、向大模型注入恶意指令，而现有研究缺乏统一、系统的评估框架来量化此类安全风险。

Method: 构建了包含4个核心任务（隐写攻击能力评估、隐写分析防御能力评估、效率评估、可迁移性评估）的基准框架，评估图像载荷和文本载荷两种隐写方式，覆盖多种封面分布，利用有害视觉语义和恶意指令模拟攻击。

Result: (i) INN和自编码器方法稳定性最优；(ii) 域内检测近乎完美且成本低于生成；(iii) 存在关键的可迁移性不对称性，攻击可泛化到新分布而检测器无法适应；(iv) 真实世界威胁在社交媒体中持续存在，载荷可抵抗最小压缩或通过模拟训练适应强压缩。

Conclusion: SADBench建立了一个系统、可复现、可扩展的量化风险评估框架，为隐写防御领域可衡量的安全驱动进步铺平了道路。

Abstract: Image steganography is widely used to protect user privacy and enable covert communication. However, it can also be abused by the adversary as a covert channel to bypass content moderation, disseminate harmful semantics, and even hide malicious instructions in images to elicit dangerous outputs from large models, posing a practical security risk that continues to evolve. To address the lack of a unified and systematic evaluation framework, we propose SADBench, a systematic benchmark that assesses the adversary's ability to inject harmful secrets via steganography and the defender's ability to detect such threats through steganalysis. Crucially, SADBench comprises $4$ core tasks, namely steganography attack capability evaluation, steganalysis defense capability evaluation, efficiency evaluation, and transferability evaluation. It evaluates both image-payload and text-payload steganography across diverse cover distributions, utilizing harmful visual semantics and toxic instructions to simulate malicious attacks. Across a broad set of attacks and detectors, SADBench reveals that (i) INN and autoencoder-based methods demonstrate superior stability compared to other architectures, (ii) in-domain detection is near-perfect and cheaper than generation, (iii) a critical asymmetry exists in transferability where attacks robustly generalize to new distributions while detectors fail to adapt, and (iv) real-world threats persist on social media, where payloads either survive minimal compression or effectively adapt to aggressive compression via simulated training. Overall, SADBench establishes a systematic, reproducible, and extensible framework to quantify risks, paving the way for measurable and security-driven advancements in steganography defense.

</details>


### [221] [LCC-LLM: Leveraging Code-Centric Large Language Models for Malware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05807)
*Christopher G. Pedraza Pohlenz,Hassan Jalil Hadi,Ali Hassan,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LCC-LLM，一个以代码为中心的基准数据集和基于证据的恶意软件归因框架。通过LCCD数据集（含约3.4万PE样本的多维度逆向工程表示）和LangGraph编排的七层RAG流水线，结合CoVE验证与多维质量门控，微调DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B模型。在43类恶意分析任务上平均语义相似度0.634，真实案例中结构化分析通过率10/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的恶意软件归因方法受限于不支持的指标和缺乏代码级证据支撑，无法有效识别恶意和脆弱代码段。

Method: 构建LCCD数据集（约3.4万PE样本，含反编译C代码、汇编、CFG/FCG、十六进制、PE元数据、可疑API证据及结构特征）；设计LangGraph编排的七层RAG流水线，集成CoVE进行IoC验证和多维质量门控；使用课程排序指令数据通过QLoRA微调DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B。

Result: 在43类恶意分析任务上平均语义相似度0.634，结构化报告生成、IoC提取、漏洞评估、恶意软件配置提取和恶意类检测等任务表现最优；MalwareBazaar真实案例中结构化分析通过率10/10，生成CFG/FCG证据、MITRE ATT&CK映射、检测指南和分析师就绪报告。

Conclusion: 以代码为中心的表示、检索归因和验证引导推理能够提升LLM辅助恶意软件归因的可靠性和操作实用性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly explored for malware analysis; however, current LLM-based malware attribution remains limited by unsupported indicators and insufficient code-level grounding for identifying malicious and vulnerable code segments.
