To address these limitations, this research introduces LCC-LLM, a code-centric benchmark dataset and evidence-grounded framework for malware attribution and multi-task static malware analysis. The proposed LCCD dataset contains approximately 34K PE samples processed through a large-scale reverse-engineering pipeline and represented using decompiled C code, assembly code, CFG/FCG artifacts, hexadecimal data, PE metadata, suspicious API evidence, and structural features. Beyond dataset construction, LCC-LLM integrates LangGraph-orchestrated static analysis with multi-source cybersecurity knowledge to support evidence-grounded malware reasoning. The framework employs a seven-layer retrieval-augmented generation pipeline, CoVe for IoC validation, and a multi-dimensional quality gate to improve factual reliability and analyst-oriented decision support. Curriculum-ordered instruction data is used to fine-tune DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B and Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B using QLoRA. Evaluation across 43 malware-analysis task types achieves an average semantic similarity of 0.634, with the highest task-level performance in structured report generation, IoC extraction, vulnerability assessment, malware configuration extraction, and malware class detection. In a real-world case study using MalwareBazaar samples, the grounded pipeline achieves a 10/10 structured analysis pass rate, producing CFG/FCG evidence, MITRE ATT&CK mappings, detection guidance, and analyst-ready reports. These results show that code-centric representations, retrieval grounding, and verification-guided reasoning improve the reliability and operational usefulness of LLM-assisted malware attribution.

</details>


### [222] [Heimdallr: Characterizing and Detecting LLM-Induced Security Risks in GitHub CI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05969)
*Bonan Ruan,Yeqi Fu,Chuqi Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Jun Zeng,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究GitHub CI工作流中LLM引入的安全风险，设计了混合分析框架Heimdallr，通过构建LLM-工作流属性图并联合触发分析、LLM辅助数据流摘要和确定性传播检测威胁向量，在300个标注工作流上实现高精度检测，并已负责任披露802个漏洞实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub CI工作流中越来越多地集成LLM来自动化代码审查、分类、内容生成和仓库维护，但LLM安全与CI安全的交叉领域尚未被充分研究，存在外部可控输入影响LLM提示和输出进而威胁安全决策、仓库状态或特权执行的新型攻击面。

Method: 提出Heimdallr混合分析框架，将工作流标准化为LLM-工作流属性图(L-WPG)，结合触发条件分析(triggerability analysis)、LLM辅助数据流摘要(LLM-assisted dataflow summarization)和确定性传播(deterministic propagation)来综合检测具体威胁向量。

Result: 在300个手动标注的工作流上评估，LLM节点识别F1=0.994，触发分类准确率99.8%，威胁向量检测微平均F1=0.917；已负责任披露802个漏洞工作流实例（涉及759个仓库），获得71个确认。

Conclusion: LLM在CI工作流中的集成带来了系统性安全风险，Heimdallr框架能有效检测此类威胁，且该问题在实际中广泛存在（发现802个漏洞实例），需要社区关注和防范。

Abstract: GitHub Continuous Integration (CI) workflows increasingly integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate review, triage, content generation, and repository maintenance. This creates a new attack surface: externally controllable workflow inputs can shape LLM prompts and outputs, which may in turn affect security decisions, repository state, or privileged execution. Although LLM security and CI security have each been studied extensively, their intersection remains underexplored. In this paper, we present the first study of LLM-induced security risks in GitHub CI workflows. We characterize the problem along the full execution chain and develop a taxonomy of high-level risk classes and concrete threat vectors. To detect such risks in practice, we design Heimdallr, a hybrid analysis framework that normalizes workflows into an LLM-Workflow Property Graph (L-WPG) and combines triggerability analysis, LLM-assisted dataflow summarization, and deterministic propagation to synthesize concrete threat-vector findings. Evaluated on 300 manually annotated unique workflows, Heimdallr achieves high accuracy on LLM-node identification (F1~=~0.994), triggerability classification (99.8%), and threat-vector detection (micro-average F1~=~0.917). As part of an ongoing detection and disclosure effort, we have so far responsibly disclosed 802 vulnerable workflow instances across 759 repositories and received 71 acknowledgments.

</details>


### [223] [LeakDojo: Decoding the Leakage Threats of RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05818)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LeakDojo是一个用于RAG系统泄漏风险评估的可配置框架。研究发现：查询生成和对抗性指令对泄漏的贡献相互独立，整体泄漏近似于两者的乘积；更强的指令遵循能力与更高的泄漏风险相关；RAG忠实度的提升可能引入更高的泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统使LLM能够利用外部知识，但同时也将宝贵的RAG数据库暴露于泄漏攻击之下。随着RAG系统日益复杂且LLM指令遵循能力增强，现有研究缺乏对RAG泄漏风险的系统性评估。

Method: 提出LeakDojo框架，一个用于可控评估RAG泄漏的配置化框架。利用该框架，在14个LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统上对6种现有攻击方法进行基准测试。

Result: (1) 查询生成和对抗性指令对泄漏的贡献相互独立，整体泄漏近似于两者的乘积；(2) 更强的指令遵循能力与更高的泄漏风险相关；(3) RAG忠实度的提升可能引入更高的泄漏风险。

Conclusion: 上述发现为实践中理解和缓解RAG泄漏提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to leverage external knowledge, but also exposes valuable RAG databases to leakage attacks. As RAG systems grow more complex and LLMs exhibit stronger instruction-following capabilities, existing studies fall short of systematically assessing RAG leakage risks. We present LeakDojo, a configurable framework for controlled evaluation of RAG leakage. Using LeakDojo, we benchmark six existing attacks across fourteen LLMs, four datasets, and diverse RAG systems. Our study reveals that (1) query generation and adversarial instructions contribute independently to leakage, with overall leakage well approximated by their product; (2) stronger instruction-following capability correlates with higher leakage risk; and (3) improvements in RAG faithfulness can introduce increased leakage risk. These findings provide actionable insights for understanding and mitigating RAG leakage in practice. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakDojo.

</details>


### [224] [LoopTrap: Termination Poisoning Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05846)
*Huiyu Xu,Zhibo Wang,Wenhui Zhang,Ziqi Zhu,Yaopeng Wang,Kui Ren,Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统定义了针对LLM Agent的"终止投毒"攻击，通过操控Agent的自我评估机制使其陷入无限执行循环。在此基础上，开发了自动化红队框架LoopTrap，通过行为画像和自适应策略合成，在8个主流Agent上平均实现3.57倍步数放大，最高达25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在执行循环中依赖自我评估判断任务是否完成，这种自我导向的终止机制存在安全漏洞——攻击者可通过注入恶意提示扭曲Agent的终止判断，使其认为任务始终未完成，导致无限计算消耗。现有研究对此威胁缺乏系统性定义和自动化攻防工具。

Method: 1) 定义Termination Poisoning攻击并设计10种代表性攻击策略；2) 对8种LLM Agent和60个任务进行实证研究，发现不同Agent的行为签名具有可迁移模式；3) 提出LoopTrap框架：先通过轻量级探测构建目标Agent在4个脆弱性维度的行为画像，再通过自适应陷阱合成选择最优策略和注入位置，最后通过自评分机制和自反思持续优化。

Result: 在8个主流Agent上，LoopTrap平均实现3.57倍步数放大，峰值达25倍。不同Agent表现出不同的行为签名，这些可迁移模式可用于对未见过的Agent和任务进行有效攻击。

Conclusion: Termination Poisoning是一种严重威胁LLM Agent安全性的新型攻击方式。LoopTrap作为自动化红队框架，能有效发现和利用Agent的终止判断漏洞，为规模化安全测试提供了可行性方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents solve complex tasks by operating in iterative execution loops, where they repeatedly reason, act, and self-evaluate progress to determine when a task is complete. In this work, we show that while this self-directed loop facilitates autonomy, it also introduces a critical risk: by injecting malicious prompts into the agent's context, an adversary can distort the agent's termination judgment, making it believe the task remains incomplete and leading to unbounded computation.To understand this threat, we define and systematically characterize it as Termination Poisoning and design 10 representative attack strategies. Through a empirical study spanning 8 LLM agents and 60 tasks, we demonstrate that different LLM agents exhibit distinct behavioral signatures that determine which strategies succeed. These transferable patterns can serve as principled guidance for crafting effective attacks against previously unseen agents and tasks, enabling scalable red-teaming beyond manually designed templates. Building on these insights, we introduce LoopTrap, an automated red-teaming framework that synthesizes target-specific malicious prompts by exploiting agent behavioral tendencies. LoopTrap first constructs a behavioral profile of the target agent along four vulnerability dimensions via lightweight probing. It then performs adaptive trap synthesis, routing to the most effective strategy and selecting optimal injections via a self-scoring mechanism. Finally, successful traps are abstracted into a reusable skill library, while failed attempts are refined through self-reflection, ensuring continuous improvement. Extensive evaluation shows that LoopTrap achieves an average of 3.57$\times$ step amplification across 8 mainstream agents, with a peak of 25$\times$.

</details>


### [225] [SkillScope: Toward Fine-Grained Least-Privilege Enforcement for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05868)
*Jiangrong Wu,Yuhong Nan,Yixi Lin,Huaijin Wang,Yuming Xiao,Shuai Wang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillScope是一个用于Agent Skill最小权限强制执行的框架，通过图分析方法建模指令级流程和代码级操作，检测并约束过度授权的行为，在检测任务中达到94.53% F1，在真实环境中验证了7,039个存在过度权限的Skill，并将触发过度权限操作的实例减少了88.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent Skill生态系统存在日益增长的合规风险：一个Skill可能执行超出用户当前任务最小必要范围的高影响力操作，从而违反最小权限原则。现有技能检测方法无法解决这一问题，因为该问题本质上是任务条件依赖的——同一操作在一个用户提示下可能是必要的，在另一个提示下则可能是过度授权的。

Method: SkillScope采用基于图的分析方法，将指令级流程和代码级操作建模为细粒度动作节点。首先提取潜在的过度权限候选，然后通过基于回放的分析在图实例化的用户任务下进行验证，最后通过控制流权限约束对验证为过度授权的操作进行限制。

Result: SkillScope在技能过度权限检测上达到94.53% F1。在真实环境中验证了7,039个存在过度授权行为的Skill，表明最小权限违反在当前Skill生态系统中普遍存在。在权限约束评估中，SkillScope将触发过度权限操作的任务内实例减少了88.56%，同时保留了合法的任务完成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SkillScope框架能够有效检测并约束Agent Skill中的过度权限问题，实验证明该方法在检测精度和权限约束效果上均表现优异，且不会影响合法任务的正常执行。

Abstract: Agent Skills have become a practical way to extend LLM agents by packaging metadata, natural-language instructions, and executable resources into reusable capability bundles. However, this growing Skill ecosystem introduces a new compliance risk: a Skill may perform high-impact actions that exceed the minimum necessary scope of the user's current task, thereby violating least-privilege. Existing skill detection approaches are insufficient for this problem because it is inherently task-conditioned: the same action may be necessary under one user prompt but over-privileged under another. In this paper, we present SkillScope, a framework for fine-grained least-privilege enforcement in Agent Skills. SkillScope adopts a graph-based analysis approach that models instruction-level procedures and code-level operations as fine-grained action nodes. It extracts potential over-privilege candidates, validates them under graph-instantiated user tasks through replay-based analysis, and constrains validated over-privileged actions via control-flow privilege constraining. We evaluate SkillScope through effectiveness experiments and large-scale real-world measurement. SkillScope achieves 94.53% F1 for skill over-privilege detection. In the wild, SkillScope validates 7,039 Skills with over-privileged behaviors, showing that least-privilege violations are prevalent in current Skill ecosystems. In the privilege-constraining evaluation, SkillScope reduces triggered over-privileged action-in-task instances by 88.56% while preserving legitimate task completion.

</details>


### [226] [PragLocker: Protecting Agent Intellectual Property in Untrusted Deployments via Non-Portable Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05974)
*Qinfeng Li,Yuntai Bao,Jianghui Hu,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Huifeng Zhu,Yier Jin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PragLocker是一种面向LLM智能体的提示保护方案，通过语义锚定和模型反馈噪声注入构建功能保持的混淆提示，有效降低跨模型可移植性，同时保持目标性能并抵御自适应攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖提示词实现基于基础LLM的任务特定能力，这些提示词是具有价值的知识产权。在不可信部署环境中， adversaries可以复制这些提示词并在其他专有LLM上复用，导致经济损失。现有方法无法满足主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性四个关键要求。

Method: PragLocker通过两个步骤构建功能保持的混淆提示：1) 使用代码符号锚定语义；2) 利用目标模型反馈注入噪声，生成仅能在目标LLM上正常工作的提示。

Result: 在多个智能体系统、数据集和基础LLM上的实验表明，PragLocker显著降低了跨LLM的可移植性，保持了目标性能，并且对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PragLocker成功解决了现有方法无法满足的四个关键挑战（主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性），为LLM智能体提示词保护提供了一种有效的方案。

Abstract: LLM agents rely on prompts to implement task-specific capabilities based on foundation LLMs, making agent prompts valuable intellectual property. However, in untrusted deployments, adversaries can copy and reuse these prompts with other proprietary LLMs, causing economic losses. To protect these prompts, we identify four key challenges: proactivity, runtime protection, usability, and non-portability that existing approaches fail to address. We present PragLocker, a prompt protection scheme that satisfies these requirements. PragLocker constructs function-preserving obfuscated prompts by anchoring semantics with code symbols and then using target-model feedback to inject noise, yielding prompts that only work on the target LLM. Experiments across multiple agent systems, datasets, and foundation LLMs show that PragLocker substantially reduces cross-LLM portability, maintains target performance, and remains robust against adaptive attackers.

</details>


### [227] [Safety Anchor: Defending Harmful Fine-tuning via Geometric Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05995)
*Guoxin Lu,Letian Sha,Qing Wang,Peijie Sun,Hao Zhou,Hua Dai,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR），通过将防御焦点从冗余参数空间转移到无嵌入层，利用几何瓶颈锚定有害查询的最终隐藏状态到安全对齐模型，从而有效抵御持续的有害微调攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（参数约束、梯度约束、内部表示约束）在持续有害微调下容易被绕过，原因是高维参数空间存在冗余性，攻击者可利用与防御约束正交的优化轨迹恢复有害能力。

Method: 提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR），将防御从冗余参数空间转移至无嵌入层这一几何瓶颈，通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的隐藏状态，使模型在持续有害微调下仍能保持安全响应。

Result: 仅使用单个安全锚点即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时在良性下游任务上保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: SBR通过聚焦几何瓶颈而非参数空间，有效解决了高维参数空间冗余性导致的防御失效问题，为LLM安全对齐提供了更鲁棒的防御方案。

Abstract: The safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains vulnerable to Harmful Fine-tuning (HFT). While existing defenses impose constraints on parameters, gradients, or internal representations, we observe that they can be effectively circumvented under persistent HFT. Our analysis traces this failure to the inherent redundancy of the high-dimensional parameter space: attackers exploit optimization trajectories that are orthogonal to defense constraints to restore harmful capabilities while deceptively adhering to safety restrictions. To address this, we propose Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR). SBR shifts the defensive focus from the redundant parameter space to the unembedding layer, which serves as a geometric bottleneck. By anchoring the final hidden states of harmful queries to those of the safety-aligned model, SBR enables the model to maintain safe responses even under persistent HFT. Extensive experiments confirm SBR's effectiveness, demonstrating that utilizing just a single safety anchor is sufficient to reduce the Harmful Score to $<$10 while preserving competitive performance on benign downstream tasks.

</details>


### [228] [Secure Seed-Based Multi-bit Watermarking for Diffusion Models from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06153)
*Enoal Gesny,Eva Giboulot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于理论分析的形式化评估框架，用于解耦生成模型与数字水印系统，并在此框架下设计了SSB水印方法，可在安全性、鲁棒性和保真度之间灵活权衡，无需依赖昂贵的实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式图像水印（尤其是种子嵌入方法）的评估高度依赖于特定模型架构，缺乏严格的安全性定义，导致无法对不同方法进行明确比较和结论性判断。作者主张水印方案的有效性应通过理论分析而非经验评估来确立。

Method: 通过将模型依赖部分与水印系统的实际决策机制解耦，构建了基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度三个维度的形式化评估框架。利用该框架的特征曲面实现不同水印系统间的精确比较。在此基础上提出SSB方法，推广了先前的种子嵌入方法，允许在特征曲面上达到任意安全性-鲁棒性-保真度组合。

Result: 提出了SSB水印方法，能够在特征曲面上灵活调整安全性、鲁棒性和保真度之间的权衡，且不依赖任何生成模型的实证评估。

Conclusion: 本研究为设计具有理论保证的现代水印系统开辟了新路径，此类系统无需代价高昂的实证评估即可提供可验证的性能保障。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of generative image models has led to the development of specialized watermarking techniques, particularly in-generation methods such as seed-based embedding. However, current evaluations in this area remain largely empirical, making them heavily reliant on the specific model architectures used for generation and inversion. This prevents any clear conclusion on the performance of any method, especially regarding security, for which a rigorous definition is lacking. Against this approach, we argue that the effectiveness of a watermarking scheme should be established purely through a thorough theoretical analysis. This is enabled by decoupling the model-dependent part from the actual decision mechanism of the watermarking system. Using this decoupling, we introduce a formal evaluation framework based on security, robustness, and fidelity. This allows precise comparisons between watermarking systems through a characteristic surface representing the trade-off between these three quantities, independent of any generative model. Based on this framework, we propose SSB, a novel watermarking method that generalizes previous seed-based methods by allowing to reach any security-robustness-fidelity regime on its characteristic surface. This work opens the door to the design of modern watermarking systems with theoretical guarantees that do not necessitate any costly empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [229] [Stateful Agent Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06158)
*Zhengchunmin Dai,Jiaxiong Tang,Liantao Wu,Peng Sun,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种跨会话、跨权限隔离的状态型智能体后门攻击方法，将攻击建模为Mealy机并实现分解式数据构建，在多个模型上达到80%-95%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大语言模型智能体的后门攻击是无状态的，攻击行为固定且局限于单一会话内，无法在权限隔离环境下跨会话持续执行。作者旨在突破这一限制，实现跨会话、可增量执行的状态型后门攻击。

Method: 将攻击建模为Mealy机（有限状态机），推导出分解框架使得每个状态转换的数据构建可独立进行。基于该框架实现了主攻击方案和两种可扩展变体（不同拓扑结构和持久化组件），利用持久化组件在会话间维护攻击状态。

Result: 主攻击在四个模型上达到80%-95%的攻擊成功率；逐状态转换分析验证了分解框架的有效性；两种可扩展变体也表现出一致的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了LLM智能体场景下的状态型后门攻击，突破了传统无状态后门的会话限制，为智能体安全研究提出了新的威胁模型。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks on Large Language Model-based agents remain stateless, executing fixed behaviors confined to a single session. We propose a stateful agent backdoor that extends the attack lifecycle across multiple sessions under permission isolation. The attack maintains state through persistent components, enabling autonomous, incremental execution across sessions following a one-time trigger injection. Formally, we model the attack as a Mealy machine and derive a decomposition framework that enables independent per-transition data construction. We instantiate this framework with a primary attack and two extensibility variants. The primary instantiation achieves an attack success rate of 80\%--95\% across four models, with per-transition analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of the decomposition. Extensibility variants with alternative topologies and persistent components demonstrate consistent effectiveness. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stateful_agent_backdoor-E89F.

</details>


### [230] [Profiling for Pennies: Unveiling the Privacy Iceberg of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06232)
*Jiahao Chen,Qi Zhang,Ruixiao Lin,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Qingming Li,Tong Zhang,Junhao Li,Yuwen Pu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM集成平台在隐私保护方面的严重不足：平台未能在技术或政策层面解决公众隐私担忧。作者提出PrivacyIceberg三级隐私风险分类体系，并开发IcebergExplorer工具，仅需最小PII作为种子，能在10分钟内以低于3美元的成本重建高保真用户画像（事实准确率超90%）。同时识别出导致隐私泄露的六大根本原因，并提出面向LLM供应商、个人和数据发布者的多利益相关方对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于LLM训练管线中通过记忆泄露PII，而从人类中心视角出发的隐私研究尚不充分。为填补这一空白，作者从人类认知和LLM集成平台实践的角度实证调查了现实世界中的隐私感知，揭示了平台未能解决公众隐私担忧的显著失调现象。

Method: 1) 实证调研人类对LLM集成平台的隐私感知与实践；2) 提出PrivacyIceberg三级分类体系（显式搜索、上下文推断、深度聚合）；3) 开发IcebergExplorer审计工具，以最小PII为种子重建用户画像；4) 识别六类隐私泄露根本原因。

Result: IcebergExplorer能在10分钟内以低于3美元的成本重建高保真用户画像，事实准确率超过90%。发现了六大隐私泄露根本原因，并提出多利益相关方对策。

Conclusion: LLM集成平台存在严重的隐私风险与实践脱节问题，需要LLM供应商、个人用户和数据发布者共同采取针对性措施以缓解隐私泄露。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized how information are collected, aggregated, and reasoned. However, this enables a novel and accessible vector of privacy intrusion: the automated and in-depth personal profiling; this engenders a chilling effect of "peepers everywhere". Existing research primarily unfolds from the training pipeline of LLM, emphasizing the exposure of Personally Identifiable Information (PII) through memorization, while privacy studies from a human-centric perspective remain underexplored. To fill this void, we empirically investigate privacy perception in the real world through the lens of human awareness and the practices of LLM-integrated platforms, revealing a significant dissonance: platforms fail to technically or policy-wise address public privacy concerns. To facilitate a systematic and quantifiable study of privacy risk, we propose the PrivacyIceberg, which categorizes real-world human privacy risks into three tiers: explicitly searched, contextually inferred, and deeply aggregated, based on the sophistication of LLM exploitation. We developed IcebergExplorer to audit privacy exposure, utilizing minimal PII as a search seed to reconstruct high-fidelity profiles, achieving over 90% factual accuracy within 10 minutes at a cost under $3, for real-world scenarios. Additionally, we identify six root causes contributing to such privacy disclosures and propose multi-stakeholder countermeasures for LLM vendors, individuals, and data publishers.

</details>


### [231] [Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Solution-Oriented Windows Event Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06330)
*Siraaj Akhtar,Saad Khan,Simon Parkinson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究微调后的小语言模型（SLM）在事件日志分析中相比大语言模型（LLM）能以更低计算成本更准确地识别问题并提供解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在事件日志分析中计算要求高、依赖云基础设施且存在安全顾虑，而现有方法大多仅识别问题不提供修复方案，因此需要一种轻量、本地化且能生成解决方案的替代方案。

Method: 首先使用高性能LLM创建大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集（包含修复操作），然后采用LoRA参数高效微调技术微调多个SLM和LLM，最后通过专家评估比较性能。

Result: 数据集准确反映真实场景；微调后的SLM在识别问题和提供相关修复方案方面持续优于LLM，且计算资源需求更低。

Conclusion: 针对特定任务微调的SLM可作为事件日志分析的实用替代方案，兼具高性能与低资源消耗的优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for event log analysis, but their high computational requirements, reliance on cloud infrastructure, and security concerns limit practical deployment. In addition, most existing approaches focus only on the identification of the problem and do not provide actionable remediation. Small language models (SLMs) present a light-weight alternative that can be fine-tuned for a specific purpose and hosted locally. This paper investigates whether SLMs, when fine-tuned for a specific task, can serve as a practical alternative for event log analysis while also generating solutions. We first create a large-scale synthetic Windows event log dataset that contains remediation actions using a high-performing LLM. We then fine-tune multiple SLMs and LLMs using the LoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique and evaluate their performance by comparing with expert assessment. The results show that the dataset accurately reflects real-world scenarios and that fine-tuned SLMs consistently outperform LLMs in identifying issues and providing relevant remediation, while requiring fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [232] [Pop Quiz Attack: Black-box Membership Inference Attacks Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06423)
*Zeyuan Chen,Yihan Ma,Xinyue Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PopQuiz Attack，一种针对大语言模型的黑盒成员推理攻击方法，通过将目标数据转化为选择题形式来测试模型是否记住了特定训练样本。在六个LLM和四个数据集上，该方法平均ROC-AUC达0.873，优于现有方法20.6%，并揭示了当前防御措施的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多种应用中表现优异，但其记忆并可能泄露训练数据的能力引发了严重的隐私担忧。现有成员推理攻击方法存在局限性，亟需更有效的攻击方法来评估和暴露LLM的隐私风险。

Method: 提出PopQuiz Attack黑盒成员推理攻击方法：将目标数据转化为多项选择题形式，通过分析模型对选择题的回答来判断该数据是否为训练集成员。在六个LLM（GPT-3.5、GPT-4o、LLaMA2-7b、LLaMA2-13b、Mistral-7b、Vicuna-7b）和四个数据集上进行评估。

Result: 平均ROC-AUC达到0.873，比现有方法提升20.6%。分析了查询复杂度、数据类型、数据结构和训练设置等因素对攻击成功率的影响。评估了基于指令、基于过滤和基于差分隐私的三种防御措施，发现这些防御虽能降低攻击性能但无法消除风险。

Conclusion: 现代大语言模型中仍然存在持续的隐私漏洞，现有防御措施不足以完全消除成员推理攻击的风险，需要更有效的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong performance across many applications, but their ability to memorize and potentially reveal training data raises serious privacy concerns. We introduce the PopQuiz Attack, a black-box membership inference attack that tests whether a model can recall specific training examples. The core idea is to turn target data into quiz-style multiple-choice questions and infer membership from the model's answers. Across six widely used LLMs (GPT-3.5, GPT-4o, LLaMA2-7b, LLaMA2-13b, Mistral-7b, and Vicuna-7b) and four datasets, our method achieves an average ROC-AUC of 0.873 and outperforms existing approaches by 20.6%. We further analyze factors affecting attack success, including query complexity, data type, data structure, and training settings. We also evaluate instruction-based, filter-based, and differential privacy-based defenses, which reduce performance but do not eliminate the risk. Our results highlight persistent privacy vulnerabilities in modern LLMs.

</details>


### [233] [Privacy by Postprocessing the Discrete Laplace Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06502)
*Quentin Hillebrand,Jacob Imola,Rasmus Pagh,Sia Sejer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 经典离散拉普拉斯机制可以通过后处理实现：(1) 对任意次指数函数f的无偏估计；(2) 输出与拉普拉斯或阶梯机制相同的分布。该机制适用于离散数据场景，优于拉普拉斯和阶梯机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明经典的离散拉普拉斯机制（几何机制）具有新的实用价值，特别是在无偏估计方面，克服传统拉普拉斯机制只能提供有偏估计的局限。

Method: 通过后处理技术，将离散拉普拉斯机制的输出转换为任意次指数函数的无偏估计量；同时证明该机制可后处理生成与拉普拉斯机制或阶梯机制相同分布的输出。

Result: 无偏估计量在最坏情况下指数时间复杂度，但对具有结构性的常见函数可实现线性或多项式时间计算。在剖面估计、熵估计及分布式/联邦数据分析等任务上进行了实证验证。

Conclusion: 离散拉普拉斯机制在多方面优于连续拉普拉斯和阶梯机制，尤其适用于离散数据或可通过ℓ₁敏感度控制实现离散化的场景。

Abstract: We show that an "old dog", the classical discrete Laplace (aka.~geometric) mechanism, can "perform new tricks":
  1. It can be post-processed to yield a simple, unbiased estimator of any subexponential function $f$ of the original data, giving a simple, discrete, multivariate version of the recent unbiasing result for the Laplace mechanism by Calmon et al. (FORC '25).
  2. It can be post-processed to output the same distribution as the Laplace mechanism or the Staircase mechanism with identical privacy parameters.
  Thus, the discrete Laplace mechanism is a versatile mechanism that should be preferred over the Laplace and Staircase mechanisms whenever the data is discrete (or can be made discrete while controlling $\ell_1$-sensitivity).
  We show bounds on the variance of our estimator, compared to the mean square error of the biased estimator that simply evaluates the $f$ on the output of the mechanism. Though our unbiased estimator has exponential running time for worst-case functions, we show that it can often be computed in linear or polynomial time for some common functions exhibiting structure. We showcase the properties of our methods empirically with several use cases including profile and entropy estimation, as well as distributed/federated data analysis applications in which unbiasedness is key to accuracy.

</details>


### [234] [On the Security of Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06508)
*Nanda Rani,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究分析了509个顶级安全会议的研究工件，发现41.60%的工件存在安全风险。作者提出了上下文感知安全评估分类法，并开发了SAFE框架，该框架通过分析代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性，以84.80%的准确率和84.63%的F1分数区分安全与非安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工件评估（AE）主要关注可重复性和功能正确性，忽视了工件中潜在的安全风险。由于研究工件被公开共享和复用，可能无意中引入攻击向量，因此需要系统化的安全评估方法。

Method: 收集509个来自顶级安全会议的研究工件；提出上下文感知安全评估分类法；进行静态分析，过滤误报并识别真实安全风险；开发SAFE框架，结合代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性进行自动化分析。

Result: 41.60%的常见发现存在实际使用中的安全风险；SAFE框架达到84.80%准确率和84.63% F1分数。

Conclusion: 安全性应成为工件评估的重要组成部分，以促进安全且负责任的研究共享。SAFE框架可作为迈向自动化安全评估的第一步。

Abstract: Research artifacts are widely shared to support reproducibility, and artifact evaluation (AE) has become common at many leading conferences. However, AE mainly checks whether artifacts work as claimed and can be reproduced. It largely overlooks potential security risks. Since these artifacts are publicly released and reused, they may unintentionally create opportunities for misuse and raise concerns about safe and responsible sharing. We study 509 research artifacts from top-tier security venues and find that many contain insecure code patterns that may introduce potential attack vectors. We propose a taxonomy for context-aware security assessment to enable structured analysis of such risks. We perform static analysis and examine the resulting findings, filtering false positives and identifying real security risks. Our analysis shows that 41.60% of the prevalent findings may pose security concerns under practical usage. To support scalable analysis, we introduce SAFE (Security-Aware Framework for Artifact Evaluation), a first step toward an autonomous framework that analyzes tool-reported findings by considering code semantics, execution context, and practical exploitability. SAFE achieves 84.80% accuracy and 84.63% F1-score in distinguishing security and non-security risks. Overall, our results show that security is also important in AE for promoting safe and responsible research sharing. The source code is available at: https://github.com/nanda-rani/SAFE

</details>


### [235] [FedAttr: Towards Privacy-preserving Client-Level Attribution in Federated LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06596)
*Su Zhang,Junfeng Guo,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAttr，一种用于联邦学习中客户端级别水印归因的新协议，通过配对子集差分机制在保护安全聚合隐私的同时，准确识别哪些客户端使用了水印数据训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印放射性检测方法在集中式LLM微调中有效，但在联邦学习场景下面临挑战：安全聚合机制保护了客户端更新的隐私，使得难以识别哪个客户端使用了水印文档进行训练。

Method: FedAttr包含三步：(i) 通过差分两个安全聚合查询来估计每个客户端的更新；(ii) 使用差分评分方法将估计结果与 watermark 检测器进行评分；(iii) 通过Stouffer方法跨轮次组合评分。理论上证明了估计的无偏性以及每轮更新互信息泄漏的有界性(O(d^*/N))。

Result: FedAttr实现了100%的TPR和0%的FPR，在TPR或FPR上至少优于所有基线44.4%或19.1%，且仅增加6.3%的训练时间开销。消融实验证实其对协议参数和配置具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedAttr成功将水印放射性检测方法扩展到联邦学习场景，在保护安全聚合隐私的同时实现了高效准确的客户端级归因，为FL中的数据所有权保护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Watermark radioactivity testing type of methods can detect whether a model was trained on watermarked documents, and have become key tools for protecting data ownership in the fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing works have proved their effectiveness in centralized LLM fine-tuning. However, this type of method faces several challenges and remains underexplored in federated learning (FL), a widely-applied paradigm for fine-tuning LLMs collaboratively on private data across different users. FL mainly ensures privacy through secure aggregation (SA), which allows the server to aggregate updates while keeping clients' updates private. This mechanism preserves privacy but makes it difficult to identify which client trained on watermarked documents. In this work, we propose FedAttr, a new client-level attribution protocol for FL. FedAttr identifies which clients trained on watermarked data via a paired-subset-difference mechanism, while preserving the privacy guarantees of SA and FL performance. FedAttr proceeds in three steps: (i) estimate each client's update by differencing two SA queries, (ii) score the estimate with the watermark detector via differential scoring, and (iii) combine scores across rounds via Stouffer method. We theoretically show that FedAttr produces an unbiased estimator of each client's update with bounded mutual information leakage (i.e., $O(d^*/N)$ per-round update). Moreover, FedAttr empirically achieves 100% TPR and 0% FPR, outperforming all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead relative to FL training time. Ablation studies confirm that FedAttr is robust to protocol parameters and configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Direct From Darwin: Deriving Advanced Optimizers From Evolutionary First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05284)
*Daniel Grimmer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文从进化第一原理推导出一系列高级梯度优化算法，通过达尔文谱系模拟（DLS）证明了Fisher和Wright对立观点的形式等价性，并表明SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法乃至Adam等优化器在添加适当的DLS噪声后均可成为符合达尔文进化论的科学模拟工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化计算长期承诺兼具高性能优化工具与达尔文进化科学模拟的双重目标，但现代算法大多放弃了进化保真度，转而采用受物理启发的启发式方法或肤浅的生物学隐喻。本文旨在从进化第一原理重新建立优化算法与进化动力学之间的严格联系。

Method: 提出达尔文谱系模拟（DLS）框架，通过将Fisher确定性演化的总人口划分为Wright随机漂变的子种群，引入DLS噪声关系（一种结构化噪声）来实现两种历史对立观点的形式统一。利用该关系的表征自由度，证明一系列梯度优化算法（SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法、Adam等）在添加DLS噪声后与进化动力学完全兼容。

Result: 证明了Fisher和Wright的进化观点在无性繁殖背景下是形式等价的；表明SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法等优化算法本质上已与进化动力学兼容；Adam优化器可通过微小数学修正实现进化合规。

Conclusion: 通过DLS框架，主流的梯度优化算法可以被重新诠释为符合达尔文进化论的科学模拟工具，为进化计算与优化算法之间建立了严格的理论桥梁。

Abstract: Evolutionary computation has long promised to deliver both high-performance optimization tools as well as rigorous scientific simulations of Darwinian evolution. However, modern algorithms frequently abandon evolutionary fidelity for physics-inspired heuristics or superficial biological metaphors. This paper derives a suite of advanced gradient-based optimization algorithms directly from evolutionary first principles. We introduce Darwinian Lineage Simulations (DLS) to prove that, in an asexual context, Fisher's and Wright's historically opposed views of evolution are actually formally equivalent. This unification requires carefully partitioning Fisher's deterministically-evolving total population into Wright's randomly-drifting sub-populations. We prove that proper bookkeeping requires introducing a specific kind of structured noise (the DLS noise relation). Crucially, however, any bookkeeping choices which satisfy this relation will result in a faithful simulation of evolution. Using this vast representational freedom, we prove that a broad family of battle-tested optimization algorithms are already perfectly compatible with evolutionary dynamics. These include: Stochastic Gradient Descent, Natural Gradient Descent, and the Damped Newton's method among many others. By simply adding DLS noise (i.e., evolutionarily faithful genetic drift), these algorithms become scientifically valid in silico simulations of Darwinian evolution. Finally, we demonstrate that even the state-of-the-art Adam optimizer can be brought into evolutionary compliance through a minor mathematical surgery.

</details>


### [237] [CoupleEvo: Evolving Heuristics for Coupled Optimization Problems Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06341)
*Thomas Bömer,Bastian Amberg,Max Disselnmeyer,Anne Meyer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: CoupleEvo提出了三种进化协调策略（顺序、迭代、集成），用于大语言模型驱动的耦合优化问题自动启发式设计。实验表明分解策略（顺序和迭代）收敛更稳定、解质量更高，而集成策略面临搜索复杂性和可变性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多优化问题由多个紧密耦合的子问题组成，需要协调求解才能达到高整体性能，但现有的大语言模型驱动的自动启发式设计方法局限于单问题场景。

Method: 提出CoupleEvo框架，包含三种进化协调策略：(1) 顺序策略——依次为每个子问题进化启发式；(2) 迭代策略——在不同子问题之间交替进行逐代启发式进化；(3) 集成策略——同时为所有子问题进化启发式。

Result: 在两个代表性耦合优化问题上的实验结果表明，基于分解的策略（顺序和迭代）提供更稳定的收敛性和更高的解质量，而集成进化策略则面临搜索复杂性和可变性增加的问题。

Conclusion: 强调了在相互依赖的子问题之间协调进化搜索的重要性，并展示了大语言模型驱动的启发式设计在复杂耦合优化问题中的潜力。

Abstract: Many real-world optimization problems consist of multiple tightly coupled subproblems whose solutions must be coordinated to achieve high overall performance. However, existing large language model driven automated heuristic design approaches are limited to single-problem settings. In this paper, we propose CoupleEvo. CoupleEvo proposes three evolutionary coordination strategies to evolve heuristics for coupled optimization problems: the sequential strategy evolves heuristics for one subproblem after the other; the iterative strategy alternates the evolution of heuristics for different subproblems over successive generations; and the integrated strategy evolves heuristics for all problems simultaneously. The approach is evaluated on two representative coupled optimization problems. Experimental results show that decomposition-based strategies (sequential and iterative) provide more stable convergence and higher solution quality, while the integrated evolution strategy suffers from increased search complexity and variability. These findings highlight the importance of coordinating evolutionary search across interdependent subproblems and demonstrate the potential of LLM-driven heuristic design for complex coupled optimization problems. The code is available: https://github.com/tb-git-kit-research/CoupleEvo.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [DADL: A Declarative Description Language for Enterprise Tool Libraries in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05247)
*Axel Dunkel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DADL是一种声明式YAML格式，用于统一描述REST API的端点、认证、分页、响应整形和访问分类。它通过单一运行时解释执行，无需为每个API部署独立服务器进程，并采用固定大小的Code Mode接口将工具目录呈现给LLM，将上下文消耗从约142,000 tokens降至约1,000 tokens（142倍压缩）。该规范以CC BY-SA 4.0发布，公共注册表包含1,833个工具定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP（模型上下文协议）在组织规模下存在两个结构性问题：1）每个API集成需作为独立服务器进程部署，带来各自的部署、依赖树和凭据管理负担，且大多数只是REST API的薄包装；2）每个工具单独注册导致上下文窗口消耗随工具目录规模线性增长，实际部署只能暴露组织实际使用API的一小部分。

Method: 提出DADL（Dunkel API描述语言），一种YAML格式，以单一声明式文件描述REST API的端点、认证、分页、响应整形和访问分类。运行时解释执行DADL文件，无需生成集成代码或部署每个API的独立服务器进程。所有工具共享同一运行时，凭据和授权集中管理。通过固定大小的Code Mode接口将工具目录呈现给LLM，该接口大小与目录规模无关。

Result: 在包含20个服务、1,833个工具定义的公共注册表上，Code Mode将工具广告的上下文成本从约142,000 tokens降至约1,000 tokens，实现了142倍的压缩。每次搜索和执行调用的成本额外计算，取决于具体任务。

Conclusion: DADL实现了企业级工具库：一个版本化、可审计的API集成集合，任何团队均可通过单一认证和授权边界扩展、共享和消费。该方案有效解决了MCP在组织规模下的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is the standard interface between large language model (LLM) agents and external tools. At organizational scale, however, it exposes two structural problems. First, every API integration is shipped as a dedicated server process with its own deployment, dependency tree, and credential handling; recent empirical work shows the overwhelming majority of these servers are thin wrappers around REST APIs. Second, the per-tool registration model causes context window consumption to grow linearly with catalog size, forcing real deployments to expose only a small fraction of the APIs an organization actually uses.
  We present DADL (Dunkel API Description Language), a YAML format describing a REST API's endpoints, authentication, pagination, response shaping, and access classification in a single declarative file. A DADL file is interpreted by an execution layer at runtime; no per-API server process is deployed and no integration code is generated, though the runtime is itself a server. Because all tools share that runtime, credentials and authorization are managed centrally, and the catalog reaches the LLM through a fixed-size Code Mode interface independent of size. The result is an Enterprise Tool Library: a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations any team can extend, share, and consume through one authentication and authorization boundary.
  The DADL v0.1 specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0, and a public registry contains 1,833 tool definitions across 20 services. On this catalog, Code Mode reduces the context cost of tool advertisement from approximately 142,000 tokens to approximately 1,000, a 142x reduction; the per-call cost of search and execute invocations is additional and depends on the task.

</details>


### [239] [Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05252)
*Santosh Vasudevan,Velu Natarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI文档智能的自动化审计交易测试框架，利用Snowflake Document AI从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据，并与权威数据源进行大规模对账，实现了从抽样测试到全量测试的转变，提升了审计覆盖率和持续鉴证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手工、基于抽样的PDF对账单审核方式劳动密集、效率低下，无法应对数百万级交易规模的审计需求，亟需一种可扩展的自动化审计交易测试方法。

Method: 采用Snowflake Document AI，仅需约20份文档的小规模标注语料即可训练模型，从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据，然后将提取数据与权威源系统数据集进行自动化对账，通过交互式仪表盘和自动化报告呈现差异结果。

Result: 实现了从抽样测试到总体级（population-level）测试的转变，显著提升了审计覆盖率；支持持续鉴证目标，能够进行大规模、近实时的风险识别。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了基于AI文档智能的自动化审计交易测试在提升审计效率和覆盖范围方面的有效性，为现代审计实践中的连续鉴证和近实时风险识别提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Audit transaction testing validates accuracy and completeness of customer-facing statements against internal systems of record. Traditional manual, sample-based review of unstructured PDF statements is labor-intensive and does not scale to millions of transactions. This paper presents an automated framework for large-scale audit transaction testing using AI-based document intelligence. The solution leverages Snowflake Document AI to extract structured data from unstructured PDF statements using a small labeled corpus (approximately 20 documents). Extracted data are reconciled against authoritative source-of-truth datasets to identify discrepancies at scale. Results are surfaced through interactive dashboards and automated reports. The framework enables population-level testing rather than sampling-based approaches, improving audit coverage and supporting continuous assurance objectives. Recent advances in document intelligence and analytics-driven audit frameworks enable scalable, near real-time risk identification and continuous assurance.

</details>


### [240] [Teaching LLMs Program Semantics via Symbolic Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06184)
*Jonas Bayer,Stefan Zetzsche,Olivier Bouissou,Remi Delmas,Michael Tautschnig,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于SV-COMP 2025的500个C语言验证任务评估框架，覆盖五种属性类型。评估发现模型在确认属性成立时表现良好，但违规检测能力差异大且随程序长度下降。通过在开源C代码上运行Soteria符号执行引擎生成验证迹，并对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练，仅用约3000条bug迹结合推理时思维链，将违规检测提升17个百分点以上，使8B模型在违规检测上超越4倍大的Qwen3-32B。迹训练与思维链的协同效应是超加性的，且提升跨所有属性类型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有C语言验证模型在总体准确率上表现尚可，但在违规检测方面存在严重缺陷，尤其是对较长程序的检测能力急剧下降。为了弥补这一差距，作者探索利用形式化验证迹（符号执行引擎生成的迹）进行训练，以提升模型的违规检测能力。

Method: 构建基于SV-COMP 2025的500个C验证任务评估框架，涵盖内存安全、溢出、终止、可达性和数据竞争五种属性。在通用开源C代码上运行Soteria符号执行引擎生成验证迹，用于对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练。采用约3000条bug迹结合推理时思维链推理策略，并设置28种配置进行消融实验。

Result: 约3000条bug迹结合思维链将违规检测提升17个百分点以上；训练的8B模型在违规检测上超越Qwen3-32B（4倍大小），整体准确率接近；迹训练与思维链的组合呈超加性效应；提升跨所有五种属性类型泛化，包括训练迹未覆盖的类型。

Conclusion: 迹训练与思维链的协同组合能有效提升C语言验证模型的违规检测能力，且其提升源于迹的语义而非代码量，迹的整理和格式对效果至关重要。

Abstract: We introduce an evaluation framework of 500 C verification tasks across five property types (memory safety, overflow, termination, reachability, data races) built on SV-COMP 2025, and evaluate 14 models across six families. We find that high overall accuracy masks a critical weakness: while most models reliably confirm properties hold, violation detection varies widely and degrades sharply with program length. To close this gap, we train on formal verification artifacts: running the Soteria symbolic execution engine on generic open-source C code and using the resulting traces for continued pretraining of Qwen3-8B. Just ${\sim}$3,000 bug traces combined with chain-of-thought reasoning at inference time improve violation detection by over 17 percentage points, producing one of the most balanced accuracy profiles among evaluated models. On violation detection, the trained 8B model outperforms the 4$\times$ larger Qwen3-32B without thinking and approaches it in overall accuracy. The interaction between trace training and chain-of-thought is superadditive: neither alone provides meaningful gains, but their combination does. Improvements transfer across all five property types, including ones the training traces do not target. Our 28 configurations confirm the gains stem from trace semantics, not code volume, and that trace curation and format matter.

</details>


### [241] [Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05267)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Peiliang Cai,Mingwei Liu,Yanlin Wang,Chong Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对114篇主要研究的系统性文献综述，建立了训练数据质量问题与代码生成缺陷之间的因果映射框架，提出了涵盖9个维度的生成代码质量问题和代码/非代码属性的训练数据质量问题分类体系，并总结了18种典型的传播映射机制及相应的检测与缓解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成任务中频繁产生从逻辑缺陷到安全漏洞的各种输出问题，虽然这些问题常被视为模型层面的限制，但实证证据表明其根源可追溯至训练语料库中的缺陷。然而，训练数据质量问题与生成代码质量问题之间的具体传播机制尚未得到系统映射，缺乏统一的因果理解框架。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，筛选并分析了114篇主要研究；建立了统一的分类体系，将生成代码质量问题分为9个维度，训练数据质量问题分为代码属性和非代码属性；基于此形式化了包含18种典型传播映射机制的因果框架；综述了数据、模型和生成生命周期中的检测与缓解技术。

Result: 揭示了质量保障正在从反应式、基于启发式的生成后过滤转向主动式、以数据为中心的治理和闭环修复的方法论转变；建立了训练数据质量与代码生成质量之间的因果映射关系；提供了18种传播机制的系统化分类。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战，提出了通过集成数据策展和持续评估来开发可靠代码生成LLM的研究方向，强调数据质量治理在提升LLM代码生成可靠性中的核心作用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate defective outputs in code generation tasks, ranging from logical bugs to security vulnerabilities. While these generation failures are often treated as model-level limitations, empirical evidence increasingly traces their root causes to imperfections within the training corpora. Yet, the specific mechanisms linking training data quality issues to generated code quality issues remain largely unmapped. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 114 primary studies to investigate how training data quality issues propagate into code generation. We establish a unified taxonomy that categorizes generated code quality issues across nine dimensions and training data quality issues into code and non-code attributes. Based on this taxonomy, we formalize a causal framework detailing 18 typical propagation mapping mechanisms. Furthermore, we synthesize state-of-the-art detection and mitigation techniques across the data, model, and generation lifecycles. The reviewed literature reveals a clear methodological shift: quality assurance is transitioning from reactive, heuristic-based post-generation filtering toward proactive, data-centric governance and closed-loop repair. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline research directions for developing reliable LLMs for code through integrated data curation and continuous evaluation. Our repository is available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code.

</details>


### [242] [Is this Build Failure Related to my Patch? An Empirical Study of Unrelated Build Failures in Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05564)
*Andie Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Grant Dick,Mariam El Mezouar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了来自7个Apache开源项目的77,354个CI构建失败案例，发现开发者平均花费4小时判断失败是否与当前代码推送相关。通过对371个确认无关的构建失败进行文档分析，发现20%的无关失败源于无关测试失败。作者提取了33个特征，采用半监督正无标签学习(PU learning)进行预测，在7个Apache项目上取得了0.70-0.88的精度、0.30-1.00的召回率、0.44-0.91的F1分数和0.63-0.97的AUC。CI延迟、重复错误消息和前置评论数量是无关构建失败的重要指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI系统中大量并发构建常导致构建失败与触发推送无关，开发者需要花费大量时间判断失败是否与当前更改相关，造成了开发效率的浪费。

Method: 从77,354个构建失败中采样分析，提取33个特征（包括issue报告、评论和提交信息），使用半监督正无标签学习(PU learning)模型来预测无关构建失败。

Result: PU学习模型在7个Apache项目上达到0.70-0.88的精度、0.30-1.00的召回率、0.44-0.91的F1分数和0.63-0.97的AUC。CI延迟、重复错误消息和前置评论数量是关键预测特征。

Conclusion: PU学习可以有效帮助开发者识别与当前推送无关的构建失败，减少不必要的调试时间。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) systems often run many builds concurrently. In this setting, a legitimate build failure may not be caused by the code push that triggered it. Such unrelated build failures can waste developer effort because developers must determine whether the failure is actionable for their current change. We study 77,354 CI build failures from seven open source Apache projects to understand and predict unrelated build failures. We find that developers spend a median of 4 hours identifying whether a failure is related or unrelated to their push. We also perform a document analysis of 371 confirmed unrelated build failures sampled from 10,316 potentially unrelated failures. The analysis shows that unrelated test failures account for 20% of the cases in which developers classify build failures as unrelated. To predict unrelated build failures, we extract 33 features from issue reports, issue comments, and commits associated with the triggering push. We build semi-supervised Positive and Unlabeled (PU) learning models for seven Apache projects. The models achieve precision from 0.70 to 0.88, recall from 0.30 to 1.00, F1-score from 0.44 to 0.91, and AUC from 0.63 to 0.97. Feature importance analysis shows that CI latency, repeated error messages, and the number of preceding comments are useful indicators of unrelated build failures. These results show that PU learning can help developers identify build failures that are unlikely to be caused by their current push.

</details>


### [243] [An Empirical Study of Proactive Coding Assistants in Real-World Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05700)
*Lehui Li,Ruixuan Jia,Guo-Ye Yang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过收集1,246名专业开发者在三天内真实的IDE交互数据，并与大模型模拟的IDE轨迹进行对比，发现模拟数据与真实数据在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式上存在显著差异。基于真实数据构建的ProCodeBench基准测试显示，当前方法在真实IDE轨迹下表现远不如模拟环境，模拟数据无法替代真实数据，但可作为微调前的补充数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的编码助手多为被动响应式，缺乏主动推断开发者意图的能力。该方向受限于缺乏大规模真实开发者行为数据，现有研究多依赖LLM模拟的IDE轨迹，但其与真实开发行为的相似度尚不明确。

Method: 使用自定义VS Code扩展收集1,246名行业开发者在连续三天内的真实IDE交互轨迹，同时构建配对的LLM模拟轨迹，从行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式三个维度进行对比分析。基于真实数据构建ProCodeBench基准测试，评估多种LLM、检索增强方法和智能体基线。最后进行训练实验，探究模拟数据与真实数据的替代与互补关系。

Result: 模拟轨迹与真实轨迹在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式上存在显著差异；当前方法在真实IDE轨迹下的表现远不如模拟环境中的评估结果，基于模拟的评估会高估实际性能；模拟数据无法替代真实数据，但可在真实数据微调前作为补充数据使用。

Conclusion: 真实开发者行为数据对评估和训练主动式编码助手至关重要，模拟数据不能替代真实数据但可起补充作用。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding assistants have made substantial progress, yet most systems remain reactive, requiring developers to explicitly formulate their needs. Proactive coding assistants aim to infer latent developer intent from integrated development environment (IDE) interactions and repository context, thereby reducing interaction overhead and supporting more seamless assistance. However, research in this direction is limited by the scarcity of large-scale real-world developer behavior data. Existing studies therefore often rely on LLM-simulated IDE traces, whose fidelity to real development behavior remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate this simulation-to-reality gap through a large-scale empirical study. We collect real IDE interaction traces from 1{,}246 experienced industry developers over three consecutive days using a custom Visual Studio Code extension, and construct paired LLM-simulated traces for controlled comparison. Our analysis shows that simulated traces differ substantially from real traces in behavioral diversity, temporal structure, and exploratory patterns. Based on the collected data, we introduce \textbf{ProCodeBench}, a real-world benchmark for proactive intent prediction. Experiments with representative LLMs, retrieval-augmented methods, and agentic baselines show that current approaches remain far from reliable under real IDE traces, suggesting that simulation-based evaluation can overestimate real-world performance. Finally, our training study shows that simulated data cannot replace real data, but can complement it when used before real-world fine-tuning. These findings highlight the importance of real developer behavior data for evaluating and training proactive coding assistants.

</details>


### [244] [From Chat to Interview: Agentic Requirements Elicitation with an Experience Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05828)
*Dongming Jin,Zhi Jin,Yaotian Yang,Linyu Li,Zheng Fang,Yuanpeng He,Wenchun Jing,Xiaohong Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为OntoAgent的需求访谈智能体，通过自动构建经验本体来引导需求获取，在网站应用领域的实验中，相比现有方法在IRE指标上提升33%，在TKQR指标上提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求获取访谈过度依赖分析师经验，且基于LLM的自动化方法仅进行自由对话，忽略了隐含需求和访谈经验，导致需求遗漏和冗余提问。

Method: OntoAgent首先自动分析领域特定需求描述构建经验本体，将需求关注点组织为本体结构；访谈过程中通过ParseUser、ScoreOnto、ReRankOnto、GatePrune四个操作识别相关需求关注点，再结合当前对话上下文生成启发式问题。

Result: 在网站应用领域的定量实验中，OntoAgent在IRE指标上提升33%，在TKQR指标上提升21%；消融实验验证了各关键组件的贡献；定性用户研究证明了其在真实场景中的实用优势。

Conclusion: OntoAgent通过经验本体引导的结构化访谈显著提升了需求获取的效果和效率，并可推广至其他领域的需求访谈任务。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews are crucial and time-consuming in requirements engineering, but heavily rely on the experience of requirements analysts. Although recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to automate this process, existing approaches rely solely on LLMs for free-form chat without taking into account the interview and development experience. That leads to the omission of implicit requirements and redundant questions. Practically, experienced analysts implicitly follow a structured cognitive framework when conducting requirements elicitation. Inspired by this observation, this paper proposes an interview agent named OntoAgent for the elicitation of requirements guided by an experience ontology. OntoAgent automatically analyzes domain-specific requirements descriptions to construct an experience ontology, which organizes requirements concerns into an ontology to support systematic and explainable interviews. During the interview, OntoAgent first performs four operations (i.e., ParseUser, ScoreOnto, ReRankOnto, GatePrune) guided by the ontology to identify the relevant requirement concerns. The selected concern is then combined with the current dialogue context to generate the elicitation question. To validate OntoAgent, we conduct comprehensive quantitative experiments using the widely adopted website application domain. The results show that OntoAgent significantly outperforms existing baselines in both elicitation effectiveness and questioning efficiency, achieving a 33% improvement in IRE and a 21% improvement in TKQR. Ablation studies further validate the contribution of each key design component. In addition, a qualitative user study demonstrates its practical advantages in real-world scenarios. We believe that OntoAgent can also be extended to requirements interview tasks in other domains.

</details>


### [245] [On Fixing Insecure AI-Generated Code through Model Fine-Tuning and Prompting Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05867)
*Ali Soltanian Fard Jahromi,Amjed Tahir,Peng Liang,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了提升AI生成代码安全性的策略（包括微调和提示工程），发现安全改进高度依赖于策略和模型，且修复漏洞时常引入新的弱点，不存在普适有效的"防弹"解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成代码存在严重安全漏洞，现有缓解策略效果不一，缺乏系统性的跨模型、跨语言评估，且对修复副作用（引入新弱点）的研究不足。

Method: 基于Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE)列表，采用微调和提示工程两种策略，对多个模型和编程语言进行系统实验，分析安全弱点的严重性、共现性以及修复的副作用。

Result: 安全改进高度依赖具体策略和模型；某些方法虽能减少特定类型弱点，但常引入新的弱点作为副作用；没有任何策略能在所有模型和场景下一致地消除弱点。

Conclusion: 不存在普适有效的"防弹"解决方案来保证AI生成代码的安全性，修复过程本身可能引入新风险，需要更谨慎的评估和多元化的安全策略。

Abstract: The security of AI-generated code remains a major obstacle to its widespread adoption. Although code generation models achieve strong performance on functional benchmarks, their outputs frequently contain bugs and security weaknesses that undermine their trustworthiness. Prior work has explored a range of approaches to mitigate security issues in AI-generated code, e.g., using static analysis-guided generation and prompt engineering. However, their effectiveness varies widely across models and settings. This paper presents a systematic investigation of strategies for hardening model-generated code against a list of Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). We assess the extent to which these strategies improve security across models and programming languages, using fine-tuning and prompting approaches for model output refinement. Beyond the prevalence of security weaknesses, we analyse the severity of identified CWEs, their co-occurrence, and the unintended consequences of remediation (i.e., whether fixing certain weaknesses introduces new weaknesses elsewhere in the same code). Our results show that security improvements are highly strategy- and model-dependent. Although some approaches reduce specific classes of weaknesses, they often introduce new weaknesses as side effects of the fixes. Moreover, no strategy consistently eliminates weaknesses across all models and scenarios, highlighting the absence of a universally effective "bulletproof" solution for secure AI-generated code.

</details>


### [246] [Evaluating Non-English Developer Support in Machine Learning for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05902)
*Jonathan Katzy,Yongcheng Huang,Gopal-Raj Panchu,Maksym Ziemlewski,Paris Loizides,Sander Vermeulen,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言代码注释生成中，大语言模型在非英语场景下的性能严重下降，以及现有自动评估方法（包括神经指标和LLM作为评判者）在检测非英语注释错误时不可靠，最终得出结论：人类判断在多语言代码工具中仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成与评估主要基于英语，缺乏对多语言开发场景（代码中包含非英语自然语言）的系统性研究，无法了解当前工具在非英语环境中的真实表现。

Method: 评估5个代码LLM（CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, StarCoder2）在5种自然语言（荷兰语、英语、希腊语、波兰语、中文）上的注释生成能力；对12,500个生成注释进行开放编码研究，构建人工标注数据集和包含26种错误类型的分类体系；利用人工标注评估神经指标和LLM-as-a-judge管线的性能。

Result: 非英语场景下生成性能显著下降，语言错误最高增加15.1倍，频繁出现不连贯生成和语义错误；所有自动评估方法（重叠指标、现成神经指标、多语言/语言特定/代码特定扩展模型、LLM-as-a-judge）都无法提供可靠一致的评估，神经指标无法区分正确/错误/随机噪声输出，LLM-as-a-judge虽然与人类一致性最高但无法可靠捕获语言相关和语义错误。

Conclusion: 评估和生成是多语言代码工具面临的关键障碍，人类判断在多语言代码开发中仍然不可替代。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used in software engineering, but both code generation and its evaluation remain predominantly English-centric. This leaves a major gap in our understanding of how well current tools support multilingual development, where code contains non-English natural language. In this paper, we investigate non-English code comment generation and the reliability of current methods for evaluating such outputs. We evaluate five code LLMs (CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, and StarCoder2) across five natural languages: Dutch, English, Greek, Polish and Chinese. We further conduct an open-coding study of 12,500 generated comments, from which we derive a publicly released human-annotated dataset and a taxonomy of 26 error types. We use these human annotations, to evaluate the performance of neural metrics, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.
  Our findings show that generative performance deteriorates substantially outside English, with linguistic errors increasing by up to 15.1$\times$, alongside frequent incoherent generations and a rise in semantic errors. More critically, we show that detecting errors in non-English comments underperforms. Across classical overlap-based metrics, off-the-shelf neural metrics, extended neural metrics using newer multilingual, language-specific, and code-specific models, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, no automatic approach provides reliable and consistent assessment. Neural metrics fail to distinguish correct comments from incorrect outputs or even random noise, and tend to overestimate quality in non-English settings. LLM-as-a-judge methods achieve the highest agreement with human annotations but fail to reliably capture important language-related and semantic errors. Overall, our results show that evaluation and generation are key barriers for multilingual tooling, and that human judgment remains indispensable.

</details>


### [247] [Exploring the Effectiveness of Abstract Syntax Tree Patterns for Algorithm Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06098)
*Denis Neumüller,Florian Sihler,Raphael Straub,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于抽象语法树的自动化算法识别方法，通过领域特定语言描述算法特征并匹配，在BigCloneEval基准测试中平均F1分数达0.74，显著优于大型语言模型CodeLlama（0.35）和代码克隆检测工具（最高召回率0.20）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法识别方法存在可用性差、可扩展性不足且缺乏准确性评估的问题，无法有效支持代码维护和重构活动，特别是识别低效算法（如冒泡排序）并推荐优化方案的需求。

Method: 基于抽象语法树构建原型系统：设计领域特定语言（DSL）捕获算法关键特征并表达搜索模式，开发匹配算法定位特征，创建初始可复用模式目录。通过Web搜索算法名称获取参考实现，用DSL描述其关键特征作为搜索模式。

Result: 在BigCloneEval基准的Fibonacci、冒泡排序、二分查找等算法子集上，平均F1分数达0.74（CodeLlama为0.35），召回率0.62（最佳克隆检测工具为0.20）。

Conclusion: 基于AST的算法识别方法在准确性和召回率上显著优于大型语言模型和代码克隆检测工具，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: The automated recognition of algorithm implementations can support many software maintenance and re-engineering activities by providing knowledge about the concerns present in the code base. Moreover, recognizing inefficient algorithms like Bubble Sort and suggesting superior alternatives from a library can help in assessing and improving the quality of a system. Approaches from related work suffer from usability as well as scalability issues and their accuracy is not evaluated. In this paper, we investigate how well our approach based on the abstract syntax tree of a program performs for automatic algorithm recognition. To this end, we have implemented a prototype consisting of: A domain-specific language designed to capture the key features of an algorithm and used to express a search pattern on the abstract syntax tree, a matching algorithm to find these features, and an initial catalog of "ready to use" patterns. To create our search patterns we performed a web search using the algorithm name and described key features of the found reference implementations with our domain-specific language. We evaluate our prototype on a subset of the BigCloneEval benchmark containing algorithms like Fibonacci, Bubble Sort, and Binary Search. We achieve an average F1-score of 0.74 outperforming the large language model Codellama which attains 0.35. Additionally, we use multiple code clone detection tools as a baseline for comparison, achieving a recall of 0.62 while the best-performing tool reaches 0.20.

</details>


### [248] [Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06111)
*Yujia Chen,Yang Ye,Xiao Chu,Yuchi Ma,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ASTOR提出了一种通过效用驱动协调的多任务代码强化学习框架，利用分层数据调度和自适应策略优化，使单一模型在多个编码任务上超越任务专用专家模型9.0%-9.5%，并优于现有MTRL基线7.5%-12.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法为每个编码任务部署独立专家模型，成本随任务数量线性增长；而统一的多任务RL方法对所有任务采用固定数据课程和共享优化策略，忽略了不同任务间的学习差异和协同潜力，限制了多任务训练效果。

Method: ASTOR框架以任务效用（捕捉各任务学习潜力与跨任务协同的信号）为核心，包含两个耦合模块：1) 分层效用路由数据调度模块，层级化分配训练预算并优先选择信息量大的提示；2) 自适应效用校准策略优化模块，动态缩放每个任务的KL正则化强度，使其与当前训练状态匹配。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的LLM上，覆盖四个代表性编码任务，ASTOR使单一模型在所有任务上持续提升，超过最佳任务专用专家9.0%-9.5%，超越最强MTRL基线7.5%-12.8%。

Conclusion: 基于效用驱动协调的多任务框架能够有效解决代码RL后训练中专家模型部署成本高和现有MTRL方法优化策略僵化的问题，实现单一模型在多任务上的统一且高效学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has proven effective at post-training LLMs for coding, yet deploying separate task-specific specialists incurs costs that scale with the number of tasks, motivating a unified multi-task RL (MTRL) approach. However, existing MTRL methods treat all coding tasks uniformly, relying on fixed data curricula under a shared optimization strategy, ultimately limiting the effectiveness of multi-task training. To address these limitations, we propose ASTOR, a multi-tASk code reinforcement learning framework via uTility-driven coORdination. Centered on task utility, a signal capturing each task learning potential and cross-task synergy, ASTOR comprises two coupled modules: 1) Hierarchical Utility-Routed Data Scheduling module hierarchically allocates training budget and prioritizes informative prompts, steering training toward the most valuable data and 2) Adaptive Utility-Calibrated Policy Optimization module dynamically scales per-task KL regularization, matching update constraints to each tasks current training state. Experiments on two widely-used LLMs across four representative coding tasks demonstrate that ASTOR consistently improves a single model across all tasks, outperforming the best task-specific specialist by 9.0%-9.5% and surpassing the strongest MTRL baseline by 7.5%-12.8%.

</details>


### [249] [Breaking, Stale, or Missing? Benchmarking Coding Agents on Project-Level Test Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06125)
*Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang,Haichuan Hu,Chunrong Fang,Liang Xiao,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TEBench是首个项目级别的测试演化基准，包含314个来自Defects4J项目的任务实例，覆盖三种测试演化类型（Test-Breaking、Test-Stale、Test-Missing）。评估表明，现有智能体框架和基础模型存在性能天花板（F1约45.7%-49.4%），且Test-Stale类型最具挑战性（F1约36%），因为系统依赖执行失败信号而缺乏主动语义推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试演化基准仅在方法级别操作且提供预配对的输入，绕过了从整个项目中定位受影响测试的任务，也完全排除了对新测试的需求。因此需要一个项目级别的基准来真实评估系统在代码演进时自主识别需要修改的测试、确定需要新测试的位置并生成相应测试补丁的能力。

Method: 通过四阶段流水线在Defects4J项目上构建TEBench，从10个项目中筛选出314个任务实例，每个实例带有开发者编写的真实标签，并标注了三种演化类型：Test-Breaking（失败的测试）、Test-Stale（通过但不再有效验证更新行为的测试）和Test-Missing（需要为新行为添加的新测试）。评估了7种配置，涵盖3个工业智能体框架（Claude Code、Codex CLI、OpenCode）和6个基础模型，以及一个启发式基线。

Result: 所有7种配置的识别F1集中在45.7%到49.4%之间，揭示了跨框架和基础模型的共享性能天花板。Test-Stale是最具挑战性的类型，平均F1约36%。在更新任务上，配置生成的测试修改具有高度可执行性，但其表面形式与真实标签存在显著差异。轨迹分析揭示了反应式的"执行-失败-修复"循环，该循环对破坏性测试有效，但结构上无法处理陈旧和缺失的测试。

Conclusion: TEBench揭示了当前测试演化方法的局限性，特别是对Test-Stale和Test-Missing类型的处理能力不足。现有系统依赖执行失败信号而缺乏主动语义推理，导致存在明显的性能天花板。该基准为未来测试演化研究提供了更有挑战性的评估标准。

Abstract: As production code evolves, the test suite must co-evolve to remain effective. Existing benchmarks for test evolution operate at method-level granularity with pre-paired inputs, bypassing the task of locating affected tests from the full project and excluding the need for new tests entirely. We present TEBench, the first project-level benchmark for test evolution. Given a project repository and a code-changing commit, TEBench requires systems to autonomously identify tests requiring modification, determine where new tests are needed, and produce the corresponding test patch. We construct TEBench through a four-stage pipeline over Defects4J projects, curating 314 task instances from 10 projects with developer-written ground truth. Each instance is annotated with one or more of three evolution types: Test-Breaking (tests that fail), Test-Stale (tests that pass but no longer meaningfully validate updated behavior), and Test-Missing (new tests needed for introduced behavior). We evaluate seven configurations spanning three industrial agent frameworks (Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode) and six base models, alongside a heuristic baseline. All seven configurations converge on an identification F1 of 45.7% to 49.4%, revealing a shared performance ceiling across both frameworks and base models. Test-Stale is the most challenging type, averaging F1 around 36%, since configurations rely on execution failure signals and lack proactive semantic reasoning. On the update task, configurations produce highly executable test modifications whose surface form diverges substantially from ground truth. Trajectory analysis reveals a reactive "execute-fail-fix" loop that succeeds for breaking tests but structurally cannot address stale or missing tests. TEBench is available at https://github.com/iSEngLab/TEBench with a leaderboard at https://tebench-leadership.vercel.app.

</details>


### [250] [BUILD-AND-FIND: An Effort-Aware Protocol for Evaluating Agent-Managed Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06136)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BUILD-AND-FIND协议，评估代码生成智能体生成的仓库是否能让下游智能体有效恢复设计意图，通过行为正确性与制品可恢复性分离评估，并测量恢复准确性、可重复性、实现覆盖率和检查成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准仅关注代码行为正确性，但仓库级工程中生成的代码同时也是未来工作的沟通媒介；即使行为正确，不同仓库在暴露设计意图的清晰度上存在差异，缺乏评估制品可恢复性的方法。

Method: BUILD-AND-FIND协议：构建者根据隐藏规范创建代码库；查找者仅凭代码库和规范追踪的多选题库恢复意图。分离行为正确性与制品可恢复性，报告准确性、重复性、覆盖率和检查成本。设置问题-only和规范-only对照，并通过审计区分遗漏主张和查找者失败。

Result: 在高先验任务包中，恢复准确率接近饱和，因此检查成本和查找者特定效应成为主要比较指标。

Conclusion: BUILD-AND-FIND提供了一种评估生成仓库沟通质量的协议，在行为正确性之外补充了设计意图可恢复性这一维度，尤其适用于强智能体行为正确性已接近饱和的场景。

Abstract: Most coding-agent benchmarks ask whether generated code behaves correctly. That remains essential, but repository-level engineering is increasingly agent-managed: one agent writes a repository, and later agents inspect, audit, or extend it as working context. In that setting, a generated repository is not only an answer to a task but also a communication artifact for future work. Even when strong agents nearly satisfy the visible behavioral objective, repositories can differ in how clearly they expose the intended behavior and design choices behind that behavior. We introduce BUILD-AND-FIND, a protocol for evaluating whether downstream agents can recover those intended choices from generated repositories, and how much inspection that recovery requires. For each task, a builder sees a hidden repository specification and creates a codebase; a finder sees only the codebase and a specification-traced multiple-choice question bank. The protocol separates behavioral correctness from artifact-side recovery and reports recovery accuracy, repeatability, implementation coverage, and inspection effort. Accuracy and stability act as gates: effort is interpreted only when recovery succeeds reliably. Among artifacts from which the same intent can be recovered, lower effort by the same finder suggests that the artifact makes that intent easier to locate. Question-only and spec-only controls quantify generic priors and specification access, while audits separate omitted claims from finder failures and check whether correct answers cite artifact evidence. In the released high-prior task pack, recovery accuracy is near saturation, so inspection effort and finder-specific effects provide the main panel-local comparison.

</details>


### [251] [SiblingRepair: Sibling-Based Multi-Hunk Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06209)
*Xinyu Liu,Jiayu Ren,Yusen Wang,Qi Xin,Xiaoyuan Xie,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SiblingRepair是一种基于LLM的多hunk自动程序修复技术，通过令牌和嵌入代码匹配搜索语义相关的兄弟候选，利用LLM识别失败相关兄弟并采用同步修复和迭代修复两种策略生成一致补丁，在Defects4J和GHRB基准上显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多hunk修复技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在实现相关功能时常在多个代码位置（称为兄弟）犯类似错误，现有SOTA方法Hercules受限于对兄弟位置的强假设、提交历史的可用性、基于AST的僵化匹配以及基于模板的补丁生成灵活性不足，亟需一种更灵活、更准确的兄弟修复方法。

Method: SiblingRepair首先基于频谱故障定位识别可疑位置，然后通过令牌级和嵌入级代码匹配搜索语义相关的兄弟候选（不局限于失效测试覆盖或提交历史），接着使用LLM识别与失败相关的兄弟，并通过两种互补策略生成一致补丁：同步修复（联合修复兄弟）和迭代修复（逐步分析候选构建补丁），最后保留之前可疑位置产生的有前景补丁并组合为广义多hunk补丁。

Result: 在Defects4J和GHRB基准上的评估表明，SiblingRepair显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多hunk修复技术，同时实验证明了其修复效率、兄弟检测和修复组件的有效性，以及LLM数据泄漏对结果影响有限。

Conclusion: SiblingRepair通过结合LLM的语义理解能力和灵活的搜索策略，克服了Hercules的强假设和刚性匹配限制，推进了自动化的兄弟修复和广义多hunk修复技术。

Abstract: Developers often make similar mistakes across code locations implementing related functionalities. These locations, called siblings, share similar issues and require similar fixes. Accurately identifying siblings and consistently repairing them are crucial for automated program repair. Hercules is a SOTA technique designed for sibling repair. However, it is limited by strong assumptions about sibling locations and commit-history availability, rigid AST-based sibling matching, and inflexible template-based patch generation. To address these limitations, we present SiblingRepair, a new LLM-based multi-hunk APR technique specialized for sibling repair. Starting from a suspicious location identified by spectrum-based fault localization, SiblingRepair searches for semantically related sibling candidates using token- and embedding-based code matching, without restricting discovery to failing-test coverage or commit history. It then uses an LLM to identify failure-relevant siblings and generate consistent patches through two complementary strategies: simultaneous repair, which jointly repairs siblings, and iterative repair, which progressively analyzes candidates for patch construction. SiblingRepair further preserves promising patches generated from earlier suspicious locations and combines them into generalized multi-hunk patches. We evaluate SiblingRepair on the Defects4J and GHRB benchmarks. The results show that SiblingRepair substantially outperforms SOTA multi-hunk repair techniques including Hercules. Our evaluation further demonstrates its repair efficiency, the effectiveness of its sibling detection and repair components, and limited impact of the LLM data leakage on the results. Overall, SiblingRepair advances automated sibling and general multi-hunk repair.

</details>


### [252] [To What Extent Does Agent-generated Code Require Maintenance? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06464)
*Shota Sawada,Tatsuya Shirai,Yutaro Kashiwa,Ken'ichi Yamaguchi,Hiroshi Iwata,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析AIDev数据集和GitHub上的100个流行仓库（超过1000个文件和约3200次变更），实证比较了AI生成代码与人类编写代码的长期可维护性。发现：AI生成文件维护频率低于人类代码，且更新只影响小部分文件；AI代码最常修改的是功能扩展，而人类代码更新主要是修复bug；绝大多数维护工作由人类开发者完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的自主编码Agent在代码生成方面表现出色，但AI生成代码的长期可维护性问题尚未得到充分研究，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用AIDev数据集（包含AI生成的PR）和GitHub数据，对100个流行仓库中的1000多个文件和约3200次变更进行分析，比较AI生成文件与人类编写代码在维护频率、修改类型和人类参与程度方面的差异。

Result: (i) AI生成文件的维护频率低于人类编写代码，更新仅影响文件的一小部分；(ii) AI代码最常见的修改类型是功能扩展，而人类代码更新以修复bug为主；(iii) 绝大多数维护工作由人类开发者执行。

Conclusion: AI生成代码在长期维护方面存在明显不足，维护频率低、修改范围小，且主要依赖人类开发者进行维护，说明当前AI编码工具在生成可维护代码方面仍需改进。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous coding agents have reshaped software development. While these agents excel at code generation, open questions persist about the long-term maintainability of AI-generated code. This study empirically investigates the maintenance extent, human involvement, and modification types of AI-generated files versus human-authored code. Using the AIDev dataset of AI-generated pull requests and GitHub, we analyzed over 1,000 files and approximately 3,200 changes from 100 popular repositories. Our findings show that: (i) AI-generated files receive less frequent maintenance than human-authored code, with updates affecting only a small fraction of file size; (ii) the most frequent modifications to AI code are feature extensions, whereas human updates focus on bug fixes, and (iii) human developers perform the large majority of this maintenance.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [A GPU-Accelerated Hybrid Method for a Class of Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05208)
*Zhenyu Lei,Jin-Kao Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合算法求解多车场车辆路径问题(MDVRP)，该算法集成了学习驱动的多样性控制交叉算子、多惩罚评估函数引导的可接受不可行搜索框架、专用车场局部搜索算子，以及基于张量GPU加速和多移动更新策略的增强版本。在三个MDVRP变体的基准测试中，该方法在大规模实例上显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDVRP在众多实际应用中广泛存在，但其内在复杂性使其在计算上难以求解，现有方法在处理大规模问题时面临效率和可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出混合算法：(1) 学习驱动的多样性控制路径交换交叉算子；(2) 多惩罚评估函数引导的多车场支持可行-不可行搜索框架；(3) 两个专用的车场相关局部搜索算子；(4) 基于张量的GPU加速结合新颖的多移动更新策略以提升计算效率和可扩展性。

Result: 在三个MDVRP变体的基准实例上的大量计算实验表明，所提算法与最先进方法相比极具竞争力，尤其在大规模实例上表现突出。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合算法有效解决了MDVRP的计算挑战，通过集成学习驱动机制、多惩罚搜索框架和GPU加速，在大规模问题上实现了优越性能，为多车场路径优化提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRPs) are prevalent in a variety of practical applications. However, they are computationally challenging to solve due to their inherent complexity. This paper proposes an effective hybrid algorithm for a class of MDVRPs. The algorithm integrates a learning-driven, diversity-controlled route-exchange crossover and a multi-depot-supported feasible-and-infeasible search framework guided by a multi-penalty evaluation function. Two dedicated depot-related local search operators are incorporated to further strengthen the search capability in multi-depot settings. To improve computational efficiency and scalability, an enhanced version of the algorithm is developed that uses a tensor-based GPU acceleration combined with a novel multi-move update strategy. Extensive computational experiments on benchmark instances of three MDVRP variants show that the proposed algorithms are highly competitive with state-of-the-art methods, especially for large-scale instances.

</details>


### [254] [Monitoring autonomous persistent surveillance missions using invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06062)
*Vladislav Nenchev,Prodromos Sotiriadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑箱自主机器人在持久监视任务中的运行时监控问题，提出了一种基于离线不变量的组合式监控方法，并在真实机器人迷宫中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人进行持久监视时，其自主栈往往是黑箱，难以直接分析系统行为。需要一种能够在运行时有效监控系统是否偏离预期行为的机制。

Method: 将环境划分为有限区域，每个区域携带不确定性状态。将闭环系统建模为状态依赖的混合系统（线性参数变化动力学），基于离线计算的不变量设计监控器。为应对大规模空间，提出组合式监控器：对每个不确定性区域分布式计算低维不变量集，在线检查其合取。

Result: 在常见独立性假设下，组合式监控器关于全系统不变量是完备且可靠的。通过真实机器人在迷宫中持久监视的案例研究，验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 组合式方法有效解决了大规模空间下不变量难以获取的问题，使得黑箱自主机器人的运行时监控在实践中可行。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime monitoring for persistent surveillance by autonomous robots when the autonomy stack is a black box. The environment is partitioned into finitely many parts, each carrying an uncertainty state that decreases when observed and increases otherwise. We model the closed loop as a state-dependent hybrid system with linear parameter varying dynamics and design a monitor based on an invariant computed offline. As this invariant is typically hard to obtain for large to-be-surveyed spaces, we propose a compositional monitor obtained by decentralized computation of low-dimensional invariant sets for each uncertainty region, and checking their conjunction online. Under common independence assumptions, the compositional monitor is sound and complete with respect to the full-system invariant. The approach is applied in a case study with a real robot persistently monitoring a labyrinth, emphasizing its applicability in practice.

</details>


### [255] [DexSim2Real: Foundation Model-Guided Sim-to-Real Transfer for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05241)
*Zijian Zeng,Fei Ding,Huiming Yang,Xianwei Li,Yuhao Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSim2Real是一个利用视觉-语言基础模型弥合灵巧操作中仿真到现实迁移差距的集成框架，通过基础模型引导的域随机化、触觉-视觉交叉注意力策略和渐进式技能课程三大组件，在六项任务中实现了78.2%的平均真实世界成功率，将仿真到现实的性能差距缩小至仅8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到现实的迁移仍然是灵巧操作策略部署的关键瓶颈，现有依赖手工设计域随机化或任务特定适应的方法缺乏跨多样化操作场景的泛化能力。

Method: 提出DexSim2Real框架，包含三个组件：(1) 基础模型引导的域随机化(FM-DR)，使用视觉-语言模型作为视觉真实性评判器，通过闭环CMA-ES优化仿真参数；(2) 触觉-视觉交叉注意力策略(TVCAP)，适应交叉注意力视觉-触觉融合实现零样本仿真到现实强化学习；(3) 渐进式技能课程(PSC)，基于LLM任务分解与难度调度器处理接触密集的灵巧任务。

Result: 在六项挑战性灵巧操作任务的盲评中，DexSim2Real实现了78.2%的平均真实世界成功率，优于DrEureka和DeXtreme，并将仿真到现实的性能差距降低至仅8.3%。

Conclusion: DexSim2Real通过视觉-语言基础模型的直接视觉反馈有效弥合了灵巧操作的仿真到现实差距，证明了基础模型引导方法相比传统文本驱动策略的优越性。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer remains a critical bottleneck for deploying dexterous manipulation policies learned in simulation to real-world robots. Existing approaches rely on manually designed domain randomization or task-specific adaptation, limiting their generalizability across diverse manipulation scenarios. We present DexSim2Real, an integrated framework that leverages vision-language foundation models to bridge the sim-to-real gap for dexterous manipulation. Our system combines three components: (1) Foundation Model-Guided Domain Randomization (FM-DR), which uses a vision-language model as a visual realism critic to optimize simulation parameters via closed-loop CMA-ES, complementing text-based approaches like DrEureka with direct visual feedback; (2) a Tactile-Visual Cross-Attention Policy (TVCAP) that adapts cross-attention visuo-tactile fusion to zero-shot sim-to-real RL; and (3) a Progressive Skill Curriculum (PSC) that builds on LLM-based task decomposition with a difficulty scheduler tailored to contact-rich dexterous tasks. Extensive experiments on six challenging manipulation tasks with blinded evaluation demonstrate that DexSim2Real achieves a 78.2% average real-world success rate, outperforming DrEureka and DeXtreme while reducing the sim-to-real performance gap to only 8.3%.

</details>


### [256] [Lie Group Formulation of Recursive Dynamics Algorithms of Higher Order for Floating-Base Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06498)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了浮基树形机构在李群SE(3)上的高阶时间导数动力学算法，包括牛顿-欧拉、组合体惯量和混合动力学方法，分析了其闭式运动方程、可容许科里奥利矩阵和不变的组合惯性张量，并应用于12自由度空中机械臂，验证了计算成本随导数阶数呈二次缩放，远优于自动微分的指数缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮基机器人动力学算法主要关注零阶和一阶导数，缺乏对高阶时间导数的高效计算方法，而高阶导数在轨迹优化、模型预测控制和非线性估计中具有重要应用价值。

Method: 基于李群SE(3)和空间旋量表示，推导了浮基树形机构（基座位姿流形为SE(3)，关节构型流形为T^{n1}×R^{n2}）的牛顿-欧拉、组合体惯量和混合动力学算法的高阶时间导数递归过程，并将递归聚合成闭式运动方程，识别了满足无源性性质的可容许科里奥利矩阵。

Result: 在12自由度空中机械臂上成功评估了高达五阶的几何正/逆动力学及其一阶时间导数；数值基准测试表明，所提方法的计算成本随导数阶数呈二次增长，而自动微分基线呈现指数增长。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶导数动力学算法在计算效率上显著优于自动微分方法，为需要高阶动力学的轨迹优化和控制应用提供了实用的高效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe procedures for computing higher-order time derivatives of the Lie-group Newton-Euler, Articulated-Body Inertia, and hybrid dynamics algorithms for floating-base trees, where the base configuration evolves on SE(3) and the attached mechanism is an open kinematic tree with configuration on the (n1+n2)-dimensional manifold T^{n1} \times R^{n2}, using spatial representation of twists. After presenting the algorithms, we collect the resulting recursions into closed-form equations of motion, identifying an admissible Coriolis matrix satisfying the passivity property, and showing that the articulated inertia tensor remains unchanged across all time derivatives. We then apply the developed methods to a 12-DoF aerial manipulator to derive analytical expressions for its geometric forward and inverse dynamics along with their first time derivatives whereas the numerical simulations successfully evaluate these dynamics up to fifth order.
